# THIM working log (mathematical facts)

Source: cat/03.txt (94642 lines, 241 files).
## ValuationClockChainBridge (Pillar I)
- primeRadical(d) = Π_{p|d} p; lagVisibilityClock(d) = d/rad(d) (from BVI: mode q visible at lag h ⇔ clock(q) | h). clock·rad = d. d/clock(d) prime ⇔ d prime power ⇒ Λ(d) = log(d/clock d) if prime else 0.
- Prime power p^{a+1}: clock = p^a; invisible at 1 ≤ h < p^a; at h = p^a: sieveLagMultiplier = −1/(p−1), Ramanujan lag coefficient = −p^a.
- Prime-chain edge m < k (same p-complement) has k/m = p^r, Λ(k/m) = log p; CHMD chainVariance rewritten with clock radical test.
## TransHorizonRoughAbsorptionAndReset (Pillar II)
- IsTransHorizonRough N r: every divisor ≤ N of r is 1 (generated by primes > N; monoid; coprime to all d ≤ N).
- sieveSeq N c (q·r) = sieveSeq N c q; sieveSeq N c r = sieveSeq N c 1 (prime p > N resets). Active centered sawtooth sum resets to 0 at r−1, r. sieveSeq N μ (q r) = 0 for 2 ≤ q ≤ N. Power-damped weight: sieveSeq = divisorCoeff(q).
- 2·renormalizedPrefixDefect(N, r−1, c) = −Σ_{k<r}(s(k+1) − s(k))² ≤ 0 at rough r (boundary s(r)² − s(1)² vanishes). Cofinal: for any N, B ∃ M > B with defect ≤ 0 (M = p−1).
## BuchstabHorizonFiltrationAndSawtoothEvolution (Pillar III, start)
- Horizon step N → N+1 adds spike c(N+1)·1_{N+1|k}; band N₁ < d ≤ N₂: sieveSeq difference = Σ_{d∈(N₁,N₂], d|k} c(d); prefix discrepancy shifts by −Σ c(d)·fractDivSeq(d,M).
- sieveSeq M c k = Σ_{d|k} c(d) for k ≤ M (complete). sieveSeq(N,c,k) = Σ_{d|k}c(d) − Σ_{d∈(N,M], d|k}c(d). First octave k ∈ [N+1, 2N]: sieveSeq = Σ_{d|k}c(d) − c(k).
- Prefix discrepancy(N,M) = discrepancy(M,M) + Σ_{d∈(N,M]} c(d)·fractDivSeq(d,M) (trans-horizon sawtooth tail).
## QuotientSpectralGapAndOddChainCoercivity (Pillar IV; CHMD resolvent)
- Odd mass vectors ⊥ ground state oneL ⇒ every nonzero odd resolvent eigenvector has μ < 1 (ergodicity). ¬RH ⇔ ∃ odd eigenvector with μ ∈ (½, 1).
- signedRadical has zero slack ⇒ sourceEnergy(reducedSource p) = sourceEnergy p. RH ⇔ sourceEnergy(reduced p) ≥ 0 for all odd p; positivity on primitive radical-orthogonal vectors ⇒ RH.
- Zero-silent (fullSpectralNullspace) with polarCosine = 0 ⇒ sourceEnergy = 2·polarSine² ≥ 0.
- Total prime Doob jump = sameSide(v) + sameSide(v(−·)) + Σ_{chains} chainIntegral. RH ⇔ for all odd v ∈ C²_c: phiDoobCoshMass(v²) ≤ archJump + sameSide jumps + complete prime-power chain variance (odd prime-chain Poincaré).
## TransHorizonMartingaleTelescopeAndPrimeCheckpoints (Pillar V)
- W = weightedCenteredSawtoothSum; telescope W(M)² − W(1)² = Σ(s(n+1) − Y(1))(s_{c/d}(n+1) − Y(1)) + 2Σ(s(n+1) − Y(1))·I(n+1,n) (active-deleted diagonal + inactive martingale) ≥ −W(1)².
- At primes p > N: s(p) = s_{c/d}(p) = s(1); active part vanishes; I = W (fully inactive); W(p) − W(p−1) = s(1) − Y(1) constant; W(p)² − W(p−1)² = (s(1)−Y(1))² + 2(s(1)−Y(1))W(p−1).
## ComplementaryQuotientAndSecondOctaveLaw (Pillar VI, start)
- Σ_{d|k, d>N} c(d) = complementaryQuotientTail = Σ_{q|k, k/q>N} c(k/q) (involution d ↦ k/d). For k ≤ (B+1)N the quotients q ∈ [1,B]. B-rough k ≤ (B+1)N: sieveSeq = Σ_{d|k}c(d) − c(k) (odd k ≤ 3N; k coprime to 6 ≤ 4N).
- Band [2N+1,3N]: sieveSeq = Σ_{d|k}c − c(k) − 1_{2|k}c(k/2); band [3N+1,4N]: also − 1_{3|k}c(k/3).
## SecondAndThirdBandRenormalizedDefectCalculus
- Power-damped weight c_ε(d) = μ(d)d^{−ε}; full divisor coefficient τ_ε(k) = Σ_{d|k}c_ε(d) = Π_{p|k}(1 − p^{−ε}) ∈ [0,1]; |τ_ε − c_ε(k)| ≤ 1 (k ≥ 2, ε ≥ 0). Rough complementary quotients ⇒ |s_N(c_ε,k)| ≤ 1 on rough bands; endpoint bound |s(M+1)| ≤ 1 ⇒ 2·renormDefect ≤ −Dirichlet energy ≤ 0 (polarization 2D = −E + s(M+1)² − s(1)²).
- Prime multiplication: c_ε(pu) = 0 if p|u else −p^{−ε}c_ε(u). Prime-deleted response τ(pu) − c(pu) − c(u) = F_p(u)(τ(u) − c(u)), F_p ∈ [0,1] ⇒ unit envelope through 3N for all k (second band including even k). Möbius defect ≤ 0 at every checkpoint M+1 ≤ 3N; through 4N if 2 ∤ M+1 or 3 ∤ M+1.
- Möbius responses: odd k ≤ 3N (or 6-coprime k ≤ 4N): s_N(μ,k) = 0 if k ≤ N else −μ(k) (three-valued). Sub-horizon squarefree k (e.g. primes p ∈ (3,N]) obstruct the unsplit −μ(k) identity.
## TwoPrimeDeletionAndFullThirdBandEnvelope (start)
- Two-prime deletion: τ(pqu) − c(pqu) − c(qu) − c(pu) = Aτ(u) + (1−A)c(u), A = F_p(u)F_q(u) ∈ [0,1]; |·| ≤ max(A, 1−A) ≤ 1 ⇒ |s_N(c_ε,k)| ≤ 1 for all 2 ≤ k ≤ 4N (no parity restriction).
- |two-prime deleted response| ≤ max(A, 1−A). Unit envelope transports to radical fibres: |s_N(c_ε,k)| ≤ 1 whenever horizonRadicalState(N,k) ≤ 4N; powers k^m of bases ≤ 4N. Prefix defect 2D ≤ −E ≤ 0 for M+1 ≤ 4N (no exclusions); rough-start short windows dissipate.
## FourthBandEnvelopeAndSharpFiveHorizonBoundary
- Band normal form: B·N < k ≤ (B+1)N ⇒ sieveSeq N c k = Σ_{d|k}c(d) − Σ_{q≤B, q|k}c(k/q). Fourth band: 4|k compresses (sieveSeq of 4u = of 2u since nonsquarefree weights vanish); else one/two-prime deletion ⇒ unit envelope through 5N.
- SHARP 5N BOUNDARY: for prime p > 5, N = 6p−1, k = 30p = 5N+5: s_N(μ, 30p) = 2 (μ(30p) − μ(15p) − μ(10p) − μ(6p) with full divisor sum 0 ⇒ −1 + 1 + 1 + 1 = 2). Unit envelope on 2 ≤ k ≤ CN holds for all N iff C ≤ 5 (for μ and uniformly for all ε ≥ 0). Response-2 also at all powers (30p)^m. (Truncated sum = Helfgott–Thompson D(n;a).)
- Envelope ⇒ dissipation: prefix defect 2D ≤ −E for M+1 ≤ 5N, radical ≤ 5N fibres, powers; rough-start short windows through 5N.
## ExactAdditiveEnvelopeAndFirstFailure
- Least omitted divisor is N+1 ⇒ band normal form holds for k < (B+1)(N+1). Unit envelope |s_N(c_ε,k)| ≤ 1 for all 2 ≤ k < 5(N+1) = 5N+5 (one-prime deletion at u=1 gives 0, two-prime at u=1 gives 1). Real additive buffer b after 5N admissible ⇔ b < 5; natural ⇔ b ≤ 4. 30p = 5(N+1) is the FIRST failure at each horizon N = 6p−1. Response > 1 ⇒ horizonRadicalState ≥ 5(N+1).
## IntegerJumpEnergyAndBeyondEnvelopeDefect
- Möbius path s_N(k) = Σ_{d|k,d≤N}μ(d) is integer-valued ⇒ |z| ≤ z² ⇒ path displacement ≤ jump energy (no length factor). s(1)=1, s(2)=0 (N ≥ 2) ⇒ prefix energy E ≥ 1 + |s(M+1)| ⇒ 2D ≤ s(M+1)² − |s(M+1)| − 2. So |s(M+1)| ≤ 1 ⇒ D ≤ −1 (strict margin); |s(M+1)| ≤ 2 ⇒ D ≤ 0.
- Fifth integer band (5N < k < 6(N+1)): |s_N(c_ε,k)| ≤ 2 (two/three deletion ≤ 1 plus |c_ε(k/5)| ≤ 1). ⇒ Möbius prefix defect ≤ 0 for all M+1 < 6(N+1), radical fibres < 6(N+1), powers; the response-2 family 30p has nonpositive defect: unit-envelope boundary is not a sign obstruction. Positive Möbius defect ⇒ radical ≥ 6(N+1).
## StrictMobiusMarginAndUniformDampingStability
- Bertrand prime p ∈ (N, 2N]: s(1),s(2),s(p),s(2p) = 1,0,1,0 ⇒ for M+1 ≥ 4N energy ≥ 3 + |s(M+1)| ⇒ |s(M+1)| ≤ 2 ⇒ D_N(M) ≤ −1 (N ≥ 2, M ≥ 1). Strict margin on all M+1 < 6(N+1).
- Continuity in ε ⇒ ∃ δ_N > 0: |ε| < δ_N ⇒ D_N(M; c_ε) < −½ on all prefixes with radical R_N(M+1) < 6(N+1) (finitely many base checkpoints; radical/equal-response monotonicity; rough endpoints via Bertrand prime); all powers. Response-two family keeps response > 1 with defect < −½ under small damping. Excluding ε = 0 doesn't change sharp ratio 5 / buffer < 5.
## QuantitativeDampingRadiusAndDefectPerturbation
- |c_ε(d) − μ(d)| ≤ ε log d; Σ_{d≤N} ≤ εN log N; bounded paths (|s| ≤ B) with response error A ⇒ |ΔD| ≤ 4MBA; six-horizon ⇒ |D_ε − D_0| ≤ 8MεN log N. Explicit radius δ_N = 1/(96N(N+1)log N): 0 ≤ ε ≤ δ_N ⇒ D ≤ −½ on certified radical fibres and powers; response error ≤ 1/(96(N+1)); counterfamily powers have response ≥ 3/2 and defect ≤ −½ (amplitude separated from defect energy).
## DivisorIncidencePerturbationAndLogarithmicDamping (start)
- |D_c(M) − D_b(M)| ≤ 4B Σ_{d≤N}|c(d) − b(d)|⌊(M+1)/d⌋ (charged at divisor incidences). Σ_{d≤N} log d/d ≤ (log N)² ⇒ |D_ε − D_0| ≤ 8ε(M+1)(log N)²; radius δ_N = 1/(96(N+1)(log N)²); counterfamily response ≥ 31/16.
- Divisor-incidence perturbation: Σ_{j≤K}|s_c(j) − s_b(j)| ≤ Σ_d|c(d) − b(d)|⌊K/d⌋; edge defect perturbation ≤ B(3|Δx| + |Δy|) ⇒ |ΔD| ≤ 4B·Σ_{j≤M+1}|Δs(j)|. Incidence radius 1/(96(N+1)(log N)²) strictly larger than 1/(96N(N+1)log N). Finite six-horizon certificate transfers to all squarefree radical fibres (prefix_defect_le_of_finite_radical_certificate: radical ≥ 2 uses radical monotonicity; radical = 1 uses Bertrand prime p ∈ (N,2N] with equal response).
## WeightedCoefficientNeighborhoodAndRadicalDefectStability
- divisorIncidenceDistance W(N,K;c,b) = Σ_{d≤N}|c−b|(d)⌊K/d⌋ (seminorm on coefficients). Only the reference path needs a bound: |D_c − D_b| ≤ 4(B + W)W. At K = 6(N+1): D_c(M) ≤ −1 + 4(2 + W)W for squarefree-supported c on radical fibres < 6(N+1); W ≤ 1/20 ⇒ D_c ≤ −59/100 (uniform on infinite fibres and powers). Any direction h normalized by 20(1 + W(h_sf)) gives explicit perturbation of μ inside the ball; counterfamily response ≥ 39/20 with defect ≤ −59/100 throughout the ball.
## InterCheckpointSawtoothExcursionAndPronicSandwich (Pillar VII, start)
- 2(D(M) − D(r−1)) = −Σ_{k=r}^M (Δs)² + s(M+1)² − s(1)² for rough r ≤ M+1 ⇒ if s(M+1)² ≤ s(1)² then D(M) ≤ D(r−1) − ½Σ(Δs)² ≤ 0. D(r) − D(r−1) = s(1)(s(r+1) − s(1)).
- Rough r: sieveSeq of pronic (r−1)r = s(r−1), of r(r+1) = s(r+1).
- Pronic neighbour sum/gradient across rough r equal linear s(r−1) ± s(r+1).
## ClockScheduleSynthesisAndPrimePowerPhaseRecovery (Pillar VIII)
- p-adic clock filter at lag p^a on mode p^b: passband b ≤ a: multiplier 1, Ramanujan coefficient φ(p^b); frontier b = a+1: −1/(p−1), −p^a; stopband b ≥ a+2: 0.
- Clock wavelet (lag p^a minus lag p^{a−1}) supported exactly on b ∈ {a, a+1}: normalized p/(p−1), −1/(p−1); Ramanujan dipole +p^a, −p^a (net charge 0).
## CompositeClockWaveletTensorAndPhaseRecovery
- sieveLagMultiplier and Ramanujan lag coefficient are multiplicative in coprime moduli at any lag; lag factors coprime to modulus are invisible; cross-coprime split q₁q₂ at h₁h₂ = product. Mixed four-lag difference = tensor product of lag differences.
- Composite wavelet on p^b·u at transverse lag L (coprime to p) = (p-adic wavelet)·(transverse multiplier at L); transverse factor nonzero iff clock(u) | L. Canonical L = clock(p-free part of q) recovers v_p(q) ∈ {a, a+1} and the sign (+p^a at b = a, −p^a at b = a+1) for every nonzero q (any number of prime factors). Two independent prime clocks isolate the four corners of an adjacent valuation rectangle.
## TransHorizonGramMatrixAndRadicalProjection (Pillar IX, start)
- s squarefree, d | k ⇒ (s | dk ⇔ s | k). Hence for squarefree-supported c and EVERY horizon N: sieveSeq N c (dk) = sieveSeq N c k, sieveSeq(k^m) = sieveSeq(k): truncated sieve is radical/power invariant above the horizon (constant along p-adic chains).
- Buchstab L² band decomposition: E_{N₂}(M) − E_{N₁}(M) = Σ(Δs)² + 2Σ s_{N₁}·Δs.
## FermiValuationSamplingAndPronicObservability
- Fermi cell response on reciprocal cells (from BVI); boundary jump J(n) = R(n) − R(n−1). J(2m+1) = −s(2m+1) (any c, any horizon). J(2m+2) = 2s(m+1) − s(2m+2). J(4m+4) − s(2m+2) = s(2m+2) − s(4m+4).
- Exact recovery J(4m+4) = s(2m+2) for all m ⇔ c vanishes on multiples of 4 (≤ N). Möbius-twisted weights μ·w (squarefree) satisfy it at every horizon (valuation saturation). So odd boundaries + boundaries 4m+4 read every positive sieve value s(n).
- Pronic-weighted energies (weights 1/(n(n+1))): selected ≤ paired ≤ 4·selected; exact slack 4·sel − paired = Σ(3s(2m+1)²/pronic + s(2m+2)²/(pronic·(4m+5))). Constants sharp: inverse 4 forced already at horizon 2; forward 1 attained (odd indicator). Prime-square coefficient (c = 1 at 2, 4; N = 4) aliases: selected energy 0, paired 1/6.
## FermiSelectedCoefficientTransportAndRecovery (start)
- Selected readings form a divisor sieve after dyadic coefficient transport b(d) = c(d) (odd d), (2 if 4|d else 1)c(d) − c(2d)·1_{2d≤N} (even d). Inverse energy: Σc² ≤ (3/2)Σb² with exact residual 3Σb² − 2Σc² = Σ alias residual²; transport injective.
- Selected reading = literal sieve of transported coefficients: R_sel(n) = sieveSeq N b (n+1). First N selected readings vanish ⇔ all admitted coefficients vanish (no support premise); traces equal ⇔ coefficients equal.
## FermiSelectedCoefficientSharpInverse
- Dyadic packets (3 at 2, (2/3)^{j−2} at 2^j, j ≤ K+2): alias residual only at terminal 2^{K+2}, residual energy 4(2/3)^{2K} → 0 while coefficient energy ≥ 9 ⇒ best inverse constant exactly 3/2.
## FermiValuationDepthAndFiniteWindowStability
- d < 2^L ⇒ (d | 2^{L+1}n ⇔ d | 2^L n): every horizon saturates on dyadic chains after L = ⌊log₂N⌋+1 levels. Even sieve values recovered by finite dyadic telescope of selected boundary jumps (weights 2^{−(j+1)}, terminal doubled). Without support restriction: paired pronic energy on first M ≤ (L+4)(L+1)·selected energy on 2^L·M (quadratic valuation-depth factor).
## FermiCompleteTraceValuationStability
- Finite coefficient horizon ⇒ both complete pronic traces summable (|s| ≤ Σ|c|, weights 1/(n(n+1))). Complete-trace inverse: sieveTotal ≤ (L+4)(L+1)·selectedTotal, L = ⌊log₂N⌋+1.
## FermiDyadicRampAndQuadraticTraceLoss
- Dyadic ramp weight (coefficient columns on powers of 2, horizon 2^{L+1}): sieve value on 2-adic shell k equals 2^{k+1}(L−k); odd jumps 0; even jumps = 2^{k+2} on shells. sieveTotal ≥ L(L+1)(2L+1)/12 (cubic) while selectedTotal ≤ 2L (linear) ⇒ quadratic loss ((L+1)(2L+1)/24) forced; NO horizon-uniform inverse constant for complete selected trace.
## FermiNonnegativeDyadicRampTraceObstruction (start)
- Removing the negative endpoint gives an explicitly nonnegative ramp (2(K+1) at 2, 2^{k+1}(K−k−1) at 2^{k+2}); its sieve energy ≥ the signed ramp's (cubic (K+1)(K+2)(2K+3)/12); endpoint correction energy ≤ ½ (Σ1/t² ≤ 2).
- Nonnegative ramp: selectedTotal ≤ 4K+5 (linear), sieveTotal cubic ⇒ quadratic loss persists in nonnegative cone; no uniform inverse even for c ≥ 0.
## FermiMixedBoundaryRayStability
- Normalized dyadic ray x_k = s(2^k b)/2^k; normalized jump J(2^{k+1}b)/2^{k+1} = x_k − x_{k+1} (exact forward difference). Mixed-boundary sine bound (reflect path, RHTower autocorrelation chain sine certificate): (2 − 2cos(π/(2n+1)))Σ_{k<n}(x_{a+k} − x_{a+n})² ≤ Σ jumps². Horizon saturation: x_{D+1} = x_D/2 (D = ⌊log₂N⌋+1); terminal jump = x_{D+1}.
## FermiPronicRayDecomposition / FermiPronicRayWeightAndTailControl (start)
- Index m+1 = 2^k(2j+1): complete sieve energy = odd readings + Σ_k Σ_j x²·w(k+1, 2j+1); selected energy = odd + Σ jump²·w(k+2, ·). Ray weight w(k,b) = 4^k/pronic(2^k b) = 1/(b(b + 2^{−k})) ∈ [2^k/((2^k+1)b²), 1/b²], increasing in k, ≥ (1 − 2^{−k})/b².
- Terminal mass bound with Young; beyond depth, x decays by exact powers 2^{−j}: tail Σ x² = (4/3)x_{D+a}²; first unadmitted tail mass = (4/3)·(terminal jump)². Complete ray (with geometric tail) controlled by finitely many observed jumps.
## FermiPhysicalTraceSineUpperBound
- Per-ray energies: sieve E_ray(b) = Σ_k x_{k+1}²·w(k+1,b), selected Q_ray(b) = Σ jump²·w(k+2,b). Head cells bounded by (5/2)(D+1)Q_ray; tail via mixed-boundary sine. UNCONDITIONAL TRACE BOUND: for 1 ≤ K ≤ D = ⌊log₂N⌋+1, n = D+1−K, λ = 2 − 2cos(π/(2n+1)), M ≥ 0: Mλ·sieveTotal ≤ [(1 + 2^{−(K+1)})((M+1) + M(M+1)λn + (4/3)Mλ) + (5/2)(K−1)(D+1)Mλ]·selectedTotal. (λ ~ π²/(4n²): inverse loss quadratic in depth.)
## FermiNonnegativeCosinePhysicalTraceLowerBound (start)
- Cosine profile x_k = cos((k+½)π/(2r+1)): x_r = 0, nonneg on [0,r], recurrence x_k + x_{k+2} = 2cos(θ)x_{k+1}; Σ_{k<r}x² = (2r+1)/4 (odd Dirichlet kernel); Σ(Δx)² = (2 − 2cosθ)Σx² (eigenvector of mixed-boundary path). Synthesized by nonnegative dyadic coefficients (2x_0 at 2; 2^{k+1}(2x_{k+1} − x_k) ≥ 0 at 2^{k+2}); sieve on odd rays = profile then geometric tail; selected homogeneous energy = (2 − 2cosθ)(2(L+1)+1)/4 − (2/3)x_L².
- Cosine packet per ray: sieve ≥ (S − 1)/b², selected ≤ λS/b² (S = (2r+1)/4) ⇒ (S − 1)·selected ≤ λS·sieve on the full trace; odd readings vanish (odd rays only).
## FermiPhysicalSineAsymptotics / FermiPhysicalOptimalConstants
- n²(2 − 2cos(π/(2n+1))) → π²/4. Uniform upper: for every ε, eventually (dyadic N = 2^r) sieveTotal ≤ (4/π² + ε)r²·selectedTotal for ALL coefficients. Nonnegative cosine family attains (4/π² − ε)(L+1)². SHARP: best asymptotic leading coefficient = 4/π² (signed and nonnegative).
- Optimal physical inverse constant (sup of sieve/selected ratio) C(N) satisfies C(N)/(log₂N)² → 4/π² for ALL horizons N (nonneg version too); horizon zero-extension preserves both energies (Buchstab band), squeeze between dyadic horizons.
## FermiOddTraceCenteringAndExtremalRestriction
- Shift weight c + t(e₂ − e₁): fixes even sieve readings and selected even jumps; odd readings shift by −t. Both total energies change by the same quadratic −2t·A + t²·T (A = odd pronic mean Σ s(2m+1)/pronic, T = odd pronic mass > 0); energy difference invariant. Centering (A = 0) uniquely maximizes the ratio on the orbit when sieve > selected (exact gain formula). Nonneg-preserving shifts: t ∈ [−c(2), c(1)]; A ≥ T·c(1) for c ≥ 0, equality ⇔ odd coefficients > 1 vanish; removing c(1) doesn't lower ratio > 1. A signed-extremizer that is nonnegative with ratio > 1 has all admitted odd coefficients zero.
## FermiDilatedPronicMassAndRationalBounds
- E_d = Σ_n 1/pronic(dn) > 0; odd split Σ 1/pronic(d(2m+1)) = E_d − E_{2d}; (4 − 2/(d+1))E_{2d} ≤ E_d; E_4 ≤ 9/80; 4E_4 − E_2 ≥ 1/30; (10/3)E_4 ≤ E_2 ≤ (100/27)E_4; (26/7)E_12 ≤ E_6; 2E_6 ≤ E_3 (rational, no numerics).
## FermiHorizonThreePhysicalGram (start)
- N = 3, c(1) = 0, a = c(2), b = c(3): sieve energy = E₂a² + 2E₆ab + E₃b²; selected = E₄a² + 2E₁₂ab + (E₃ − E₆ + E₁₂)b²; odd mean = b(E₃ − E₆).
## FermiFiniteHorizonPositiveConeGap
- Two-variable Rayleigh: if (A/D)E < B (cross gain) and C/F ≤ A/D then ratio maximum on the nonneg quadrant is attained at an interior point. At horizon 3: (E₂/E₄)E₁₂ < E₆ (100/27 < 26/7) ⇒ nonnegative supremum attained at a, b > 0 with ratio > 1; odd mean > 0 there ⇒ centering gives a signed input with strictly larger ratio. STRICT CONE GAP: nonnegativeConstant(3) < signedConstant(3) (same 4/π² asymptotics, but finite horizons differ).
## FermiHomogeneousEnergyAndPronicComparison
- Reciprocal-square ("homogeneous") forms in odd-ray coordinates: pronic energies ∈ [½, 1]·homogeneous (both sieve and selected).
## FermiPrimeExponentGramBounds / FermiPrimeBoxGramBounds (classical)
- Kernel r^{|i−j|}: (1−r)/(1+r) ≤ Gram ≤ (1+r)/(1−r) uniformly in dimension (induction with explicit square defects). Tensorizes over finite prime boxes via PSD Kronecker products: constants Π(1−r_i)/(1+r_i), Π(1+r_i)/(1−r_i) (cf. Aistleitner–Berkes–Seip GCD matrices; r = 1/p).
## FermiGcdGramPrimeProductBounds / FermiOddDivisorGramAndFrameBounds (classical support)
- gcd(a,b)²/(ab) = Π_p (1/p)^{|v_p(a) − v_p(b)|}; on any finite support with prime factors in P: Π(p−1)/(p+1) ≤ GCD Gram ≤ Π(p+1)/(p−1).
- Odd-divisor profile φ_d(m) = d/(2m+1)·1_{d|2m+1}: Σ_m φ_dφ_e = M_odd·gcd(d,e)²/(de), M_odd = Σ1/(2m+1)² = π²/8 (positive). Packet energy = M_odd·GCD Gram form ⇒ frame bounds M_odd·Π(p∓1)/(p±1).
## FermiDyadicAntichainCoordinates
- Dyadic antichain: odd indices d ∈ S with one depth j(d) each, coefficient 2^{j}d·a(d) at 2^{j(d)}d (indices distinct). Normalized sieve ray /(2m+1) = odd-divisor packet with coefficients impulse(j,k)·a(d), impulse(j,k) = 2^{j−k}·1_{j≤k}; selected jumps = consecutive differences. Each impulse: full homogeneous mass Σ_k impulse² = 4/3; selected mass ≥ 1/3 (any depth).
## FermiBooleanParityGram (start)
- Boolean cube product kernel Π(x_i = y_i ? 1 : r_i): row sum Π(1+r_i); parity vector eigenvalue Π(1−r_i); parity² = 1, parity sums to 0. Gram form of affine-parity functions = 2^{|I|}(ac·Π(1+r) + bd·Π(1−r)).
- Parity indicators (0/1, disjoint classes) Gram = (2^{|I|}/4)(Π(1+r) ± Π(1−r)).
## FermiBooleanPrimeCube / Packets
- Boolean choice of primes in P ↦ squarefree index Π p^{x_p} (injective, odd for odd P); GCD kernel of cube indices = Boolean product kernel with r_p = 1/p; odd-divisor packet energy on the cube = M_odd·Boolean Gram form.
## FermiPrimeCapacityBanks
- Parity ratio Π(p−1)/(p+1) ≤ 1/(1 + 2Σ1/p) (via 1 + Σ ≤ Π(1+·)). Divergent reciprocal-prime capacity ⇒ arbitrarily many disjoint nonempty prime banks each with parity ratio < 1/(4L).
## FermiBankParityTensorGram
- Product parity packets over banks are 0/1, one-hot odd-bank choices have disjoint supports; Gram factorizes = Π_i (card/4)(Π(1+r) ± Π(1−r)).
## FermiFinitePrimeAntichainBound / FermiBoundedPrimeCapacityObservability
- Antichain with odd indices whose prime factors lie in finite P: selected homogeneous ≥ M_odd·Π(p−1)/(p+1)·(1/3)Σa², sieve homogeneous ≤ M_odd·Π(p+1)/(p−1)·(4/3)Σa² ⇒ physical bound sieveTotal ≤ 8(Π(p+1)/(p−1))²·selectedTotal (all horizons, signed amplitudes). With Σ_{p∈P}1/p ≤ M: constant 8e^{6M} (Euler correction bound from RHTower.Sieve). Bounded reciprocal capacity ⇒ uniform native observability.
## FermiDyadicAntichainSupportRepresentation
- Admissible packet (horizon N, allowed primes): distinct active indices in [1,N] have distinct odd parts, all prime factors of active odd parts allowed. Every admissible packet = parameterized antichain on [1,N] (odd part support, depth = 2-adic valuation, amplitude c(n)/n; nonneg preserved); both native energies read only [1,N]. ⇒ finite-prime constant 8B² and bounded-capacity constant 8e^{6M} for EVERY admissible signed packet.
## FermiMultiBankPrimeCube / FermiBankPacketNormalizedGram
- Independent Boolean choices in disjoint prime banks multiply to injective odd squarefree indices; GCD kernel = product of bank kernels. Bank masses A = Π(1+1/p), D = Π(1−1/p) = θA (θ = Π(p−1)/(p+1)); parity class mass E = 2^{|T|}(A+D)/4; parity correlation ρ = (1−θ)/(1+θ). One-hot parity packets (odd in bank j, even elsewhere): 0/1, disjoint, nonempty; normalized Gram = 1 on diagonal, ρ_jρ_k off; θ < 1/(4L) ⇒ entries ∈ [1 − 1/L, 1].
## FermiDivergentCapacityAntichainPackets (start)
- Divergent reciprocal capacity: L disjoint banks with θ < 1/(4L); one-hot packet of bank t at dyadic level t+1 (amplitude 1 then ½) gives a NONNEGATIVE dyadic antichain with homogeneous P_h ≥ L−1, 0 < Q_h ≤ (L−1)/(2L) + 1/3; native P ≥ (L−1)/2, 0 < Q < 5/6 ⇒ unbounded energy ratio.
- Mechanism: bank t at dyadic level t+1, amplitude 1 (t=0) or ½; normalized ray state k ≤ L is a convex combination of normalized packets (Σ = 1), halves beyond L. Gram entries ∈ [1−1/L,1] ⇒ each of first L states has norm² ≥ 1−1/L (P_h ≥ L−1); each interior selected difference = half-state minus half-new-packet has norm² ≤ 1/(2L); geometric tail = ⅓ terminal norm². Nearly-parallel packets make the ray almost constant ⇒ selected jumps tiny while full energy grows linearly in L.
## FermiDyadicAntichainPrimeCapacityClassification (Theorem A)
- For a set Π of odd primes, TFAE: (1) ∃C, P ≤ C·Q for every admissible signed dyadic-antichain packet; (2) same for nonnegative packets; (3) Σ_{p∈Π}1/p < ∞; (4) finite reciprocal subsums bounded. (4→1) constant 8e^{6M}; (2→4) divergent banks. Arbitrary allowed set A: only its odd primes matter (odd parts never see 2).
- Consequence: over all odd primes (Σ1/p = ∞) native Fermi observability fails even on nonnegative dyadic antichains.
## FermiHomogeneousPlainSumsAndImpulseMasses
- n = 2^k(2j+1) reindexing: P_h = Σ_{n≥1} s_N(n)²/n²; Q_h = Σ_{n odd} J(n)²/n² + Σ_{4|n} J(n)²/n². P_h/2 ≤ P ≤ P_h, Q_h/2 ≤ Q ≤ Q_h (P,Q reciprocal-pronic natives).
- Scalar dyadic impulse at level j: selected mass 13/12 (j=0), 1/3 (j=1), 4/3 (j ≥ 2); full mass 4/3 always. Level 1 is the worst depth (ratio 4).
## AllHorizonRadicalSawtoothAndDefectReduction
- Squarefree support is preserved by harmonic division c(s)/s ⇒ both active sawtooth endpoints (k−1 and k) are invariant under k ↦ dk (d|k) and k ↦ k^m at EVERY horizon.
- Defect dissipation: if s_N(l) = s_N(k), 2 ≤ k ≤ l, then 2(D(l−1) − D(k−1)) = −Σ_{j=k}^{l−1}(s(j+1)−s(j))² ≤ 0. Along powers k^a, D(k^b − 1) ≤ D(k^a − 1) for a ≤ b (any horizon).
- Damped unit mass: ε ≥ 0, k ≥ 2: τ_ε(k) + |μ(k)k^{−ε}| ≤ 1 (a prime p|k gives τ_ε(k) ≤ 1 − p^{−ε} and |w| ≤ p^{−ε}). First-octave reciprocity ⇒ |s_N(c_ε,k)| ≤ 1 for all 2 ≤ k ≤ 2N ⇒ s_N(c_ε,k^m)² ≤ 1 ⇒ D(k^m − 1) ≤ 0 for every m ≥ 1 (Möbius = ε = 0). Arbitrarily large nonpositive checkpoints.
## HorizonRadicalStateAndDefectNormalForm
- Radical state R_N(k) = gcd(k, primorial N): positive, squarefree, divides k and N#; preserves every squarefree divisibility test d ≤ N; all squarefree-supported sieve responses factor through it; R_N(k) = R_N(l) ⇔ all squarefree observers agree; image = divisors of N#; idempotent; least positive representative of its class; R(kl) = lcm(R(k),R(l)); R(k^m) = R(k).
- Defect normal form: 2(D(k−1) − D(R(k)−1)) = −Σ_{j=R(k)}^{k−1}(Δs)² ⇒ the canonical representative MAXIMIZES the defect in its class; equality ⇔ additive flatness on [R(k), k−1]. Global bound ∀M D(M) ≤ B ⇔ finitely many tests at r−1, r | N#.
- R_N(k) ≤ 2N ⇒ damped (and Möbius) defect D(k−1) ≤ 0, no size bound on k.
## RadicalCheckpointSignRigidityAndGaugeLaw
- Unit shift c ↦ c + a·δ_1: active sawtooth (all horizons, all positions) blind; sieve responses shift by a (N ≥ 1); all differences invariant; D_M ↦ D_M + a(s(M+1) − s(1)) (affine). Defect is quadratic under scaling.
- ∀a D_M(c + aδ_1) ≤ 0 ⇔ s(M+1) = s(1); else the unit-shift orbit realizes every real defect.
- R_N(k) = 1 ⇔ k trans-horizon rough (k ≥ 1). Rough ⇔ D(k−1) ≤ 0 for every nonnegative squarefree weight; converse witnessed by weights supported on {1, p} (p ≤ N visible prime of k), nonneg, unbounded positive defect. Sawtooth data cannot determine defect at nonrough checkpoints (unit gauge).
- Rough ⇔ D(k−1) ≤ 0 for ALL weights ⇔ for all squarefree ⇔ for nonneg squarefree ⇔ for normalized ones (c(1) = 1, 0 ≤ c ≤ 1; witness c = δ_1 + t·δ_p, t = 1/(|D|+1), defect t²D + t > 0). Universal bound B exists ⇔ rough ∧ B ≥ 0 (sharp 0). Möbius/damped families impose extra relations; no counterexample claimed for them.
## ActiveSawtoothPowerSamplingAndCalibratedRecovery
- 2·A_N(c;k,k) = Σ_{d|k} [c(d) − c(d)/d]·1_{d≤N} (divisor transform of nonunit part). Inverse: c(d) = (2d/(d−1))·Σ_{uv=d} μ(u)A(v,v) for 2 ≤ d ≤ N. Squarefree support: samples at j^m (any m ≥ 1, beyond any scale B) recover the same; only squarefree bases needed.
- Kernel of the sample map = agreement on [2,N]; unit coefficient = harmonic scalar H_N(c) = Σ c(d)/d difference. Samples determine every additive jump and Dirichlet window; samples + H determine all of c on [1,N] and every prefix defect. Defect ambiguity = (ΔH)·(s_b(M+1) − s_b(1)).
- Stability: |c(d) − b(d)| ≤ 4Σ_{uv=d}|ΔA(v^m)| (l¹, factor 2d/(d−1) ≤ 4).
## ActiveSawtoothInterpolationAndSharpConditioning
- Surjectivity: any squarefree sample vector a and any harmonic scalar H realized by an explicit squarefree-supported weight, simultaneously at every common power (beyond any scale). Each sample indispensable even at fixed H. Exact sup-norm amplification at squarefree d: (2d/(d−1))·(τ(d) − 1), attained by Möbius-sign sample vector; independent of the power m.
- Sharp constant iff: (∀ squarefree c, H fixed, |samples| ≤ 1 ⇒ |c(d)| ≤ C) ⇔ C ≥ (2d/(d−1))(τ(d)−1); same for two-weight errors (calibration does not help).
## FiniteMultiplicativeObservationState
- L_N = lcm(1..N); state σ_N(k) = lcm{d ≤ N : d|k}: divides k and L_N; preserves all tests d ≤ N; complete invariant for ALL real weights (σ(k) = σ(l) ⇔ ∀c s_N(c,k) = s_N(c,l)); idempotent; σ(kl) = σ(σ(k)σ(l)); σ(k + tL_N) = σ(k) ⇒ every state recurs beyond every bound; multiplicative contexts add nothing; σ(k) divides every integer with the same observation.
## SuccessorStateObstructionAndPhaseRefinement
- Phase φ_N(k) = k mod L_N. φ(k) = φ(l) ⇔ k ≡ l mod d ∀ d ≤ N ⇔ all future observations agree ⇔ the first N future observations agree (t < N). Phase carries successor, addition, multiplication.
- Successor descends to multiplicative states ⇔ N ≤ 2 (any nonlinear update F exists ⇔ N ≤ 2). Witness for N > 2: k = 1 + tL, l = L − 1 + tL have equal states (both rough-like = state of 1) but successors 2 + tL vs L + tL differ (N | L, N ∤ 2); occurs beyond every window.
## ArithmeticUncertaintyAndSpikeCancellation
- Spike cancellation: 2D(M) = −Σ_{k=1}^{M−1}(Δs)² + 2s(M)s(M+1) − s(M)² − s(1)². ⇒ s(M)s(M+1) ≤ 0 ⇒ 2D(M) ≤ −s(M)² − s(1)² ≤ 0; s(M) = 0 ⇒ D(M) ≤ −s(1)²/2 regardless of spike s(M+1).
- Two-point average: D(M−1) + D(M) = −Σ_{k=1}^{M−1}(Δs)² + s(M)s(M+1) − s(1)² ⇒ ≤ 0 if s(M)s(M+1) ≤ s(1)².
- Path Poincaré: (f(a+L) − f(a))² ≤ L·Σ_{j<L}(Δf)² (identity L((L+1)(S+Y²) − (X+Y)²) = (L+1)(LS − X²) + (X−LY)²). Uncertainty: spike at distance L from a zero anchor costs s(a+L)²/L Dirichlet energy; from a rough anchor (s(a) = s(1)) costs (s(a+L) − s(1))²/L.
## MultiPointDefectAveragingAndPeriodConservation
- s_N(c,·) is L_N-periodic. Period Dirichlet energy E_N(c) = Σ_{j<L_N}(Δs)² is phase-independent. 2(D(M + L_N) − D(M)) = −E_N (all M) ⇒ D(M + qL_N) = D(M) − qE_N/2: exact linear drift; phase subsequences antitone.
- E_N = 0 ⇔ s constant ⇔ all defects 0. Dichotomy: flat response with D ≡ 0, or E_N > 0 and D → −∞ along every phase subsequence. Oscillation within one period: (s(a+t) − s(a))² ≤ t·E_N (t ≤ L_N).
- Full-period moving window W(M) = Σ_{j<L_N} D(M+j): 2(W(M+1) − W(M)) = −E_N exactly; average slope −E_N/(2L_N): phase fluctuations cancel.
## PrimeChainSieveBridgeAndSpectralCutoff
- Renormalization: p prime, gcd(p,b) = 1: s_H(c, pb) − s_H(c, b) = s_{⌊H/p⌋}(c(p·), b) (every horizon, every scale).
- Low-pass cutoff: p > H ⇒ s_H(c, p^r b) = s_H(c, b) ⇒ CHMD prime-chain bipartite energy and variance energy of s_H vanish for all p > H.
- Excited-shell collapse (squarefree c, any p): p|m ⇒ s_H(m) = s_H(p·ordCompl_p m); excited-to-excited jumps vanish; ground-to-excited jump = s_{⌊H/p⌋}(c(p·), b). Right-chain scatter vanishes; bipartite/variance energy = [b ∈ chainLeft]·|chainRight|·s_{⌊H/p⌋}(c(p·),b)² (rank one, ground state only).
- Multiplicative fixed point: c(pd) = c(p)c(d) on d|b ⇒ variance = [b ∈ L]|R|c(p)²s_{⌊H/p⌋}(c,b)². Terminal octave H/2 < p ≤ H (⌊H/p⌋ = 1): s_H(c,pb) − s_H(c,b) = c(p) for EVERY weight and every b coprime to p; variance quantized to [b ∈ L]|R|c(p)².
## SlidingPeriodPoincareCoercivityAndHarmonicDecomposition
- Spectral (Selberg) coordinates Y_{N,c}(e) (Y(1) = H_N(c) harmonic sum). Selberg diagonal form S = Σ_{e≤N} φ(e)Y(e)²; successor reserve R = Σ_{e=2}^N (φ(e) − μ(e))Y(e)². Excess 2R − (S − H²) = Σ_{e=2}^N (φ(e) − 2μ(e))Y(e)² ≥ 0 (2μ(e) ≤ φ(e), equality only e = 6) ⇒ S − H² ≤ 2R for EVERY weight (no harmonic-null hypothesis).
- Any common return period L (all d ≤ N divide L): s_N is L-periodic; every sliding window [M+1, M+L]: Σs² = L·S, Σs(k)s(k+1) = L·(Bézout coprime form), Σ(Δs)² = 2L·R. Combined prefix defect is L-periodic; D(M + qL) = D(M) − qL·R; D(M) = D(M mod L) − ⌊M/L⌋L·R.
- Unit-step torus Poincaré with constant 1 (independent of N, L ~ e^N): Σ_{window}s² − L·H² ≤ Σ_{window}(Δs)², excess L·(Poincaré excess). Mechanism: gcd(k,k+1) = 1 kills non-coprime correlations in one step (generic torus constant would be L²/4π²).
## BuchstabFermiCovarianceAndQuotientChainBridge (start)
- One horizon step: s_{N+1}(k) = s_N(k) + c(N+1)1_{N+1|k}; band self-energy = c(N+1)²⌊M/(N+1)⌋; E_{N+1}(M) − E_N(M) = c(N+1)²Q + 2c(N+1)Σ_{j≤Q} s_N((N+1)j), Q = ⌊M/(N+1)⌋. Non-squarefree N+1 & squarefree c ⇒ E invariant.
- Prime horizon p = N+1 (rough): s_N(pj) = s_N(j) ⇒ ΔE = c(p)²Q + 2c(p)Σ_{j≤Q}s_N(j). Quadratic window p ≤ M < p² (Q ≤ N sub-horizon): Möbius Σ_{j≤Q}s_N(μ,j) = 1 ⇒ ΔE = Q − 2 (Q = 1: contraction by 1; Q = 2: conservation). Damped first band: ΔE = p^{−2ε} − 2p^{−ε} ≤ 0.
## TotientObservabilityThresholdAndValuationFiberCollision
- σ_N(k) = gcd(k, L_N); fiber of k mod L_N ↦ gcd over g | L_N has size φ(L_N/g).
- Prime-power totient observability: W consecutive gcd(k+t, p^a) observations determine k mod p^a ⇔ W ≥ φ(p^a) = p^a − p^{a−1}. Collision: k = 1, l = 1 + p^{a−1} (shift by valuation clock p^{a−1}) agree for t+1 < φ(p^a) (gcd(x,p^a) = gcd(x,p^{a−1}) when p^a ∤ x). Sufficiency by induction: return times forced into the tail of length p^{a−1} where residues mod p^{a−1} are unique.
- Horizon threshold Φ_max(N) = max_{p^a ≤ N} φ(p^a) < N; any W ≥ Φ_max(N) consecutive full observations determine the phase k mod L_N (improves W = N).
- Squarefree observers: radical phase k mod N#; W ≥ max_{p≤N}(p−1) radical-state observations suffice; W = p − 2 fails on k = 1, l = 2.
## ValuationTraceDistanceAndPrimePowerPeriod
- Trace distance d_L(W;k,l) = Hamming distance of (gcd(k+t,L))_{t<W} and (gcd(l+t,L))_{t<W}; decomposes as ⌊W/L⌋·d(L) + d(W mod L). Distinct phases disagree at both divisibility return times ⇒ ≥ 2 per full period.
- Prime power q = p^a: sharp floor F_q(W) = 2⌊W/q⌋ + [φ(q) ≤ W mod q]; attained for every W by the collision pair (1, 1 + p^{a−1}) (disagreements exactly at t = φ(q)−1 and q−1).
## ValuationTraceCoprimeCompletionAndObservationState
- Projection to a divisor never increases trace distance; CRT completion (k ≡ 0 mod coprime cofactor) lifts any local pair with exact distance. Exact separation for any modulus L > 0: (distance ≤ b ⇒ k ≡ l mod L) ⇔ b < F_{p^{v_p(L)}}(W) for every p | L.
- p^a | L_N ⇔ p^a ≤ N; σ_N(k) = gcd(k, L_N) (literal lcm state = gcd model).
## ValuationTraceErrorsErasuresAndRecoveryClock
- Coding lemma: two words admit a common received word with ≤ e errors and ≤ s erasures ⇔ Hamming distance ≤ 2e + s.
- Prime-power recovery clock C_q(e,s) = (e + ⌊s/2⌋)q + (s even ? φ(q) : q); 2e + s < F_q(W) ⇔ C_q(e,s) ≤ W (parity dependence).
## SharpCorruptedValuationClockRecovery (start)
- Horizon state observations with ≤ e corrupted and ≤ s erased symbols determine the phase k mod L_N ⇔ W ≥ max_{p^a ≤ N} C_{p^a}(e,s) (empty max 0 for N ≤ 1); ambiguity witness below threshold.
- Recovery ⇔ trace separation at budget 2e+s; recovery clock monotone under divisibility of prime powers; collision witness below the clock.
## ShortWindowCorrelationDefectAndIsoStateClassification
- Short window [M₀+1, M₀+M]: D_win = D(M₀+M) − D(M₀) = C − V (shifted correlation minus second moment) = ΔLcmFractDefect + ΔBézoutSawtooth − M·R (classical Selberg/Coppola–Murty–Saha remainders; triangle bound O((Σ|c|)²) beats main term only if M ≫ N²).
- Polarization: 2D_win = −E_win + s(M₀+M+1)² − s(M₀+1)². Boundary depends only on endpoint states, not on period return.
- Iso-state classification: D_win ≤ 0 ∀ weights ⇔ σ_N(M₀+M+1) = σ_N(M₀+1); ∀ squarefree weights ⇔ equal radical states (unit-shift gauge).
- Contraction regimes: windows [a, da−1] (d|a) and [a, a^m−1] have zero boundary ⇒ 2D = −E (squarefree c, any horizon). Right endpoint zero (Möbius zero cone q·r, 2 ≤ q ≤ N, r rough) ⇒ 2D = −E − s(M₀+1)² for any start. Damped: rough start, 2 ≤ end ≤ 3N ⇒ D ≤ −E/2 ≤ 0 (|s_N(c_ε)| ≤ 1 up to 3N).
- Universal envelope D_win ≤ ½s(end)² ≤ ½(Σ|c|)².
## NonnegativeWeightEndpointOrderAndCalibratedDefect (start)
- σ(k) | σ(l) ⇔ visible divisor inclusion; radical analogue via primes. Nonnegative weights: s monotone in divisibility; σ(k) | σ(l) ⇔ s_N(c,k) ≤ s_N(c,l) ∀ nonneg c.
- Nonneg c: s(end) ≤ s(start) ⇒ D_win ≤ −E_win/2. Reverse state order σ(end) | σ(start) (or radical order for squarefree) ⇒ D_win ≤ −E/2.
- Classification (nonneg cone): D_win ≤ 0 ∀ nonneg c ⇔ σ_N(M₀+M+1) | σ_N(M₀+1); squarefree nonneg ⇔ radical-state divisibility. Necessity survives fixing harmonic scalar H > 0 and box 0 ≤ c ≤ H: two-atom witness on {1, q} (q newly visible divisor/prime), defect tH + t²Q > 0. H ≤ 0 on nonneg cone ⇒ all visible coefficients 0 ⇒ defect 0. Full harmonic-slice classification: H ≤ 0 ∨ state order. Signed Möbius/damped families are NOT in these cones.
## DownwardBuchstabL2TelescopeAndSawtoothCoupling
- Linear prefix: Σ_{j≤Q} s_N(c,j) = Q·H_N(c) + discrepancy = Q·H_N(c) − Σ_d c(d){Q/d} (Báez-Duarte sawtooth); (fract sum)² ≤ variance·dual energy.
- Prime step p = N+1: ΔE = Q(c(p)² + 2c(p)H_N(c)) − 2c(p)·FractSum(N,c,Q), Q = ⌊M/p⌋.
- Square completion: Q(ΔE + E_N(Q)) = (c(p)Q + Σ_{j≤Q}s_N(j))² + (Q·E_N(Q) − (Σs)²) ≥ 0 ⇒ downward subadditivity E_N(M) ≤ E_{N+1}(M) + E_N(⌊M/p⌋) (every prime p = N+1, every c). Upward: E_{N+1}(M) ≤ E_N(M) + 2c(p)²Q + E_N(Q).
- Telescope over horizons; squarefree c: only squarefree steps n+1 contribute. Möbius top closure: 1 ≤ M ≤ N₂ ⇒ E_{N₂}(M; μ) = 1; hence E_N(M; μ) = 1 − Σ_{n∈[N,M−1], n+1 squarefree} ΔE_n.
## OffLineHyperbolicPrimeChainExcessAndCardinalRecurrence
- Polar edge factorization: (r_m cosθ_m − r_k cosθ_k)² + (r_m sinθ_m − r_k sinθ_k)² = (r_m − r_k)² + r_m r_k·[2(1 − cos(θ_m − θ_k))].
- Off-line envelope r_β(m) = m^β (β = Re ρ − ½), r_β r_{−β} = 1. Symmetrized excess on any bipartite cut: ½(V(β) + V(−β)) − V(0) = ½(Var(r_β) + Var(r_{−β})) + A(β,θ), A = Σ ½(e^{βℓ/2} − e^{−βℓ/2})²·(critical phase jump), ℓ = log m + log k; all terms ≥ 0 (cosh − 1 = 2sinh²).
- Strict: β ≠ 0 and nonempty prime-chain cut ⇒ critical variance < symmetrized off-line variance for every phase θ (radial part already positive since log m < u < log k).
- Chain wave Φ = e^{βt}cos(θ₀ + αt): second-order recurrence Φ(t+τ) − 2e^{βτ}cos(ατ)Φ(t) + e^{2βτ}Φ(t−τ) = 0 (τ = log p); same for sine. Phase-free step Casimir: two-channel step² = e^{2βt}((e^{βτ} − 1)² + 2e^{βτ}(1 − cos ατ)).
- Note: identity-level only; no link to actual zeros (an energy comparison of model profiles).
## CompositeSquarefreeStepAndMultiOctaveL2Energy (start)
- General step (any n+1): ΔE = c(n+1)²Q + 2c(n+1)Σ_{j≤Q}s_n(c,(n+1)j). M < n+1 ⇒ ΔE = 0. First band (Q = 1): ΔE = c(n+1)² + 2c(n+1)s_n(c,n+1); Möbius s_n(μ, n+1) = −μ(n+1) (any n ≥ 1) ⇒ ΔE = −μ(n+1)² ≤ 0, prime or composite. First-octave closed form: E_N(M; μ) = 1 + Σ_{n∈[N,M)} μ(n+1)² for 1 ≤ N ≤ M ≤ 2N+1.
- Second band (Q = 2, n ≥ 2): even n+1 ⇒ ΔE = −2μ(n+1)²; odd n+1 ⇒ ΔE = 0 (s_n(μ, 2(n+1)) = 0 by 2-adic switching). Every step monotone E_{n+1} ≤ E_n for M < 3(n+1).
- Dilated subadditivity (any n, c): E_n(M) ≤ E_{n+1}(M) + Σ_{j≤Q}s_n(c,(n+1)j)².
- Two-band closed form (2 ≤ N ≤ M < 3(N+1)): E_N(M; μ) = 1 + Σ_{n∈[N,M)} [M < 2(n+1) ? μ(n+1)² : (2|n+1 ? 2μ(n+1)² : 0)].
- Dilated energy Σ_{j≤Q}s_n(c,(n+1)j)² = sub-sum of E_n(M) on multiples of n+1, ≤ E_n(M); Cauchy–Schwarz on dilated sequence.
## GlobalPrimeChainHyperbolicCoercivityAndClockStepBound
- Lift to global CHMD chainVariance (Σ_p log p Σ_b over divisors of N): ½(G(β) + G(−β)) − G(0) = ½(chainVar(r_β) + chainVar(r_{−β})) + global amplification ≥ 0.
- Valuation-clock step: r_β(pb) = r_β(b)p^β; (r_β(b) − r_β(pb))² = r_β(b)²(p^β − 1)². If b, pb | N, ordCompl_p b = b, log b < u < log pb: log p·r_β(b)²(p^β − 1)² ≤ chainVar(N,u,r_β), and the global off-line excess ≥ (log p/2)(r_β(b)²(p^β−1)² + r_{−β}(b)²(p^{−β}−1)²) > 0 for β ≠ 0.
## ThirdBandCompositeStepAndLinearL2EnergyBounds (start)
- Third band (Q = 3): prime step p ≥ 5 gives ΔE = +1 > 0 (Q − 2). s_n(μ, 3(n+1)) for n ≥ 3: 3|n+1 ⇒ −μ(n+1); gcd(n+1,6) = 1 ⇒ 0; 2|n+1, 3∤n+1, squarefree ⇒ −μ(n+1). Uniformly μ(n+1)s_n(μ,3(n+1)) = −μ(n+1)²·[2|n+1 ∨ 3|n+1].
- Third-band step (n ≥ 3, Q = 3): ΔE = −3μ(n+1)² if 2|n+1; −μ(n+1)² if odd and 3|n+1; +μ(n+1)² if gcd(n+1,6) = 1 (first non-dissipative composite/prime steps). Three-band closed form for 3 ≤ N ≤ M < 4(N+1). Linear bounds: E_N(M; μ) ≤ 1 + k(M − N) on k bands (k = 1,2,3).
## MultiShellPrimePowerHyperbolicSumAndGroundCutCoercivity
- Shell p^r b: (r_β(b) − r_β(p^r b))² = r_β(b)²(p^{rβ} − 1)²; global chainVariance ≥ log p·(that) across any straddling cut.
- Ground cut u ∈ (0, log 2), any N: Σ_{p|N} log p·(p^β − 1)² ≤ chainVar(N,u,r_β); off-line excess ≥ Σ_{p|N} (log p/2)((p^β−1)² + (p^{−β}−1)²).
## AllCutPrimeChainCoercivityAndContinuousDensityBridge (start)
- (e^x − 1)² + (e^{−x} − 1)² ≥ x² ⇒ quadratic floor ½β²Σ_{p|N}(log p)³ ≤ ½(chainVar(r_β) + chainVar(r_{−β})) at ground cut.
- Simultaneous shells along one chain: Σ_{r∈S} r_β(b)²(p^{rβ} − 1)² ≤ chain variance; straddling bases B at any cut u: log p(p^{rβ}−1)²Σ_{b∈B}r_β(b)² ≤ chainVar(N,u,r_β).
- Continuous pullback: v^cos_{β,γ}(x) = e^{−βx}cos γx, v^sin = −e^{−βx}sin γx; v(u − log m) = e^{−βu}·(polar channel with r_β, θ_m = γ log m − γu). CHMD chainDensity/density of the wave pair = e^{−2βu}·log p·fiber(N,u)·(discrete off-line variance). Cut-normalized symmetrized density − critical = fiber(N,u)·(½(chainVar(r_β) + chainVar(r_{−β})) + amplification); ground cut, N ≥ 2: ≥ ½fiber·β²Σ_{p|N}(log p)³.
- Note: model-wave computation; the coercivity is for the explicit test wave, not a statement about ξ's zeros.
## OddEigenvectorNodalModulusBalanceAndFourthBandStepLaw (start)
- CHMD odd sector: odd graph vector p (reflect p = −p): positive/negative lobes carry equal vertex/edge norms; balancedNodalGraph p = (½‖p_fst‖²)·absGraph p; p ⟂ absGraph p in every inner product and sourcePair.
- Eigenvector resolvent w = μw, q = rieszLift w: sourceEnergy(|q|) − 4·sourcePair(q⁺, q⁻) = ((1 − 2μ)/μ)‖q_fst‖². μ > ½ ⇒ sourceEnergy(|q|) < 4·sourcePair(q⁺,q⁻) ≤ 0. RH ⇔ ∀ odd eigenvector with μ > ½: 4·sourcePair(q⁺,q⁻) ≤ sourceEnergy(|q|).
- Conditional RH criterion (premises, not proved): if every odd eigenvector with μ > ½ has primitive-modulus Rayleigh ≤ ½ and oneL-defect 2⟨|p|_fst, oneL⟩² ≤ −4·sourcePair(p⁺,p⁻), then RH. (Open inputs; trap: do not cite as progress.)
- Centered step (n ≥ 1, M ≥ n+1, B = ⌊M/(n+1)⌋): ΔE = (B − 2)μ(n+1)² + 2μ(n+1)Σ_{q=2}^B s_n(μ, q(n+1)). With sharp 5n envelope |s_n(μ,k)| ≤ 1 (2 ≤ k ≤ 5n): B(n+1) ≤ 5n ⇒ E_n − E_{n+1} ≤ Bμ(n+1)². Four-band bound E_N(M; μ) ≤ 1 + 4(M − N) for 4 ≤ N ≤ M < 5(N+1).
## CompactWindowedOfflineChainIntegralAndOriginCollarDuality (start)
- Windowed waves w(x)e^{−βx}cos γx etc.: continuous, compact support ⇒ chainIntegral summable to Σ primeCrossing (CHMD complete chain HasSum).
- Windowed two-channel jump = radial jump + radial product × phase jump; w ≥ 0 ⇒ two-channel varianceEnergy, chainDensity, density, chainIntegral ≥ pure radial ones.
- Two-point plateau: only w(u) = 1 and w(u − log p) = 1 needed for ground step bound e^{−2βu}(p^β − 1)² ≤ windowed variance; multi-prime density bound e^{−2βu}fiber·Σ log p(p^β−1)²; symmetrized quadratic floor ½β²fiber·Σ(log p)³.
## CenteredSquareCompletionAndMultiBandResidueCancellation
- S₂ = Σ_{q=2}^B s_n(μ, q(n+1)). Square completion: (B−2)(E_n − E_{n+1}) = (μ(n+1)S₂)² − ((B−2)μ(n+1)² + μ(n+1)S₂)² ≤ S₂². B ≥ 3 ⇒ E_n − E_{n+1} ≤ S₂²/(B−2) (ground term fully absorbed).
- Half-band cancellation: B ≥ 2 and |S₂| ≤ (B−2)/2 ⇒ E_n ≤ E_{n+1}. So controlling the higher-quotient sum S₂ (a Möbius sum over multiples) is the open input for large ratios.
- Two-step parity pairs: second band E_n − E_{n+2} ≤ 2; third band ≤ 4, and = 3μ(n+1)² − 1 ≤ 2 when n+1 even, n+2 squarefree, 3 ∤ n+2 (e.g. n+2 prime ≥ 5).
## OddPolarCollarParallelogramAndPrimeClockBridge (start)
- evenFold v(x) = v(|x|). For odd v on a bipartite chain cut (left nodes u − log m > 0, right nodes u − log k ≤ 0): v(u−log m) − v(u−log k) = evenFold v(·m) + evenFold v(·k). Parallelogram: bip(v) + bip(evenFold v) = 2ΣΣ(v_m² + v_k²); difference = 4ΣΣ evenFold_m·evenFold_k. v ≥ 0 on [0,∞) ⇒ origin flip reinforces jumps: variance/chainDensity/density/chainIntegral of evenFold v ≤ those of v (compact support inherited).
- Smooth even complex-hyperbolic pair: w₀(x)cosh(βx)cos(γx), w₀(x)sinh(βx)sin(γx) (both even, C^∞ when w₀ is). Pointwise γ-cancellation: (Re)² + (Im)² − (critical)² = (w₀ sinh βx)². ⇒ CHMD odd-profile collar mixed density and per-divisor crossing+collar excess over β = 0 = Λ(d)∫_0^{log d} collarMixed(a, w₀ sinh β·) ≥ 0 (a > 0).
- Finite packet: stationary energy of a sum ≥ 0 ⇒ off-diagonal stationary sum ≥ −Σ diag ≥ −(1 + ‖groundPerturbation‖)Σ‖a_i‖²; off-diag sourcePair = stationary − arch − primeShift ⇒ lower bound.
## OddEigenvectorHalfMassLobeAndStrictPrimeShiftClosure (start)
- Primitive p (⟨p_fst, 1_L⟩ = 0): both lobes have equal ground projections; ⟨|p|,1⟩ = 2⟨p⁺,1⟩; nodal defect 2⟨|p|,1⟩² = 8⟨p⁺,1⟩⟨p⁻,1⟩ (cross-lobe rank one, same footing as prime-shift cross pairing).
- Odd eigenvector: sourceEnergy(|q|) − 4·sourceEnergy(q^±) = ((2μ−1)/μ)‖q_fst‖² (cross term eliminated); sourceEnergy(|q|) = 4‖q⁺_snd‖² − ‖q_fst‖²/μ; three-term balance ‖q⁺_snd‖² − ⟨stationary cross⟩ + ⟨primeShift q⁺, q⁻⟩ = ((1−μ)/(2μ))‖q_fst‖²; μ > ½ ⇒ each lobe energy ≤ ((1−2μ)/(2μ))‖q_fst‖² < 0.
- Conditional: Rayleigh ≤ ½ on primGraph(|q|) and 2⟨q⁺,1⟩⟨q⁻,1⟩ ≤ ⟨primeShift q⁺, q⁻⟩ ⇒ μ < ½ strictly. RH ⇔ ∀ odd eigenvectors sourceEnergy(|q|) ≤ 4 sourceEnergy(q⁺).
- Conditional sufficiency for RH (premises open): Rayleigh ≤ ½ + 2⟨q⁺,1⟩⟨q⁻,1⟩ ≤ ⟨primeShift q⁺,q⁻⟩ for all odd eigenvectors with μ ∈ (½,1). Equivalence RH ⇔ sourceEnergy(|q|) ≤ 4·sourceEnergy(q⁺) for odd eigenvectors (μ < 1 automatically).
## OddProfileCollarToContinuumArchimedeanBridge
- Odd profile m_a(x) = (Θ(x−a) − Θ(x+a))/(2 sinh(a/2)) strictly increasing on [0,a] (Θ even, decreasing on [0,∞)). Low-prime window 3t/2 ≤ a, x ∈ (0,t/2): m_a(x) < m_a(t−x) < m_a(t) < m_a(t+x).
- Obstructions: collar jump ~O(x) vs 2D arch integrand ~O(x²) near origin; 1D line vs 2D area. Resolution: Schur identity direct − collar + (m_a(x)/(m_a(t+x) − m_a(t−x)))·across = (m_a(x)/Δ)(m_a(t+x)(w(x)−w(x+t)) − m_a(t−x)(w(x)−w(t−x)))² ≥ 0; across jump between t−x and t+x (both ≥ t/2 from origin). Anchor bound collar ≤ 2 direct + 2 across.
- Arch Lévy density ω(s) = e^{−s/2}/(1 − e^{−2s}): ω(2y+s) ≤ e^{−y}ω(s) ⇒ (1 − e^{−y})ω(s)·shiftJump ≤ absorbed arch integrand; strip lower bound with y ≥ y₀, s ≤ M.
- Continuum bridge: z ∈ (3t/2+δ, 3t/2+δ+L) ⊂ (3t/2, a]: across ≤ 2(leg t−x→z) + 2(leg t+x→z), each leg ≤ absorbed integrand / ((1−e^{−t/2})ω(t+δ+L)). Integrate over z then x; reflection t−x and translation t+x fold onto y ∈ (t/2, 3t/2).
- Integrated bridge (3t/2 + δ + L ≤ a, w continuous): L(1−e^{−t/2})ω(t+δ+L)·∫_0^t collar ≤ 4L(...)∫_0^{t/2} direct + 8∫_{t/2}^{3t/2} absorbedArchSlice(δ, t+δ+L). With Schur ratio hypothesis m_a(x) ≤ κ(m_a(t+x) − m_a(t−x)): collar − 2·direct ≤ 4κ·(2D slice); even compactly supported w ⇒ 2∫_{t/2}^∞ direct ≤ full-line jump + 4κ·slice. Summed over low primes Λ(d)-weighted.
## UnconditionalSchurRatioAndGlobalLowPrimeBudget (start)
- Θ log-derivative bounds: Θ'(u) ≤ −πe^{2u}Θ(u) (u ≥ ½log 2) ⇒ drop Θ(u₁) − Θ(u₂) ≥ πe^{2u₁}Θ(u₂)(u₂−u₁); Θ'(u) ≥ −2π(e^{2u}+2)Θ(u) (u ≥ 0) ⇒ drop ≤ 2π(e^{2v₂}+2)Θ(v₁)(v₂−v₁).
- Drop comparison (t ≥ 0, a ≥ 3t/2 + ½log 2, x ∈ [0,t/2]): Θ(a−x) − Θ(a+x) ≤ 4e^{4t}exp(−πe^{2(a−t+x)}(t−2x))·(Θ(a−t−x) − Θ(a−t+x)) ≤ 4e^{4t}(...): a-dependence cancels (e^{2a+t}/e^{2a−3t} = e^{4t}).
- 2 sinh(a/2)(m_a(t+x) − m_a(t−x)) = (Θ(a−t−x) − Θ(a−t+x)) + (Θ(a+t−x) − Θ(a+t+x)) (second ≥ 0) ⇒ unconditional horizon-uniform Schur ratio m_a(x) ≤ 4e^{4t}(m_a(t+x) − m_a(t−x)) (κ = 4e^{4t}, independent of a). Hence collar − direct ≤ 4e^{4t}·across pointwise; collar − 2·direct ≤ 16e^{4t}·slice (premise-free, needs ½log 2 ≤ δ + L).
- Premise-free positivity: full-line prime shift jump·L(1−e^{−t/2})ω(t+δ+L) + 16e^{4t}·slice ≥ 2·tail ≥ 0. absorbedArchSlice monotone in upper cutoff; every per-prime slice ≤ global rectangle ∫_0^a slice(δ,a); slice ≤ half-line arch direct − collar difference. Summed low-prime budget with weights 16Λ(d)e^{4 log d} = 16Λ(d)d⁴ (polynomial growth in d; the constant is not uniform).
- Unweighted ratio primeCollarArchRatio(δ,L,d) = 16d⁴/(L(1 − d^{−1/2})ω(log d + δ + L)); Obstacle-B finite sum: Σ Λ(d)∫collar ≤ 2ΣΛ(d)∫_0^∞ direct + (ΣΛ(d)·ratio)·global slice.
## SupportRadiusCollarCutoffAndAllScaleObstacleBBridge
- w even, supported in (−R,R). For 2R ≤ t ≤ a − R and x ∈ (0,t/2): collar ≤ direct pointwise (x ≥ R: collar = 0; x < R: w(t−x) = w(x+t) = 0, compare m_a(t−x) ≤ m_a(x+t)) ⇒ zero archimedean cost above twice the support radius; full-line jump ≥ 2·tail alone.
- Two-regime gluing (a ≥ 3R + δ + L): low primes log d < 2R use the Schur bridge, higher ones cost nothing; truncated ratio vanishes for log d ≥ 2R ⇒ Σ_{d∈S}Λ(d)·ratio ≤ C_supp(R,δ,L) = Σ_{d ≤ e^{2R}}Λ(d)·ratio, independent of S and a.
- Master all-scale Obstacle-B comparison (w even, supp ⊂ (−R,R), a ≥ 3R + δ + L, ½log 2 ≤ δ + L, log d + R ≤ a, plus integrability hypotheses on the 2D integrand): 2ΣΛ(d)·halfCollar(log d) ≤ 2ΣΛ(d)∫_0^∞ direct + C_supp·∬_{(0,∞)²}(ω(s) − ω(2y+s))·shiftJump; full-line version ≥ 2·tail. (Integrability of the 2D absorbed integrand is an assumed input in the master theorem.)
## CollectiveLowPrimeChebyshevTransportAndSinhAbsorption (start)
- Reflection x ↦ t − x leaves ∫_0^t invariant; collarMixed(a,w,t,x) = diag(x) + diag(t−x) with kernel Θ(t−x)(Ψ_a(x) + Ψ_a(t−x)) ⇒ ∫_0^t collarMixed = 2∫_0^t m_a(x)K(x)w(x)² (one-point diagonal potential).
- Ground-state collar duality: Λ(d)∫direct_ℝ + Λ(d)∫collarMixed = 2Λ(d)∫_0^∞ direct + primeCrossing(oddPolarWeight a w, d) (≥ 0).
- Two-regime collective identity: Σ(2·halfCollar) + Σ_{log d < 2R} crossing ≤ Σ_{high} 2Λ∫direct + Σ_{low} 2Λ∫ m_a K w²: zero 2D archimedean consumption.
- Chebyshev step Ψ_½(s) = Σ_{log d < s} Λ(d)d^{−1/2}; discrepancy Δ(s) = Ψ_½(s) − 2e^{s/2}; Ψ_½(s) ≤ (2 log 4 + 16)e^{s/2} (s ≥ 0) ⇒ |Δ(s)| ≤ (2 log 4 + 16)e^{s/2}. Stieltjes by parts on [0,T]: Σ_{log d < T}Λ(d)d^{−1/2}G(log d) = Ψ_½(T)G(T) − ∫_0^T Ψ_½ G'; main term 2e^{T/2}G(T) − ∫2e^{s/2}G' = 2G(0) + ∫_0^T e^{s/2}G(s).
- Discrepancy transport: Σ_{log d < T}Λ(d)d^{−1/2}G(log d) = 2G(0) + ∫_0^T e^{s/2}G + Δ(T)G(T) − ∫_0^T Δ·G' (PNT pole + discrepancy).
- sinh dominance: s ≤ sinh s; |Θ'| ≤ M on [a−R,a+R] ⇒ m_a(x) ≤ (2M/sinh(a/2))sinh(x/2) on [0,R]; ratio κ = 2M cosh(R/2)/(Θ(R)sinh(a/2)) ⇒ Ψ_a(x)cosh(x/2) ≤ κ sinh(x/2) ⇒ Θ-Doob cosh mass of oddPolarWeight ≤ κ·2∫ m_a sinh(x/2) w².
- Conditional (premise θ ≥ κ, θ ≤ 1 NOT proved; feasibility as R grows open): arch jump + 2·sameSide + Σ chainIntegral ≤ markovEnergy + θ·sourcePair(Ψ_a, p).
## PolarRadicalInvarianceNodalDriftAndFermiGradientBridge
- Ψ_a − Ψ_b ∈ signedRadical: sourcePair(Ψ_a, q) = ⟨q_fst, tanhL⟩ (shift-independent pole moment); oddPrimitivePart_b q is sourcePair-orthogonal to every Ψ_a; primitive/odd q: sourceEnergy(q) = weilForm(q) + 2⟨q_fst, tanhL⟩².
- Primitive eigenvector ν: Rayleigh excess of primGraph(|q|) = 4(⟨R q⁺, q⁻⟩ − 2(1−ν)⟨q⁺,1⟩⟨q⁻,1⟩); odd, ν > 0: 0 < −ν²sourcePair(q⁺,q⁻) ≤ ⟨R q⁺, q⁻⟩. Stationary Cauchy–Schwarz ‖q‖⁴ ≤ ⟨S|q|,|q|⟩·(4‖q⁺_snd‖² − ((1−μ)/μ)‖q‖² + arch + primeShift).
- Odd q, both lobes: ⟨P q^±, q^±⟩² + ¼⟨D_odd q, |q|⟩² ≤ ⟨P_diag q^±, q^±⟩² (chiral drift tightening).
- Second octave Fermi reciprocity: m ∈ [N, 2N−1]: second difference of fermiCellResponse(N, μ) at 2m+1 + forward gradient Δs_N(μ, 2m+1) = −2μ(m+1) ⇒ 2μ(m+1)² ≤ (curv)² + (grad)² ⇒ second-octave Fermi+gradient energy ≥ 2Σ_{first octave} μ² ≥ 2#oddSquarefreeWindow(N).
## PronicCausalQuadratureInteriorSawtoothAndFermiGramBridge
- Nyquist cell primitive F_c(t) = −e^{−t}((c−t)² + 1) − 2(c+1)t + t² + e^t has derivative e^{−t}(c + 1 − t − e^t)²; cell integral over [log n, log(n+1)] = c²/(n(n+1)) − 2c((1−1/(n+1))log(n+1) − (1−1/n)log n) + baseline: exact pronic quadratic coefficient 1/pronic(n).
- Pronic Abel: Σ_{n≤T} ψ(n)²/(n(n+1)) + ψ(T)²/(T+1) = Σ_{n<T}(ψ(n+1)² − ψ(n)²)/(n+1) (ψ(1) = 0).
- Interior sawtooth step identity (all n, no sub-horizon restriction): Δ(W² − I²) = (s(n) − Y(1))(s(n+1) − Y(1)) − (ΔI)² + ...; ⇒ upper bound dropping −(ΔI)².
- Damped interior: ∀ε > 0 ∃C: |interior gradient sum + 2·local impulse − 2·counterterm·H_T^comp| ≤ C (O(1) compensated remainder, uniform in T).
- Conditional RH criterion (premise open): cofinal ε → 0 with strict contraction 2·martingale + 2·counterterm·H ≤ (1−δ)·damped energy + C.
- Route PM cofactor weight: iso-state window ⇒ 2D = −E ≤ 0; energy-centered pronic Gram form = damped Ramanujan energy variance. Barban–Vehov divisorSource on [1,X]: unit L² energy, zero unit-shift correlation (all excess sits in (X, M]). Vasyunin–Fermi head/tail Gram = pronic sums; first tail cell energy μ(2M+1)²/pronic(2M+1).
## NodalDefectMarkovSubinvarianceAndCenteringPronicGramBridge (start)
- Disjoint lobe supports ⇒ |p| leaves all local diagonal forms (θ-volume norm, arch, prime diagonal, ground potential) invariant.
- Eigenvector μ: five-term |q| jump sum (stationary + primeGenerator − 4·stationary cross + 4·primeShift cross + ((2μ−1)/μ)‖q‖²) = c_∞‖θq‖² + ⟨V_prime q,q⟩ = ‖q‖² − ⟨V_ground q,q⟩ ≤ (1 + B_ground)‖q‖².
- 1/cosh(x/2) ≤ 1/cosh(R/2) + Θ(x)/(Θ(R)cosh(x/2)) (collar/far-field split; Θ even, decreasing).
- Spectral-to-nodal-defect: (sourceEnergy(|q|) − ((1−2μ)/μ)‖q‖²)² = 16 sourcePair(q⁺,q⁻)² ≤ 4(‖D‖² + statEnergy(D))(‖q‖² + statEnergy(|q|)), D = nodalDefectGraph μ q (a.e. ≥ 0 for 0 < μ ≤ 1; lobes Markov sub-invariant).
- Pronic Gram (BVI forms): meanCentered − discrepancy Gram = (Σ(φ(e)/2 − Λ(e))Y_e)² − (ΣΛ(e)Y_e)². Route PM Gram quadratic = (energy mean)² + energy variance (≥ mean²); variance splits at any H into head energy + (H+1)(head mean)² + tail variance. Second-order mean approximation: Σ_{M≤H} prefix/pronic − mean − (½Σφ(e)w_e)/(H+1) = O(1/((H+1)(H+2))).
- Boost light cone: autocorr f(log(N+k)) = 0 for k < N ⇔ cosh- and sinh-boosted autocorrelations coincide at those nodes; then producer–consumer Lévy duality on [log N, log 2N].
## CoshCollarNodalDefectBezoutShellAndMobiusWaveBridge (start)
- |thetaGroundPotential(x)| ≤ B_ground/cosh(x/2) (retain cosh denominator); Θ(R) ≤ Θ(x) on |x| ≤ R; ⇒ |⟨V_ground u,u⟩| ≤ (B/cosh(R/2))‖u‖² + (B/Θ(R))‖θu‖²; ‖u‖² − ⟨V_ground u,u⟩ and ⟨V_prime u,u⟩ bounded by (1 + B/cosh(R/2))‖u‖² + (B/Θ(R))‖θu‖² (resp. − c_∞‖θu‖²). Lobe five-term jump sums bounded by these collar splits; odd sector: half-mass version with ‖q‖²/2.
- Odd q, 0 < μ ≤ 1: nodalDefectGraph = stationaryGraphLift(collarPotential(q⁻)) − q⁻; ≥ 0 a.e.; ⟨D, q⟩_ground = 0; ⟨D, |q|⟩_ground = 2(−stat cross + primeShift cross); Cauchy–Schwarz. Odd p: balancedNodalGraph = ½‖p‖²·|p| (also after primGraph).
- Conditional RH criteria (premise open): positive-lobe four-term jump ≥ ‖q⁺‖² − ⟨V_ground q⁺,q⁺⟩ (or ≥ the collar split bound at some R) for all odd eigenvectors with μ ∈ (½,1) ⇒ RH. Single eigenvector: that inequality ⇒ μ ≤ ½.
- Bézout shells of depth r factor through σ_N; iso-state windows annihilate (2r−1)-step shell sums. Route PM: damped centered prefix = Δ(active) + (second layer(n−1) − second layer(n+2)) + four-step interior. Common-return window zero ⇔ vanishing on Icc 2 (min(max(b+1,a),N)) + harmonic & raw tail moments vanish; centered radius n+1 at level n+3 ⇔ sieveVariance = 0 (sharp 2N−3 detection).
- Klein–Gordon Chebyshev transport = CHMD prime kernel − (Möbius wave + (γ+1)·tail pairing + remainder); |remainderFold| ≤ 7L e^{−v/2}; |lightConeBessel| ≤ 1; Möbius fold tail ≤ C·envelope(y)·N^{−(π−3/2)}; RH ⇔ the three Möbius pieces bounded above on y ≥ 0 ⇔ bounded below (RHTower equivalences, re-exported).
## WindowedQuarterZeroDensityAndCHMDBoundStateCount
- windowedQuarterZeros(σ,T) = quarter-strip zeros with Re ≥ σ, T ≤ Im ≤ 2T: injects into zeroHeightBox ⇒ unconditionally finite (no offLineZeros.Finite needed); Ingham–Huxley count D(1+log T)^B T^{(A(σ)+ε)(1−σ)}; empty for σ > 1 − routeT width (zero-free region) and T ≥ T₁. VK: |Σ_{N<d≤Q}Λ(d) − (Q−N)| ≤ B·Q e^{−κ·vkScale(Q)/24} + same at N, eventually.
## OddPolarAbsorptionBoundaryAndCompensatedHorizon (start)
- 2 sinh(a/2) ≤ a e^{a/2}. Tail growth: x − a ≥ ½log 2 ⇒ Ψ_a(x) ≥ π e^{2x − 5a/2} (odd polar function grows exponentially in the tail).
- Cosh tail price: 0 < a ≤ ½log 2, x ≥ log 2: πe^{2x − (5/4)log 2}sinh(x/2) ≤ Ψ_a(x)cosh(x/2). Price P(L) = πe^{2L − (5/4)log 2} > 1 for L ≥ log 2 ⇒ cosh/sinh ratio κ ≥ P(R) > 1: unit absorption by a derivative majorant is INFEASIBLE at every radius ≥ log 2 (refutes the θ ≤ 1 premise route).
- Counterexample: for every θ there is a smooth compact even w with θ·sinhPotential < cosh mass (localize near L where defect density > 0). So no fixed coefficient absorbs cosh mass.
- Positive survivors: (i) confinement P(L)·sinhTail_L ≤ cosh mass (bounded cosh mass forces exponential confinement of sinh potential); (ii) exact compensated identity markovEnergy + θ·sinhPot + Defect = archJump + 2 sameSide + Σ chainIntegral + Reserve (Defect/Reserve = positive/negative parts of density ρ_θ(x) = ΘΨ_a²cosh(x/2) − θ m_a sinh(x/2)) ⇒ markovEnergy + θ sinhPot + Defect ≥ 0; (iii) a continuous even h corrects cosh mass on all even compact w ⇔ h ≥ ρ_θ pointwise; max(ρ_θ,0) is the minimal nonnegative correction (no integrated improvement).
- Localization w ↦ χw (even smooth χ) keeps odd compact zero-mean source class.
## CanonicalOddPrimitiveHorizonBounds (start)
- |sourcePair(p,q)| ≤ ‖p‖‖q‖ (graph norm); |E(p) − E(q)| ≤ ‖p−q‖(2‖q‖ + ‖p−q‖). Cutoff edge budget ∝ (Lip·scale_n)²·∫e² → 0; translated cutoff mass error → 0. Canonical odd cutoff (translate cutoff by ±a, divide by 2 sinh(a/2)) converges to Ψ_a in graph norm with budget → 0; |sourceEnergy(cutoff) − ½| ≤ √budget(2‖Ψ_a‖ + √budget).
- Canonical odd cutoff is C², compact, odd, mean zero; equals m_a on |x| + |a| ≤ n+1, zero beyond 2(n+1) + |a|; cosh-moment 0, sinh-moment = cosh-moment of cutSource n. markovEnergy(cutoff) → ½ (eventually > ¼).
- Primitive cutoff Ψ_a^{(n)} − Ψ_b^{(n)}: both polar moments cancel exactly; limit oddCritical(a,b) ∈ signedRadical (sourceEnergy 0) ⇒ |markovEnergy(primitive cutoff)| ≤ 2(budget_a + budget_b) (radical subtraction removes square-root error); budget ≤ rate·scale_n² via translated source moment ∫((x−a)·ratio)² (finite: theta tail super-exponential) ⇒ |markovEnergy| ≤ 2(rate_a + rate_b)/(n+1)² (unconditional inverse-square horizon bound). Explicit shift sequence: tests eventually nonzero (oddCriticalSequence linearly independent). Full chain identity retained (arch jump + sameSide ± + Σ chainIntegral − cosh mass). No finite-horizon sign and no density in the primitive sector.
## FourthRootPrimeWindowSieveAndGallagherLinearTransfer
- Fourth-root Brun–Titchmarsh (z = q^{1/4}): near-diagonal weight (700q/log q + 1)|cΛ(q)|² = (700 q log q + (log q)²)|c|² (one log cancels); prime-supported mean value ∫_{−W}^{W}|Σ c_qΛ(q)q^{−iw}|² ≤ (5/4)Σ(700 q log q + log²q)|c_q|²; truncated Perron-line mean square of prime-supported Dirichlet series (Selberg fibre error).
- Gallagher transfer for μ: |linear (x, x+L] sum|² ≤ 2|multiplicative window sum|² + 2(y − x − L + 1)²; multiplicative window mean square ≤ M₁δ + M₂·2/(π²W).
- windowedQuarterZeros: uniform Huxley T^{(12/5+ε)(1−σ)} for σ ∈ [½,1]; uniform density hypothesis T^{(2+ε)(1−σ)} for σ ∈ [5/6,1] (polylog factors).
## MatomakiTeravainenPaperScaleSieveAndRankinEulerTail (start)
- Rankin–Euler: e | P(z): d(e)^K e^{1/log z} ≤ (2^K e)^{ω(e)}; Σ_{e|P(z), e > D} d(e)^K/e ≤ D^{−1/log z}exp(2^K e(log log z + C_Mertens)).
- MT sieve coefficients (re-export): Σ_{n≤Y} d₃(n)|c_{1,n}| ≤ BY(1+log Y)^7 D^{−1/log z}exp(8e(log log z + C)); c_{2,n}, c_{3,n} d₃-weighted first moments ≤ BY(log X)^{−3A} at paper width (log X)^{−10A}.
## ShiftedGroundStateCollarDualityAndLamzouriFourTrace (re-exports from ACT/HilbertMetric)
- Odd positive ground state ω, even u: two-sided generator = folded generator + reflected collar 2Θ(x)Θ(t−x)max(ω(t−x),0); full-line Doob jump = 2·same-side + reflected (0,t) jump + 2∫u²ω·foldedGen.
- Shifted ground state ω_a: collar duality R_a + D_a = 4∫_{x>0}u² m_a C_a; markovEnergy(Φ(ω_a u)) = 2S_a − D_a + 4 sinh(a/2)∫u² m_a sinh(x/2); nonneg ⇔ variation inequality D_a ≤ 2S_a + 4 sinh(a/2)∫...
- Any nonneg nonzero combination c₁ω_a + c₂ω_b is a STRICT folded subsolution near the origin and near infinity (combination failure).
- Horizontal Lamzouri four-trace: 4·mass − E − 2N + quadratic mixed gain (#M − S)²/#M ≤ #H; pair routing identity/inequality.
## CofactorSquarefreeFermiSamplingAndPronicObservability
- fermiSelectedCoefficient N c = c on [1,N] ⇔ c vanishes on multiples of 4 in [1,N] (fixed-point subspace); there the 3/2 transport constant is an exact L² isometry and selected reading = sieve value.
- Route PM cofactor real weight Re(Σ_{m≤Q/d}μ(m)w_ε(dm)·...) is squarefree-supported ⇒ four-free ⇒ transport identity at every N, Q; exact pair recovery (odd and 4m+4 boundaries give s(2m+1), s(2m+2)); constant-4 pronic sandwich F ≤ P ≤ 4F with explicit positive slack Σ(3s(2m+1)²/pronic + s(2m+2)²/(pronic(4m+5))); factors through radical state.
## DampedPrimeBoundaryDissipationAndCofinalDefectSign (start)
- τ_ε(p) = 1 − p^{−ε}; 2|n ⇒ τ_ε(n) ≤ 1 − 2^{−ε}; |s_N(c_ε, n+1)| ≤ d(n+1). Odd prime p, p+1 ≤ N: jump (s(p+1) − s(p))² ≥ (2^{−ε} − p^{−ε})² (prime boundary dissipation; vanishes at ε = 0: Möbius path is constant below horizon).
- Damped defect budget (1 ≤ N ≤ M+1): 2D(M) + Σ_{3≤p<N prime}(2^{−ε} − p^{−ε})² ≤ d(M+1)² − 1. Beyond 2^{1+1/ε} each prime pays ≥ (2^{−ε}/2)²; Chebyshev: #primes ≥ N/(4 log N) + K eventually ⇒ dissipation ≥ δN/(4 log N), δ = (2^{−ε}/2)².
- Cofinal coercivity at fixed ε > 0 (no shrinking radius): eventually in N, D(M) ≤ −δN/(16 log N) for every N ≤ M+1 ≤ N^{r+2}+1 (divisor square ≤ D√N). Radical-state transport: eventually, D(M) ≤ 0 whenever R_N(M+1) ≤ N^{r+2} (any M); same for literal Möbius (using first-octave Möbius cofinal bound −N/(2π²)).
## GlobalRenormalizedDefectSignAtLargeHorizons
- Window observability constant S_N = Σ_{q≤N}σ(q)²/φ(q) ≤ N⁵; |H_N(c)| ≤ N for |c| ≤ 1; Bertrand prime p ∈ (N/2, N] has Selberg coordinate c(p)/p ⇒ variance ≥ φ(p)(c(p)/p)².
- Long-window law (phase-uniform window Poincaré, BVI): 2D(M) ≤ 4H² − (M − 11S_N)V ⇒ for |c| ≤ 1, M ≥ 11N⁵: 2D(M) ≤ 4N² − (M − 11N⁵)φ(p)(c(p)/p)². Möbius: mode ≥ 1/(2N) ⇒ 2D ≤ 4N² − (M − 11N⁵)/(2N), D ≤ 0 for M ≥ 11N⁵ + 8N³ (explicit, every N ≥ 2). Damped ε ≤ k: mode ≥ 1/(2N^{2k+1}), D ≤ 0 for M ≥ 11N⁵ + 8N^{2k+3}.
- GLOBAL SIGN: eventually in N, D_N(M; μ) ≤ 0 for EVERY prefix M (regimes: M+1 < 6(N+1) unit envelope; M ≤ N⁶ polynomial cofinal; beyond: long-window). Same for every fixed ε > 0 (threshold depends on ε).
## RoughPrimeBoundaryUniformDampingDefectSign (start)
- 4 | k, 1 ≤ k ≤ 2N ⇒ s_N(c_ε, k) ≤ 1 − 2^{−ε} (in first octave the omitted divisor k is non-squarefree). Prime p ∈ (N, 2N) is N-rough: s(p) = 1. Boundary p−1 or p (whichever neighbour pair crosses a 4-divisible point) carries jump ≥ 4^{−ε}, distinct primes distinct boundaries.
- Rough-prime dissipation: N ≥ 2, M ≥ 2N−1: jump energy ≥ 4^{−ε}·#{primes in (N,2N]}; PNT ⇒ #primes in (N, 2N] ≥ N/(2 log N) eventually.
- UNIFORM GLOBAL SIGN: eventually in N (threshold independent of ε), for every 0 ≤ ε ≤ 1 and every M, D_N(M; c_ε) ≤ 0. Regimes: sub-horizon; M+1 < 5(N+1) unit envelope; 2N−1 ≤ M ≤ N⁶ rough-prime dissipation ≥ N/(8 log N) beats divisor square D√N; M > N⁶ long-window law with k = 1.
## FermiAllBoundaryTraceFourfoldConditioningGain
- All-boundary trace (all cell boundaries observed): K^all_N/(log₂N)² → 1/π² (real and nonneg coefficients); selected trace K_N/(log₂N)² → 4/π² ⇒ K_N/K^all_N → 4.
- Mechanism: selected schedule leaves the first doubling step on each dyadic ray unobserved (ray free at bottom, pinned at top: mixed boundary, ground mode cos((k+½)π/(2L+3)), constant (2L/π)²); n ≡ 2 mod 4 cells observe it ⇒ Dirichlet chain sin((k+1)π/(L+2)), constant (L/π)²: halving effective ray length divides conditioning by 4; loss still quadratic in log₂N.
- Explicit: N ≥ 4, L = ⌊log₂N⌋: K^all ≥ (1 − 4/(L+2))(L+2)²/π²; for m ≤ L+1, r = L+m+6: K^all ≤ (r²/(π²(1 − 5π²/(48r²))) + 1)(1 + 2^{−(m+1)}).
## FermiAllBoundaryTraceRisingSinePackets (start)
- Rising sine profile y_k = sin((k+1)θ), θ = π/(L+2), on L+1 dyadic levels via c(1) = y₀, c(2^{k+1}) = 2^{k+1}y_{k+1} − 2^k y_k (nonnegative since 2 sin((k+1)θ) ≥ sin(kθ)); Dirichlet ground mode: jump energy = λS, λ = 2 − 2cos θ, S = (L+2)/2; saturation tail costs sin²θ/3; sieve energy ≥ (S−2)/b² per ray ⇒ K^all ≥ (1 − 4/(L+2))/λ.
- Profile mass Σ sin²((k+1)θ) = (L+2)/2 (telescoping cosine sum); chain v_j = sin(jθ), v_0 = v_{L+2} = 0, v_{j+2} + v_j = 2cos θ v_{j+1} ⇒ Σ(Δv)² = (2 − 2cos θ)Σv². On ray 2^k b (b odd), N ≥ 2^L: response = 2^{min(k,L)}y_{min(k,L)} (telescoping prefix); normalized head = y_k, tail halves.
## FermiAllBoundaryTraceConditioningBound
- Physical sieve and all-boundary energies decompose over odd-base dyadic rays (twice-odd cells n = 4m+2 give each ray's bottom jump). Dirichlet ray inequality ⇒ P ≤ ((1/λ) + 1)/ρ_m·Q_all, λ = 2 − 2cos(π/(log₂N + m + 6)), ρ_m = 2^{m+1}/(2^{m+1}+1), every m ≤ log₂N + 1.
## FermiAllBoundaryTraceDirichletRayBound
- Ray weights w_k(b) = 4^k/((2^k b)(2^k b + 1)): b²w_k ∈ [2^k/(2^k+1), 1], ≥ ½, ≥ ρ_m beyond level m. Saturation tails: values and jumps beyond D = log₂N + 1 sum to y_D²/3. Ray inequality b²·sieveRay ≤ (1/λ + 1)·R, R = ½(y₀² + Σ_{k<m}(Δy)²) + Σ_{m≤k<D}(Δy)² + y_D²/3 ≤ b²·allBoundaryRay/ρ_m.
## DirichletChainDoubledPrefixAndRobinRamp
- Pinned chain: (2 − 2cos(π/(n+1)))Σz² ≤ Σ(Δz)² (via RHTower autocorrelation chain sine certificate). Soft prefix: p half-weighted steps split into half-steps lengthen the chain by p (constant 2 − 2cos(π/(n+p+1))). Robin ramp: halving continuation costs y²/3 = three-step linear ramp to zero. Ray bound: (2 − 2cos(π/(D+m+5)))Σ_{k≤D}y² ≤ ½(y₀² + Σ_{k<m}(Δy)²) + Σ_{m≤k<D}(Δy)² + y_D²/3.
## BuchstabSlopeLawAndPrimeHorizonDissipation (start)
- d | mj ⇔ d/gcd(d,m) | j ⇒ dilation Σ_{j≤B}s_N(c, mj) = Σ_{d≤N}c(d)⌊B gcd(d,m)/d⌋.
- Exact horizon step (any m = n+1): ΔE = μ(m)²B + 2μ(m)Σ_{d<m}μ(d)⌊B gcd(d,m)/d⌋, B = ⌊M/m⌋. Buchstab slope λ(m) = 1 + 2μ(m)Σ_{d<m}μ(d)gcd(d,m)/d; squarefree m: |ΔE − Bλ(m)| ≤ 2(m−1) uniformly in M. Prime: λ(p) = 1 − 2m(p−1), m(x) = Σ_{d≤x}μ(d)/d. Composite can be negative: m = 6 gives ΔE = −B − 2⌊B/5⌋ exactly.
- Prime horizon step = B − 2Σ_{d<p}μ(d)⌊B/d⌋; Σ_{d≤B}μ(d)⌊B/d⌋ = 1 (B ≥ 1). Half-band criterion at a prime = Möbius floor mean bound. Theorem: eventually in p (PNT: m(x) → 0, M(x) = o(x)), for every M with B = ⌊M/p⌋ ≥ 2, E_{p−1}(M) ≤ E_p(M) (prime horizon steps never lower prefix energy). Three ratio regimes: B < p (head = 1), 4p ≤ B (harmonic Möbius mean ≤ ¼), p ≤ B < 4p (⌊B/d⌋ ∈ {1,2,3} ⇒ three Mertens values, |M(x)| ≤ (x+1)/24).
## BuchstabSlopeInheritanceAndSelbergDerivative (start)
- Core A(m) = Σ_{d<m}μ(d)gcd(d,m)/d, λ(m) = 1 + 2μ(m)A(m). Horizon derivative of Selberg form: Q_Sel(m) − Q_Sel(m−1) = μ(m)²(1 + 2μ(m)A(m))/m (= λ(m)/m squarefree): slope = derivative of period mean square.
- Coprime harmonic Möbius m_f(n) = Σ_{d≤n,(d,f)=1}μ(d)/d; prime removal m_f(n) = m_{fp}(n) − m_{fp}(n/p)/p; contracting recursion transmits → 0 ⇒ m_f → 0 for every squarefree f (finite prime set). G_a(n) = Σ μ(d)gcd(d,a)/d = Σ_{f|a}φ(f)μ(f)/f·m_f(⌊n/f⌋) → 0.
- Slope inheritance: squarefree a, prime q > a: A(aq) = G_a(aq−1) + A(a)/q − A(a); λ(aq) = λ(a) − 2μ(a)(G_a(aq−1) + A(a)/q) → λ(a) as q → ∞ (large primes invisible to horizon steps; rough absorption for horizon steps). λ(2) = −1 ⇒ eventually in q, horizon 2q strictly LOWERS Möbius energy for M ≥ 2q(8q+1); λ(q) → 1 (primes inject), λ(3q) → 0.
## DampedPrimeHorizonSlopeReversalAndCrossover
- Damped prime step (floor form): ΔE = r²B − 2rΣ_{d<p}c_ε(d)⌊B/d⌋, r = p^{−ε}. Completing floor sum to horizon gives damped divisor sum Σ_{j≤B}τ_ε(j) ≥ κB (mean 1/ζ(1+ε) > 0); omitted divisors d ≥ p cost ≤ B(1+ε)/ε·p^{−ε}.
- REVERSAL: for each fixed ε > 0, every large prime horizon strictly lowers damped energy at every M ≥ p.
- Damped prime slope λ_ε(p) = p^{−ε} − 2H_ε(p−1), H_ε = Σμ(d)d^{−1−ε}; |ΔE − p^{−ε}Bλ_ε(p)| ≤ 2p^{−ε}(p−1); λ_ε(p) ≥ λ(p) − ε log p(2p−1). Small damping: λ(p) > 0 and ε log p(2p−1) ≤ λ(p)/2 ⇒ injection at large ratios.
- No damping-uniform dissipation threshold: for each P there is ε₀ s.t. all ε ≤ ε₀ have an injecting prime p ≥ P. Crossover diverges as ε → 0.
## DampedPrimeHorizonCrossoverScale (start)
- Abel summation vs m(t): |ρ_ε − H_ε(n)| ≤ 2 sup_{t≥n}|m(t)|(n+1)^{−ε}, ρ_ε = 1/ζ(1+ε) ⇒ sign λ_ε(p) = sign(p^{−ε} − 2ρ_ε) up to o(p^{−ε}) uniformly. Crossover scale Λ(ε) = log(1/(2ρ_ε))/ε; one absolute P₀: log p ≤ (1−δ)Λ ⇒ inject; ≥ (1+δ)Λ ⇒ dissipate. ρ_ε/ε → 1 ⇒ εΛ(ε) = log(1/(2ε)) + o(1): damped observer mimics Möbius up to exp((1+o(1))log(1/(2ε))/ε).
- Abel tail: |m(t)| ≤ η for t ≥ n ⇒ |H_ε(n+1+k) − H_ε(n)| ≤ 2η(n+1)^{−ε} ⇒ |ρ_ε − H_ε(n)| ≤ 2η(n+1)^{−ε}; with η = 1/8: p^{−ε}/2 − 2ρ_ε ≤ λ_ε(p) ≤ (3/2)p^{−ε} − 2ρ_ε. Energy steps follow slope sign at large M (step-crossover theorem).
## MobiusEnvelopeLadderAndPrimeShellResponses
- Truncated divisor form: squarefree r ≥ 2: s_N(μ, r) = −μ(r)Σ_{q|r, q(N+1)≤r}μ(q) (only divisors below the radical ratio r/(N+1)); |s_N(μ,r)| ≤ ⌊r/(N+1)⌋.
- Envelope ladder: truncated Möbius divisor sums over q ≤ 10 have |·| ≤ 2 (positive channels 6, 10 need negatives 2,3,5; 7 opens after 6); over q ≤ 12 have |·| ≤ 3. ⇒ |s_N(μ,k)| ≤ 2 if R_N(k) < 11(N+1); ≤ 3 if R_N(k) < 13(N+1), every horizon. Exact: rung two family N = 105p−1, k = 1155p = 11(N+1): response −3; rung three N = 1155p−1, k = 15015p = 13(N+1): response 4.
- Defect sign up to ratio 11: R_N(M+1) < 11(N+1) ⇒ D_N(M; μ) ≤ 0; positive Möbius defect needs radical ratio ≥ 11.
- Prime shell: P primes, max Q, all pair products > Q, prime ℓ > Q: n = ℓΠP at horizon N = n/Q − 1 (ratio exactly Q) has response −(−1)^{|P|}(|P| − 1) (divisors below ratio: 1 and P). Shell of Q = primes p ≤ Q with p² > Q ⇒ |response| = |shell| − 1 ≈ π(Q) − π(√Q) − 1 ≳ Q/(4 log Q): envelope grows almost linearly in the ratio.
## FermiDyadicScheduleInjectivityClassification
- Dyadic schedule S reads Fermi jumps J(n) at v₂(n) ∈ S. Along ray n = 2^k b (b odd), z_k = s_N(c, 2^k b): level 0 reads −z_0, level k+1 reads 2z_k − z_{k+1}; saturation z_k = z_L for k ≥ L = ⌊log₂N⌋ (all levels > L read z_L ⇒ one "saturated slot" L+1). L+2 slots ⇒ relations z_0 = 0, z_k = 2z_{k−1}, z_L = 0: chain pinned at both ends.
- Classification (N ≥ 1): S injective on horizon-N rows ⇔ S misses at most one slot. Two missing slots a < b: profile z_k = 2^k on [a,b) realized by a powers-of-two row c(2^j) = discrete difference — invisible. Consequences: full schedule and "odd + 4-divisible" (missing level 1) injective; odd boundaries alone injective iff N = 1; injective schedule needs ≥ ⌊log₂N⌋+1 slots (observation depth = dyadic depth of horizon).
## SuccessorReserveHorizonLawAndPrimeDissipation (start)
- Successor reserve R_N(c) = Q_Sel − Q_Bez = Σ_{d,e≤N, gcd>1} c(d)c(e)gcd(d,e)/(de) (since 1/lcm = gcd/(de), coprime pairs of Selberg kernel = Bézout kernel): dissipation rate = non-coprime part of lcm kernel.
- Horizon law: R_m − R_{m−1} = (c(m)² + 2c(m)Σ_{d<m, gcd(d,m)>1}c(d)gcd(d,m)/d)/m. Prime p: R_p − R_{p−1} = c(p)²/p ≥ 0 for EVERY weight (Möbius cancellation drops out of the difference). Prime-supported c: R_N = Σ_{p≤N}c(p)²/p exactly.
- Möbius: R_m − R_{m−1} = μ(m)²ϑ(m)/m, reserve slope ϑ(m) = 1 + 2μ(m)Σ_{d<m,(d,m)>1}μ(d)gcd(d,m)/d = λ(m) − 2μ(m)m_m(m−1); ϑ(p) = 1; m_{aq}(aq−1) = m_a(aq−1) + m_a(a−1)/q; ϑ(aq) → λ(a) as prime q → ∞.
## FermiGapScheduleMixedChains
- Mixed (Dirichlet–Neumann) chain: u₀ = 0, free top at M: (2 − 2cos(π/(2M+1)))Σ_{i≤M}u_i² ≤ Σ_{i<M}(Δu)² — proof: reflect across the free end ⇒ Dirichlet chain of length 2M (mass, energy doubled, no jump at fold). Soft prefix (first p ≤ n steps half-weighted) via half-step splitting: constant λ at length n+p.
## FermiGapScheduleTwoChainRayBound
- One-gap trace at level g ≥ 1: all boundaries except dyadic level g (2^g·odd); g = 1 = selected trace. Along a ray the gap removes one step ⇒ ray splits into bottom mixed chain (levels 0..g−1, pinned by base reading, free at gap) and top mixed chain (levels g..L+1, saturated tail = three-step ramp y_D/3, pinned above, free at gap).
- Mixed constant λ_n = 2 − 2cos(π/(2n+1)) > 0; 1/λ_n ≤ ((2n+1)² + 2)/π² (n ≥ 2).
- Two-chain ray inequality: ray sieve energy ≤ [ρ_m·min(λ_{h+m+2}, λ_{L−h+3})]^{−1} × one-gap ray energy (gap at level h+1, m ≤ h ≤ L; ρ_m = weight floor, first m steps half-weighted).
## FermiGapScheduleMiddleGapLimit
- Global one-gap bound: P_N(c) ≤ K_ray·Q_gap(c), K_ray = 1/(ρ_m min(λ_{h+m+2}, λ_{L−h+3})); explicit K_{h+1}(N) ≤ (1 + 2^{−(m+1)})(max(2h+2m+5, 2(L−h)+7)² + 2)/π².
- Monotonicity: K^all_N ≤ K_{h+1}(N) (removing a level only worsens conditioning). Edge gap = selected trace: K_1(N) = K_N (sharp (4/π²)L²).
- MIDDLE GAP FREE: K_{⌊L/2⌋+1}(N)/(log₂N)² → 1/π² (= full-trace value). Edge/middle ratio → 4. Up to lower order, gap at relative depth α costs ≤ 4max(α,1−α)²L²/π².
## FermiGapScheduleQuarterWavePackets (start)
- Profile packet: c(1) = y₀, c(2^{k+1}) = 2^{k+1}y_{k+1} − 2^k y_k synthesizes profile y₀..y_L on EVERY odd ray (response 2^{min(k,L)}y_{min(k,L)}; halves above L). |y_k| ≤ 1 ⇒ ray sieve energy ≥ (S−2)/b² (S = Σy_k²); one-gap ray energy ≤ E/b², E = y₀² + Σ_{k<L,k≠h}(Δy)² + y_L²/3 ⇒ K_{h+1}(N) ≥ (S−2)/E.
- Quarter wave v_j = sin(jθ), θ = π/(2n+1): Σ_{j=1}^n sin²(jθ) = (2n+1)/4; free-end reflection v_{n+1} = v_n; recurrence v_{j+1}+v_{j−1} = 2cosθ v_j ⇒ jump energy = (2−2cosθ)·mass exactly (ground mode of mixed chain).
- Lower bounds: K_{h+1}(N) ≥ ((2h+3)² − 8(2h+3))/π² (bottom wave) and ≥ ((2(L−h)+1)² − 8(2(L−h)+1))/π² (top wave): matches two-chain upper bound to lower order.
## FermiGapSchedulePositionLaw / FermiGapSchedulePositionLimit
- Bottom wave y_k = sin((k+1)θ), θ = π/(2h+3) on k ≤ h; top wave y_k = sin((L+1−k)θ'), θ' = π/(2(L−h)+1) on h < k ≤ L; both give positive gap trace and K_{h+1}(N) ≥ (x² − 8x)/π² with x = 2h+3, resp. 2(L−h)+1 (uses 2 − 2cosθ ≤ θ²).
- POSITION LAW: gap level h(N)+1 with h → ∞, h/⌊log₂N⌋ → α ∈ [0,1] ⇒ K_{h+1}(N)/(log₂N)² → 4max(α,1−α)²/π². Price of one unobserved level = square of the longer half-observed chain: 1/π² at half depth (free) to 4/π² at either end (selected trace = edge). Quarter depth: 9/(4π²).
## PMCofactorRowRamanujanTruncation (start)
- Route PM cofactor row c_Q(d) = Re(dΣ_{m≤Q/d}μ(m)w(dm)) (w = Ramanujan weights of c_ε = μ d^{−ε}) is supported on [1,Q], |c_Q| ≤ 1; horizon-Q response = complete divisor sum at EVERY n: s_Q(n) = Re Σ_{q≤Q}w(q)c_q(n) (truncated Ramanujan expansion of τ_ε(n) = Σ_q w(q)c_q(n)).
- |c_q(n)| ≤ Σ_{d|(n,q)} d; d·Σ_{q>Q, d|q}q^{−1−ε} ≤ (1+ε)/ε·Q^{−ε} ⇒ |s_Q(n) − τ_ε(n)| ≤ (1+ε)/ε·τ(n)·Q^{−ε}; τ(n) = n^{o(1)} ⇒ row response = τ_ε uniformly on n ≤ Q^r as Q → ∞ (far beyond horizon the receiver row observes the complete damped divisor function).
- Uniform agreement: ∀r, η > 0, eventually in Q: |s_Q(n) − τ_ε(n)| ≤ η for 1 ≤ n ≤ Q^r + 1 (τ(n) ≤ C n^a, a = ε/(2(r+1))).
## PMCofactorRowGlobalDefectSign
- τ_ε(n) = Π_{p|n}(1 − p^{−ε}): global unit envelope 0 ≤ τ_ε ≤ 1 = τ_ε(1), first jump τ_ε(1) − τ_ε(2) = 2^{−ε}. On M ≤ Q^r, truncation error η = 4^{−ε}/64 ⇒ Dirichlet polarization 2D̃_Q(M) ≤ −(2^{−ε} − 2η)² + (1+η)² − (1−η)² < 0. No rough primes / envelope ladder / ratio-five boundary needed: the row sees the complete damped divisor function which never exceeds its initial value.
- Beyond Q^{2k+6} (k = ⌈ε⌉): long-window terminal-mode law with prime p ∈ (Q/2, Q]: c_Q(p)/p = −ρ_ε/(p^{1+ε} − 1), mode φ(p)(c(p)/p)² ≥ ρ_ε²/(4Q^{2k+1}).
- THEOREM: ∀ε > 0, eventually in cutoff Q, D_Q(M; c_Q) ≤ 0 for EVERY prefix M (actual PM receiver row has global nonpositive defect).
## MoebiusBezoutDecorrelation
- Unit-shift correlation over a full period = L·Q_Bez(N,c). Möbius Selberg coordinates Y_N(f) = μ(f)/f·m_f(N/f) ⇒ Q_Bez(N;μ) = Σ_{f≤N}μ(f)/f²·m_f(N/f)².
- |m_{Πs}(n)| ≤ 2^{|s|} (each prime at most doubles, recursion m_{fp}(n) = m_f(n) + m_{fp}(n/p)/p) ⇒ |m_f| ≤ τ(f) ≪ f^{1/4} for squarefree f; dominated convergence (bound C²f^{−3/2}) + m_f → 0 (PNT) ⇒ Q_Bez(N;μ) → 0: successive Möbius observations decorrelate completely over a period; period unit-shift correlation ≤ ηL eventually.
- R_N(μ) − Q_Sel(N;μ) → 0: dissipation rate ≈ period mean square. Q_Sel = Σφ(f)/f²·m_f(N/f)² has non-summable weights φ(f)/f² — outside this argument (open: asymptotics of Q_Sel(N;μ)).
## MoebiusBuchstabStepSmoothCoreLaw
- Möbius band step Δ(m,B) = μ(m)²B + 2μ(m)Σ_{j≤B}s_{m−1}(μ, mj) = E_m(M;μ) − E_{m−1}(M;μ), B = ⌊M/m⌋. Past horizon: s_{m−1}(mj) = −Σ_{q|mj, q≤j}μ(mj/q) (only complementary quotients q ≤ j ≤ B).
- SMOOTH-CORE LAW: squarefree m ≥ 2, B ≥ 1: Δ(m,B) depends only on r = gcd(m, B#): = B − 2 if r = 1 (rough horizon), = Δ(r,B) otherwise (rough cofactor invariance Δ(rm',B) = Δ(r,B) when primes of m' > B). Non-squarefree: Δ = 0. ⇒ band step takes ≤ 2^{π(B)} + 1 values (finite class set {0, B−2, Δ(r,B): r | B#}).
- Explicit: Δ(2,B) = −B; Δ(3,B) = 2⌊B/2⌋ − B; Δ(6,B) = −B − 2⌊B/5⌋. Even rough horizons n+1 = 2m' (m' odd squarefree, primes > B) lower energy by exactly B.
## MoebiusResponseWhiteNoiseLaw
- Lag-h period covariance diagonal in Selberg coordinates: Cov_N(h) = Σ_{2≤e≤N}c_e(h)Y_N(e)² (Ramanujan lag weights; |c_e(h)| ≤ σ(h)). Bounded multipliers × Möbius coordinates → 0 (dominated convergence) ⇒ Cov_N(h;μ) → 0 for every h ≥ 1.
- Variance floor: top octave N/2 < e ≤ N has m_e(1) = 1 ⇒ Cov_N(0;μ) ≥ Σφ(e)/e² over odd squarefree e there; Σ_{e≤X}e/φ(e) ≤ 4X ⇒ window Σ1/φ ≤ 8; Cauchy–Schwarz + odd-squarefree density 4/π² ⇒ Cov_N(0;μ) ≥ 1/288 eventually.
- WHITE NOISE LAW: Cov_N(h)/Cov_N(0) → 0 ∀h ≥ 1; period shift-difference energy /(2·variance) → 1; dissipation floor R_N(μ) ≥ 1/576 eventually (defect drops ≥ L/576 per complete period).
## MoebiusBandAverageStepAndEnergyProfile (start)
- Core class value v_B(1) = B − 2, v_B(r) = Δ(r,B). Horizons of core r: density δ_B/r, δ_B = (6/π²)Π_{p≤B}p/(p+1). Band mean step A(B) = δ_B Σ_{r|B#}v_B(r)/r.
- Telescoping from E_M(M;μ) = 1: E_N((B+1)N;μ)/N → −(B+1)Σ_{b≤B}A(b)/(b(b+1)); energy profile P(B) = −(B+1)Σ_{b≤B}A(b)/(b(b+1)) ⇔ A(B) = (B+1)P(B−1) − BP(B) (mean horizon step = second difference of energy profile). A(1) = −6/π², A(2) = −4/π², P(2) = 11/π².
- Proved: band-average law (1/X)Σ_{2≤m≤X}Δ(m,B) → A(B) (class counts via fixed-core squarefree densities); band representation E_N((B+1)N;μ) = 1 − Σ_{b≤B}(S_b(M/b) − S_b(M/(b+1))), horizons in (M/(b+1), M/b] lie in band b; A(1) = −6/π² (first band every squarefree horizon steps −1), A(2) = −(2/3)(6/π²) (odd squarefree 0, even −2), P(2) = (11/6)(6/π²).
## DampedPeriodVarianceAndNonCommutingLimits (start)
- τ_ε(n) − s_N(c_ε, n) = Σ_{d|n, d>N}μ(d)d^{−ε} (trans-horizon divisors). Gcd series G(d,e) = (de)^{−ε}/lcm(d,e) summable: boxes = Selberg forms of d^{−ε} ≤ ζ(1+ε)²ζ(1+2ε). Mean square of τ_ε − s_N over [1,X] ≤ X·(tail of G beyond N) → 0.
- (a+b)² ≤ (1+η)a² + (1+1/η)b² comparison over complete periods (N! multiples) with Cesàro means of τ_ε².
- Q_Sel(N; c_ε) → E[τ_ε²] (adaptive η_N = √T_N + 1/(N+1) squeeze); Cov_N(0; c_ε) → V(ε) = E[τ_ε²] − E[τ_ε]² (period variance → variance of complete damped divisor function); V(ε)/ε → 1/2 ⇒ V(ε) → 0.
- NON-COMMUTING LIMITS: lim_{ε→0}lim_N Cov_N(0;c_ε) = 0 < 1/288 ≤ liminf_N Cov_N(0;μ). For all small fixed ε, eventually in N damped variance < Möbius variance. Möbius variance floor is a horizon phenomenon living in trans-horizon divisors that every fixed damping suppresses.
## PrimePairingCollapseAndPrimeFactorStepLaw
- Prime pairing collapse: p | k ⇒ s_X(μ, k) = Σ_{d|k, p∤d, X/p < d ≤ X}μ(d) (pairs d, pd inside horizon cancel; response lives on top p-adic window of p-free divisors). Even k: sum of μ over odd divisors in top octave (X/2, X].
- Prime-factor step law: p ∤ a, pa squarefree: Δ(pa,B) = −B − 2μ(a)W_p(a,B), W_p(a,B) = Σ_{a<d<pa, p∤d}μ(d)⌊B·gcd(d,a)/d⌋ (a = 1 ⇒ prime step law). Odd prime q: horizon 2q steps exactly −B for all B ≤ q+1 (first odd window divisor q+2); energy: M < 2q(q+2) ⇒ E_{2q}(M) = E_{2q−1}(M) − ⌊M/2q⌋.
## ReceiverRowParsevalSpectrumAndHorizonUniformVariance
- Selberg coordinates Y_N(e; c_ε) = Σ_{e|d≤N}μ(d)d^{−1−ε} → w_ε(e) (real Ramanujan coefficient of τ_ε), dominated by e^{−1−ε}ζ(1+ε). PARSEVAL: E[τ_ε²] = Σ_e φ(e)w_ε(e)², V(ε) = Σ_{e≥2}φ(e)w_ε(e)², w_ε(1) = E[τ_ε].
- PM receiver row c_Q at horizon Q+1 has Selberg coordinates EXACTLY w_ε(e) for e ≤ Q+1 ⇒ period variance = partial Parseval sum Σ_{2≤e≤Q+1}φ(e)w_ε(e)² ↑ V(ε), never exceeds V(ε) (horizon-uniform); dissipation R_{Q+1}(c_Q) = Σ_{2≤e≤Q+1}(φ(e) − μ(e))w_ε(e)² ≤ 2V(ε).
- For small ε the receiver row stays below Möbius floors (variance < 1/288, dissipation < 1/576) at EVERY cutoff Q.
## DampedAutocorrelationAndClockInvisibility (start)
- Clock invisibility: squarefree-supported weight ⇒ Selberg coordinates vanish at non-squarefree modes; c_e(h) at squarefree e depends on h only via gcd(e,h) ⇒ period covariance at lag h depends only on rad(h): visibility clock h/rad(h) invisible.
- w_ε(e) = E[τ_ε]μ(e)/D_ε(e), D_ε(e) = Π_{p|e}(p^{1+ε} − 1) ≥ φ(e).
- w_ε(e)² ≤ E[τ_ε]²μ²(e)/φ(e)²; Σμ²(e)/φ(e)² ≤ 16. Damped autocorrelation A_ε(h) = Σ_{e≥2}c_e(h)w_ε(e)² = lim_N Cov_N(h;c_ε); |A_ε(h)| ≤ 16σ(h)E[τ_ε]²; receiver row Cov_{Q+1}(h;c_Q) = partial sum, same bound at every cutoff.
