- SMALL-DAMPING WHITENESS: E[τ_ε] ~ ε, V(ε) ~ ε/2 ⇒ A_ε(h)/V(ε) → 0 as ε → 0. Correlation structure commutes with damping limit; variance does not.
## DampedPrefixEnergyLinearityAndBandNonCommutation
- On k ≤ (B+1)N at most B divisors exceed N (cofactors ≤ B) ⇒ |τ_ε(k) − s_N(k;c_ε)| ≤ B(N+1)^{−ε}; with 0 ≤ τ_ε ≤ 1: E_N((B+1)N; c_ε)/N → (B+1)E[τ_ε²] — damped trans-horizon profile LINEAR in band index (slope E[τ_ε²] ~ ε/2): Möbius band structure invisible under damping.
- Möbius profile P(B) ≥ 6/π² (B ≥ 1) ⇒ band non-commutation: ∀B ≥ 1, small ε, large N: damped prefix energy < Möbius prefix energy.
## DampedLinearDissipationExactRate (start)
- Q_Bez(N;c_ε) → Σ_e μ(e)w_ε(e)² (bounded multipliers); R_N(c_ε) → R(ε) = E[τ_ε²] − Σμ(e)w_ε(e)² = Σ_{e≥2}(φ(e) − μ(e))w_ε(e)² > 0 (mode e = 2 alone gives 2w_ε(2)²).
- Periodic response: Cesàro mean of successor energy (1/X)Σ(s_N(k+1) − s_N(k))² → 2R_N (complete periods N!).
- Cesàro law for τ_ε (non-periodic): (1/X)Σ(τ_ε(k+1) − τ_ε(k))² → 2R(ε) (transferred from periodic s_N via gcd-tail mean-square approximation + uniform-approximation principle).
- EXACT LINEAR DISSIPATION: ∀ε > 0, B: D_N((B+1)N; c_ε)/N → −(B+1)R(ε) (Dirichlet polarization; boundary terms o(N)): damped defect dissipates linearly in window at the Haar dissipation rate of τ_ε.
## DampedPrimitiveEnergyHalfThreshold
- Zero-mean primitive (centered discrepancy Σ_{j≤n}s_N(j) − nH_N): 12E_prim(N;c) = Σ_{2≤q≤N}J₂(q)Y_N(q)², J₂(q) = q²Π(1 − p^{−2}) ≤ q². Damped mode masses ≲ ζ(1+ε)²q^{−2ε}.
- SHARP THRESHOLD ε = 1/2: ε > 1/2 ⇒ E_prim uniformly bounded, → (1/12)Σ_{q≥2}J₂(q)w_ε(q)²; 0 < ε ≤ 1/2 ⇒ prime modes J₂(p)w_ε(p)² ≥ (3/4)E[τ_ε]²/p, Σ1/p = ∞ ⇒ E_prim → ∞; Möbius (ε = 0): ⌊N/2⌋ ≤ 3456·E_prim(N;μ) (linear growth, top octave).
- Interpretation (docstring): the PM receiver barrier ε = 1/2 on the period torus — below 1/2 no horizon-uniform discrepancy bound from complete-period averages; receiver estimate must use pronic weighting of small arguments.
## HalfDampingDissipationLaw (start)
- R(ε) = Σ_{e≥2}(φ(e) − μ(e))w_ε(e)² = V(ε) − A_ε(1); |A_ε(1)| ≤ 16E[τ_ε]² = O(ε²); V(ε) ~ ε/2 ⇒ R(ε)/ε → 1/2, R(ε)/V(ε) → 1; lim_{ε→0}(1/ε)lim_N D_N((B+1)N;c_ε)/N = −(B+1)/2 (each unit of window dissipates half the damping parameter).
## MoebiusEnergyProfileThroughFourthBand
- Bands 3,4: primes ≤ B are {2,3}; four smooth-core classes 1,2,3,6, δ_B = (6/π²)(2/3)(3/4) = 3/π². A(3) = −4/π², A(4) = −2/π² ⇒ P(3) = 16/π² (E_N(4N;μ)/N), P(4) = 41/(2π²) (E_N(5N;μ)/N). Band means P(B) − P(B−1) = 6, 5, 5, 9/2 (units 1/π²); first four bands dissipate on average (A(B) < 0 for B ≤ 4).
## ReceiverRowLinearDissipationRate
- Receiver row c_Q at horizon Q: |s_Q(n) − τ_ε(n)| ≤ ((1+ε)/ε)τ(n)Q^{−ε}; divisor moments Στ ≤ X(1+log X), Στ² ≤ X(1+log X)³ make error o(Q) on window (B+1)Q; boundary bounded since τ(k) ≪ k^{ε/2}.
- THEOREM: D_Q((B+1)Q; c_Q)/Q → −(B+1)R(ε) for the actual PM receiver row (inherits Haar dissipation of τ_ε; half the damping per unit window as ε → 0).
## PronicEnergyCommutesWithDamping (start)
- |c| ≤ 1 ⇒ |s_N(n)| ≤ τ(n); τ(n)²/(n(n+1)) summable ⇒ pronic energy P(N,c) = Σs_N(n)²/(n(n+1)) follows pointwise limit of responses (dominated convergence).
- Möbius: P(N;μ) → 1/2 (only n = 1 survives); damped: P(N;c_ε) → Στ_ε(n)²/(n(n+1)); τ_ε(n) → [n=1] as ε → 0 ⇒ damped limit → 1/2.
- PRONIC OBSERVABLE COMMUTES with damping limit (unlike period variance, dissipation, trans-horizon profile): horizon effect lives in trans-horizon bulk that the pronic weight suppresses.
- lim_{ε→0}lim_N P(N;c_ε) = 1/2 = lim_N P(N;μ) (τ_ε(n) = Π_{p|n}(1 − p^{−ε}) → [n=1]; dominated by 1/pronic).
## DampedDiscrepancyEnergyGrowthLaw
- Top octave N < 2q ≤ 2N: Y_N(q) = c(q)/q (single term).
- GROWTH LAW 0 < ε < 1/2: N^{1−2ε}/10368 ≤ E_prim(N;c_ε) ≤ ζ(1+ε)²N^{1−2ε}/(12(1−2ε)) (lower: odd squarefree top octave, mode J₂(q)q^{−2−2ε} ≥ (φ(q)/q)N^{−2ε}; upper: Σq^{−2ε} ≤ N^{1−2ε}/(1−2ε)). Discrepancy grows like a walk of N^{1−2ε} unit steps; interpolates Möbius linear growth ↔ bounded above 1/2. Lower bound ⌊N/2⌋N^{−2ε} ≤ 3456E_prim valid for all ε ≥ 0.
- |m_{Πs}(n)| ≤ Π_{p∈s}p/(p−1) ⇒ |m_f| ≤ f/φ(f) (squarefree) ⇒ Möbius cap 12E_prim(N;μ) ≤ 64N(1 + log N) (Möbius discrepancy energy between N and N log N).
## MoebiusSpectralEscapeAndDampedTightness
- Möbius spectrum ESCAPES: every fixed modulus mass φ(e)Y_N(e)² → 0 while total ≥ 1/288 ⇒ ∀E eventually moduli > E carry ≥ 1/300 (variance floor carried by moduli comparable to horizon).
- Damped spectrum TIGHT uniformly in N: moduli > E carry ≤ ζ(1+ε)²((1+2ε)/(2ε))E^{−2ε} (fixed limit spectrum = Parseval spectrum of τ_ε). Opposite spectral geometry.
## DampedVarianceAndDissipationSecondOrderLaw (start)
- ζ(1+cε) − 1/(cε) → γ. E[τ_ε²] = H(ε)ζ(1+2ε)/ζ(1+ε)², Euler correction 1 − H(ε) = O(ε²) (prime deficit ≤ 64ε²p^{−3/2}); Euler constant cancels: ρ_ε²/ρ_{2ε} = ε/2 + o(ε²) ⇒ E[τ_ε²] = ε/2 + o(ε²).
- Parity kills lag-one Bézout mean: removing prime 2 factors out 1 − (2^{1+ε}−1)^{−2} = O(ε) ⇒ Σμ(e)w_ε(e)² = O(ε³).
- SECOND ORDER: V(ε) = ε/2 − ε² + o(ε²), R(ε) = ε/2 + o(ε²), A_ε(1) = −ε² + o(ε²): dissipation = full second moment (not variance), exceeds variance by squared mean; consecutive τ_ε anti-correlated at scale of squared mean (one of two consecutive integers is even; factor 1 − 2^{−ε}).
- Parity factorisation: Σ_eμ(e)w_ε(e)² = (1 − (2^{1+ε}−1)^{−2})Σ_{e odd}μ(e)w_ε(e)² (w_ε(2m) = −w_ε(m)/(2^{1+ε}−1) at odd m); parity factor ∈ [0, 8ε] ⇒ |Bézout limit| ≤ 128εE[τ_ε]² = O(ε³). R(ε) − V(ε) ~ ε² ((R−V)/ε² → 1).
## DampedCrossoverEulerConstantAndAdditiveWindow
- ρ_ε = ε − γε² + o(ε²); Λ(ε) = log(1/(2ε))/ε + γ + o(1); crossover prime e^{Λ(ε)}(2ε)^{1/ε} → e^γ.
- |m(t)| ≤ C(log t)^{−3} (Deninger harmonic partial log rate) ⇒ at crossover Abel error O(ε³p^{−ε}) ≪ gap ⇒ ADDITIVE window: ∃ absolute P₀, all small ε, primes p ≥ P₀: slope > 0 for log p ≤ Λ(ε) − ε, < 0 for log p ≥ Λ(ε) + ε. Explicit: inject for p ≤ e^{γ−δ}(2ε)^{−1/ε}, dissipate for p ≥ e^{γ+δ}(2ε)^{−1/ε} (energy steps at all large prefixes).
## QuadraticLongWindowThreshold (start)
- Long-window law 2D_N(M) ≤ 4H² − (M − 10N²)V (window observability constant exact order N²). Möbius: |m(N)| ≤ 1 (floor identity), Bertrand terminal prime mode V ≥ 1/(2N) ⇒ every N ≥ 2: 2D_N(M;μ) ≤ 4 − (M − 10N²)/(2N); D_N(M;μ) ≤ 0 for M ≥ 10N² + 8N (QUADRATIC threshold, not quintic).
- Large N: white-noise floor V ≥ 1/288 ⇒ horizon-uniform slope 2D_N(M;μ) ≤ 4 − (M − 10N²)/288; with polynomial window [2N, N³]: D_N(M;μ) < 0 STRICTLY at every M ≥ 2N. Damped: |H_ε(N)| ≤ 3, threshold 10N² + 72N^{1+2ε}.
- Damped quadratic law: 2D_N(M;c_ε) ≤ 36 − (M − 10N²)N^{−2ε}/(2N) (|H_ε(N)| ≤ 3, ρ_ε ≤ 1).
## SelbergRowPeriodCorrelationsSingularSeries
- Selberg/Barban–Vehov row ℓ_N(d) = μ(d)log(N/d), d ≤ N: response = Selberg truncated von Mangoldt (ε-tangent of Möbius identity Σ_{d|k}μ(d)log(1/d) = Λ(k)). Coordinates Y_N(e) = μ(e)T_e(N/e)/e, |T_e| ≤ A e/φ(e), T_e(Y) → e/φ(e) ⇒ Y_N(e) → μ(e)/φ(e).
- SINGULAR SERIES LAW: ∀h ≥ 1: Cov_N(h;ℓ_N) → 𝔖(h) − 1 (Hardy–Littlewood 𝔖(h) = Σμ(q)²c_q(h)/φ(q)²); H_N(ℓ_N) → 1; uncentred H² + Cov → 𝔖(h); odd h: Cov → −1; variance Cov_N(0;ℓ_N) → ∞ (modes → μ²(e)/φ(e), Σ diverges like log); normalised correlations → 0.
- Selberg row sees prime-pair correlations on period torus (literal Möbius row: all lag covariances vanish). TWO TANGENTS AGREE: horizon limit of Selberg row = second-order Haar limit A_ε(h)/ε² → 𝔖(h) − 1 of damped row.
## ParityTwinOfTheDampedMoebiusWeight (start)
- w_ε(e) = ρ_ε μ(e)/D_ε(e); every translation statistic sees only w_ε(e)² — sign μ(e) = parity of ω(e) invisible.
- Parity twin: weight with Ramanujan coefficients μ(e)w_ε(e) = |w_ε(e)|: c'_ε(d) = (ρ_ε/U_ε)·d·μ²(d)Π_{p|d}1/(p^{1+ε} − 2), U_ε = Σμ²(k)Πu_ε(p), u_ε(p) = 1/(p^{1+ε} − 2) > 0. Coprime mass recursion U_e = (1 + u_ε(p))U_{pe}; u/(1+u) = 1/(p^{1+ε} − 1); product formula U_1 = U_eΠ_{p|e}(1 + u_ε(p)); shape ≤ C d^{−1−ε/2} (summable). Twin dominates Liouville row: c'_ε(d) ≥ (ρ_ε/U_ε)μ²(d)d^{−ε}.
- Proved: W_{c'_ε}(e) = Σ_k c'_ε(ek)/(ek) = μ(e)w_ε(e) (parity involution on moduli); |W_{c'}| = |W_c| all e; twin mean Σc'_ε(d)/d = ρ_ε.
## ParityPairAtHalfDamping
- Receiver energy E_T(c) = Σ_{n≤T}A_c(n)²/(n(n+1)), A_c(n) = Σ_{j≤n}Σ_{d|j}c(d) − mn = Σ_{d≤n}c(d)⌊n/d⌋ − mn. Nonnegative weight: drift A_c(n) ≤ −nΣ_{n<d≤2n}c(d)/d (moduli d > n not yet producing divisibility events leave uncancelled drift).
- ≥ n/8 squarefree in (n, 2n] (n ≥ 100). Twin drift ≥ (K/16)n(2n)^{−ε}; cell bound K²/(4n) when ε ≤ 1/2 ⇒ Σ1/n diverges ⇒ E_T(c'_ε) → ∞ for 0 < ε ≤ 1/2.

Separating observable: prime moduli W_{c'}(p) = −w(p) > 0 > w(p) (mean along dilation k ↦ pk).
## CharacterFourParityPairAtEveryDamping (start)
- Bounded sign pattern s (partial sums ≤ B), c = s d^{−ε}: hyperbola split at D: |A_c(n)| ≤ 2BD + 4Bn(D+1)^{−1−ε}; D = ⌊n^{1/(2+ε)}⌋ ⇒ |A_c(n)| ≤ 6Bn^{1/(2+ε)}; 1/(2+ε) < 1/2 ⇒ receiver energy bounded at every ε > 0 (Abel block bounds 2Bk^{−α}).

Twist χ₄(e) = (−1)^{Ω₃(e)} on odd moduli. Identical amplitudes/translation data, opposite receiver growth at EVERY 0 < ε ≤ 1/2 (bounded vs divergent by odd-moduli drift).

## CharacterFourParityRowsAtEveryHorizon (start)
- Horizon-N character row c_N = χ₄(d)d^{−ε}1_{d≤N}; parity twin c'_N = scaledSelbergRow with sign extension σ(q) = χ₄(q) (odd), 1 (even): Selberg coordinates χ₄(q)Y_N(q).

Trans-horizon receiver separates what all complete-period quadratic data cannot.
## LocalEulerFactorsAndPronicDilationContraction
- Pronic norm ‖f‖_T = (Σ_{n≤T}f(n)²/(n(n+1)))^{1/2} (Minkowski, ‖·‖ ≤ sup|f| since Σ1/(n(n+1)) = 1 − 1/(T+1)).
- DILATION CONTRACTION at critical exponent: Σ_{r<p}1/((pk+r)(pk+r+1)) = 1/(pk(k+1)) ⇒ Σf(⌊n/p⌋)²/(n(n+1)) ≤ p^{−1}Σf(k)²/(k(k+1)), ‖f(⌊·/p⌋)‖ ≤ p^{−1/2}‖f‖.
- Local Euler factor c' = c ⋆ (δ₁ − aδ_p): mean m' = m − am/p; A_{c'}(n) = A_c(n) − aA_c(⌊n/p⌋) + am{n/p}. Forward ‖A_{c'}‖ ≤ (1 + |a|p^{−1/2})‖A_c‖ + |am|; backward (1 − |a|p^{−1/2})‖A_c‖ ≤ ‖A_{c'}‖ + |am|.
- RECEIVER GROWTH CLASS INVARIANT under every local factor with zero LEFT of the critical line (|a| < √p ⇔ Re zero = log_p|a| < 1/2). SHARP: geometric weight g(p^k) = p^{k/2} (mean 1/(1 − p^{−1/2})) has unbounded receiver energy, g ⋆ (δ₁ − √pδ_p) = δ₁ has zero energy.
- Parity flip at one prime (multiplicative along p, |x| < √p) never changes growth class ⇒ a parity pair needs infinitely many flipped primes. Window drift: c ≥ 0, n ≤ X ⇒ A_c(n) ≤ −nΣ_{n<d≤X}c(d)/d.
- Sharpness proof: window (n, pn] holds a power p^{K+1} ⇒ A_g(n) ≤ −n/√(pn) ⇒ cell ≥ 1/(2pn) ⇒ Σ diverges. Parity flip identity c^{(p)} ⋆ (δ₁ + xδ_p) = c ⋆ (δ₁ − xδ_p); χ₄ weight multiplicative along every prime with x = χ₄(p)p^{−ε}, |x| < √p ⇒ flipping χ₄ at any single prime preserves growth class.
## CubicMomentsPinTheParityOfOddRows
- Twisted row c^σ = scaledSelbergRow N c σ (σ² = 1): coordinates σ(q)Y_N(q). Cubic zero-product triple ζ_qζ_qζ_q^{q−2} = 1 (q odd, gcd(q−2,q) = 1) has coefficient Y(q)³(1 − σ(q)³) (BVI sign visibility law) ⇒ σ(q) = 1 if Y(q) ≠ 0.
- Odd-supported row (Y = 0 at even, ≠ 0 at odd moduli) + equal cubic translated moments ⇒ c^σ = c on [1,N]: NO PARITY PAIR SURVIVES CUBIC MOMENTS; separating observable of parity rows = cubic moment at one modulus. χ₄ rows satisfy hypotheses (Y_N(q) ≠ 0 for odd q via L_X > 0).
## EvenFlipIsTheFermiTransformOfTheEvenPart
- Even flip c^♭: σ = −1 at even moduli. For squarefree-supported coordinates, every zero-product tuple has an even number of even-order frequencies (raise to odd M = Π oddpart(ord); squarefree even order ↦ −1) ⇒ c^♭ invisible to EVERY translated moment at every degree.
- Four-free row supported on [1,N]: c^♭(d) = c(d) + c(2d) (d odd), −c(d) (d even) ⇒ c^♭ = c + c_e ⋆ (δ₁ − 2δ₂), even part c_e(d) = 1_odd(d)c(2d): local factor 1 − 2·2^{−s} of the Fermi cells 2⌊n/2d⌋ − ⌊n/d⌋ (zero RIGHT of critical line). Same mean. |‖A_{c^♭}‖ − ‖A_c‖| ≤ (1 + √2)‖A_{c_e}‖ + 2|m_e|.
- The only sign twist of a squarefree row invisible to all additive observables costs at most the receiver of its even part read through the Fermi trace; uniformly paid even parts ⇒ growth class preserved.
## CubicMomentsPinSquarefreeRowsUpToTheEvenFlip (start)
- Coordinates nonzero exactly at squarefree moduli (e.g. every PM cofactor row Y_{Q+1}(e) = ρ_εμ(e)/D_ε(e)): odd q: σ(q) = 1; even 2q': triple orders (2, q', 2q') via η^{q'}η²η^{q'−2} = 1 forces σ(2)σ(q')σ(2q') = 1 ⇒ σ(2q') = σ(2) ⇒ c^σ ∈ {c, c^♭}. Cubic additive data determine receiver of squarefree row up to Fermi receiver of its even part; no parity pair of PM rows survives cubic moments except through prime 2.
- THEOREM pmCofactor_twist_eq_or_evenFlip: at every Q, a sign twist of the PM cofactor row with the row's cubic translated moments equals the row or its even flip on [1, Q+1] (PM coordinates nonzero exactly at squarefree moduli).
## RealCharacterParityPairsAndTheParityBarrier
- Nontrivial real character χ mod q: Σ_{a mod q}χ(a) = 0 ⇒ |partial sums| ≤ q ⇒ c_χ = χ(d)d^{−ε} receiver bounded at every ε > 0. Positive twin c_+ = (|L_χ|/O_χ)1_{(d,q)=1}d^{−ε}; |W_{c_+}(e)| = |W_χ(e)|; L_χ = L(1+ε, χ) ≠ 0 (Dirichlet L nonvanishing Re s > 1, Mathlib); residues 1+qj ∈ (n,2n] carry drift ⇒ diverges for ε ≤ 1/2. EVERY real non-principal character gives a parity pair at every 0 < ε ≤ 1/2.
- PARITY BARRIER: for 0 < ε ≤ 1/2 and every K > 0, receiver boundedness is NOT a function of Ramanujan amplitudes on class 𝒞(K,ε) = {|c(d)| ≤ Kd^{−ε}} (rescaled χ₄ pair). A receiver estimate below half damping must read the signs of the coefficients.
## HalfDampingIsTheExactThresholdOfTheParityPair (start)
- Exact split: A_c(n) = Σ_{d≤n}c(d)(⌊n/d⌋ − n/d) − nΣ_{d>n}c(d)/d. On 𝒞(K,ε): |A_c(n)| ≤ KC_εn^{1−ε}, C_ε = 1 + 1/(1−ε) + 1/ε; pronic cell ≤ (KC_ε)²n^{−2ε}.
- ε > 1/2: receiver energy uniformly bounded on all of 𝒞(K,ε) (no parity pair). ε ≤ 1/2: growth ≤ Σ_{n≤T}n^{−2ε} (T^{1−2ε}, log T at 1/2), attained by positive twins of real characters (extremal). ⇒ receiver boundedness on 𝒞(K,ε) is a function of amplitudes IFF ε > 1/2.
- Twin drift ⇒ cell ≥ (K²/4)n^{−2ε}. char_parityPair_extremal: χ bounded; positive twin pinched between aΣ_{8q≤n≤T}n^{−2ε} and bΣ_{n≤T}n^{−2ε}. THEOREM receiverBounded_amplitudeInvariant_iff: (∃Φ, receiver bounded ⇔ Φ(|W_c|) on 𝒞(K,ε)) ⇔ ε > 1/2.
## UnsignedMajorantsAndSignBlindDataStopAtHalfDamping
- Three thresholds coincide at ε = 1/2: (1) one constant bounds the receiver on all of 𝒞(K,ε) ⇔ ε > 1/2 (absolute majorant); (2) unsigned majorant exists ⇔ boundedness is function of amplitudes |W_c(e)|; (3) boundedness is function of coefficient MAGNITUDES |c(d)| ⇔ ε > 1/2 (|χ₄(d)| = 1_odd(d): Kχ₄(d)d^{−ε} vs K1_odd(d)d^{−ε} same magnitudes, opposite receivers).
- Absolute-convergence boundary = parity barrier = blindness of magnitude-only (ℓ²) inputs, same line ε = 1/2 in the receiver's coordinates.
## DyadicMassesGovernPositiveReceivers (start)
- Nonnegative weight: both pieces of A_c(n) nonpositive (no cancellation); dyadic masses M_k = Σ_{2^k<d≤2^{k+1}}c(d).
- Lower law: block (2^{k+1}, 2^{k+2}] drifts every n ≤ 2^{k+1}: M_{k+1}²/(32·2^k) ≤ receiver block; Σ_{k<K}M_{k+1}²/2^{k+1} ≤ 16E_{2^K}(c). Bounded receiver ⇒ ΣM_k²/2^k < ∞.
- Hardy inequalities (geometric weights): forward Σ_{k<K}(Σ_{j≤k}M_j)²/2^k ≤ 12Σ_{j<K}M_j²/2^j; backward Σ_k 2^k(Σ_{k≤j<J}M_j2^{−j})² ≤ 12ΣM_j²/2^j. Finite support: −(C(n) + n·tail(n)) ≤ A_c(n) ≤ 0.
- Upper law: E_T(c) ≤ 12c(1)² + 100Σ_kM_k²/2^k (finite support then truncation limit). THEOREM: nonnegative weight has bounded receiver ⇔ Σ_kM_k²/2^k < ∞. c(d) ≍ d^{−ε} on positive density ⇒ M_k ≍ 2^{k(1−ε)} recovers threshold 1/2. Nonnegative weights have nonnegative Ramanujan coefficients ⇒ a parity pair always has a sign-changing member.
## CriticalWienerConvolutionsPreserveTheReceiver (start)
- Convolution law: A_{g⋆c}(n) = Σ_{q≤n}g(q)A_c(⌊n/q⌋) + m_cA_g(n), mean m_{g⋆c} = m_gm_c (absolutely convergent means).
- Sawtooth ψ_q(n) = ⌊n/q⌋ − n/q has pronic norm ≤ √(2/q). Critical Wiener algebra: G = Σ_q|g(q)|q^{−1/2} < ∞ (Dirichlet series absolutely convergent on Re s ≥ 1/2). ‖A_g‖ ≤ (√2+1)G; ‖A_{g⋆c}‖ ≤ G‖A_c‖ + (√2+1)G|m_c|.
- THEOREM: receiver boundedness preserved by convolution with every critical Wiener kernel (local Euler factors are the finite kernels).
- Liouville: λ d^{−ε} = 1_□d^{−ε} ⋆ μd^{−ε}; square kernel in critical Wiener algebra (G = ζ(1+2ε)) ⇒ λ receiver paid by μ receiver at every ε; receiver of μ(d)d^{−1/2} bounded (Mellin budget, uses ζ(1+it) ≠ 0); m_λ = ζ(2+2ε)ρ_ε > 0.
- LIOUVILLE PARITY PAIR: liouvilleTwin = κd^{−ε} (smooth power, κ = m_λ/ζ(1+ε)); |W_twin(e)| = |W_λ(e)| (λ completely multiplicative, |λ| = 1). For 0 < ε ≤ 1/2: IF μd^{−ε} receiver bounded THEN λd^{−ε} bounded and κd^{−ε} diverges. UNCONDITIONAL at ε = 1/2 (liouville_parityPair_at_half_damping). Parity of Ω is the whole difference between Liouville and constant weight.
- (Conditional form liouville_parityPair_of_moebius for ε < 1/2 needs boundedness of μd^{−ε} receiver — not proved below 1/2 here; that is RH-strength territory.)
## FirstPronicMomentIsTheVonMangoldtPairing
- Sawtooth superposition A_c(n) = Σ_dc(d)ψ_d(n), ψ_d(n) = ⌊n/d⌋ − n/d ≤ 0. Summation by parts: Σ_{n≤N}ψ_d(n)/(n(n+1)) = (H_{⌊N/d⌋} − H_N)/d − ψ_d(N)/(N+1); H_{⌊N/d⌋} − H_N → −log d ⇒ Σ_nψ_d(n)/(n(n+1)) = −log d/d.
- FIRST PRONIC MOMENT: Σ_nA_c(n)/(n(n+1)) = −Σ_dc(d)log d/d = −Σ_eΛ(e)W_c(e) (von Mangoldt pairing of Ramanujan coefficients; first-order signed observable on prime powers). Valid on 𝒞(K,ε), ε > 0 (Σ|c|log d/d < ∞).
- Separates every real-character parity pair: M(c_χ) − M(c_+) = Σ_eΛ(e)e^{−1−ε}(|L|χ(e)² − Lχ(e)) > 0 (L < 0: squares p² of primes coprime to q; L > 0: primes with χ(p) = −1, which exist for non-principal χ).
## FirstPronicMomentOrdersPositivePairs
- On 𝒞(K,ε): |W_c(e)| ≤ KZe^{−1−ε} (Z = ζ(1+ε)). Positive member c₊ with amplitudes of c has W_{c₊} = |W_c| ⇒ M(c) − M(c₊) = Σ_eΛ(e)(|W_c(e)| − W_c(e)) ≥ 0, strict ⇔ W_c(e) < 0 at some prime power. First pronic moment ORDERS every amplitude pair with a positive member; an unseparated pair has W_c ≥ 0 on all prime powers (parity lives on composite moduli). Liouville pair strictly ordered at every ε > 0 (W_λ(2) = −2^{−1−ε}m_λ < 0).
## FirstMomentBlindParityPairOfTheNegatedMoebiusWeight
- c_b = 2ρ_εδ₁ − μ(d)d^{−ε}: W(1) = ρ_ε, W(e) = −w_ε(e) (e ≥ 2); A_{c_b} = −A_{μd^{−ε}} ⇒ receiver = Möbius receiver. Agrees with parity twin c'_ε at every prime power (μ(p) = −1; w_ε = 0 on higher prime powers) and in all amplitudes ⇒ SAME first pronic moment. At e = pq: W_{c_b}(pq) < 0 < W_{c'}(pq). For 0 < ε ≤ 1/2: c_b has Möbius receiver, c' divergent; unconditional at ε = 1/2 (c_b bounded).
- Equality case of first-moment ordering: first observable seeing the pair is supported on ω(e) ≥ 2 (weight Λ ⋆ Λ). Twin in 𝒞(K, ε/2).
## SecondVonMangoldtMomentSeparatesTheBlindPair
- Divisor pairing: a ≥ 0, Σ_{e|d}a(e) = g(d) ⇒ Σ_dc(d)g(d)/d = Σ_ea(e)W_c(e). Λ₂ = Λ·log + Λ⋆Λ, Σ_{e|d}Λ₂(e) = log²d, 0 ≤ Λ₂ ≤ log², Λ₂(6) > 0 (supported on ≤ 2 prime factors).
- Logarithmic first moment M(c·log) = −Σ_dc(d)log²d/d = −Σ_eΛ₂(e)W_c(e) (c·log ∈ 𝒞(2K/ε, ε/2)).
- M(c_b·log) − M(c'·log) = Σ_eΛ₂(e)ρ_ε(μ(e) + μ(e)²)/D_ε(e) > 0 (positive at e = 6): logarithmic first moment separates the blind pair through moduli pq alone.
## MoebiusAndLiouvilleReceiversAreEquivalent
- λ = 1_□ ⋆ μ; square-dilated Möbius inversion μ(√·)1_□ ⋆ 1_□ = δ₁ ⇒ μ = μ(√·)1_□ ⋆ λ. Both damped kernels critical Wiener ⇒ ∀ε > 0: receiver of μd^{−ε} bounded ⇔ receiver of λd^{−ε} bounded (Möbius and Liouville parity pairs exist at exactly the same dampings).
- RH CRITERION (Liouville): bounded Liouville receivers at arbitrarily small damping ⇒ RH (via RHTower.riemannHypothesis_of_pmPowerDamped_cofinal_discrete_budget). Premise OPEN (conditional criterion).
## ReceiverOfMultiplicativeWeightsIsDecidedAtThePrimes
- f multiplicative, |f| ≤ 1, f(p) = −1 ∀p: f = g ⋆ μ, g = f ⋆ 1 with g(p) = 0, |g(p^k)| ≤ k−1; Euler factors of Σ|g(q)|q^{−1/2−ε} are 1 + O(p^{−1−2ε}) (local sum ≤ exp(32p^{−1−2ε})) ⇒ g d^{−ε} critical Wiener ⇒ f(d)d^{−ε} paid wherever μ(d)d^{−ε} is; UNCONDITIONAL at ε = 1/2. Higher prime-power values invisible.
- f multiplicative, 0 ≤ f ≤ 1, f(p) = 1 ∀p ⇒ f ≥ μ² (f = 1 on squarefree) ⇒ squarefree drift ⇒ receiver diverges ∀ε ≤ 1/2.
## PretentiousMultiplicativeWeightsAndTheReceiverTrichotomy (start)
- Multiplicative g, g(p) = 0, |g(p^k)| ≤ Bk ⇒ critical Wiener (local sum ≤ exp(32Bp^{−1−2ε})).
- Character model: f(p) = χ(p) (real non-principal χ): g = f ⋆ μχ, g(p) = 0, |g(p^k)| ≤ 2 ⇒ f(d)d^{−ε} bounded receiver at EVERY ε > 0.
- RECEIVER TRICHOTOMY (|f| ≤ 1 multiplicative): pretends χ at primes ⇒ bounded ∀ε > 0; pretends μ ⇒ bounded at ε = 1/2 (paid wherever μ is); pretends 1 with f ≥ 0 ⇒ divergent ∀ε ≤ 1/2. Arithmetic enters only through prime dilations k ↦ pk.
## HorizonLawOfPositiveRows
- Dyadic row c_J = c·1_{d≤2^J} (c ≥ 0): (1/16)Σ_{k<J−1}M_{k+1}²/2^{k+1} ≤ E_{2^{J−1}}(c_J); E_T(c_J) ≤ 12c(1)² + 100Σ_{k<J}M_k²/2^k (uniform in cutoff). Each horizon step N ↦ 2N adds one dyadic block ≍ M_J²/2^J. Smooth power d^{−ε}: row energy ≍ N^{1−2ε} (ε < 1/2), log N (ε = 1/2); real-character rows bounded.
- Horizon limit commutes with receiver for positive weights: rows uniformly bounded ⇔ weight bounded.
## UnimodularCharacterCompletionsAreParityPartnersOfTheSmoothPower
- Smooth twin κd^{−ε}, κ = |L_s|/ζ(1+ε) of a completely multiplicative unimodular sign s: equal amplitudes with s; diverges for ε ≤ 1/2 when L_s ≠ 0.
- Unimodular completion χ̂ (χ̂(p) = χ(p) for p ∤ q, 1 for p | q; for χ₄: (−1)^{Ω₃(n)}): χ̂ = 1_{q-smooth} ⋆ χ; q-smooth kernel = finite Euler product Π_{p|q}(1 − p^{−s})^{−1}, critical Wiener ⇒ χ̂(d)d^{−ε} bounded receiver at every ε > 0. L(1+ε, χ̂) = L(1+ε,χ)Π(1 − p^{−1−ε})^{−1} ≠ 0.
- UNCONDITIONAL parity pair at every 0 < ε ≤ 1/2 with bounded member taking only values ±d^{−ε} and positive member = full-density smooth power: parity of inert prime factors is the whole difference.
## SparsePrimeFlipsPreserveTheReceiver (start)
- UnimodularSign structure (completely multiplicative, |f| = 1). f' = g ⋆ f, g = f' ⋆ μf, g(p^{m+1}) = f'(p)^m(f'(p) − f(p)): vanishes over primes where f, f' agree, |g| ≤ 2 elsewhere; Euler factors 1 off flip set P, ≤ 1 + 8p^{−1/2−ε} on P.
- Σ_{p∈P}p^{−1/2−ε} < ∞ ⇒ kernel critical Wiener ⇒ receivers bounded together (receiver blind to parity changes on prime sets of convergent p^{−1/2−ε} mass).
- Consequence: completely multiplicative unimodular s with Σ_{s(p)=−1}p^{−1/2−ε} < ∞ (0 < ε ≤ 1/2) has DIVERGENT receiver (in class of smooth power). A bounded unimodular sign must flip a prime set of divergent p^{−1/2−ε}-mass (inert primes of real characters: positive density).
## LogarithmicMomentHierarchySeparatesEveryPositivePair
- Higher von Mangoldt Λ_{k+1} = Λ_k·log + Λ_k ⋆ Λ (Λ_1 = Λ): Σ_{e|d}Λ_k(e) = log^k d, 0 ≤ Λ_k ≤ log^k, Λ_{k+1}(e) > 0 iff e ≥ 2 has ≤ k+1 distinct prime factors.
- Log-power weight c·log^k ∈ 𝒞(K(2(k+1)/ε)^k, ε/2); M(c·log^k) = −Σ_eΛ_{k+1}(e)W_c(e) (additive observables reading W_c on moduli with ≤ k+1 prime factors). Each orders a positive-member pair: M(c·log^k) − M(c₊·log^k) = Σ_eΛ_{k+1}(e)(|W_c| − W_c) ≥ 0.
- Möbius inversion over multiples: c(d)/d = Σ_mμ(m)W_c(dm) on 𝒞(K,ε) ⇒ Ramanujan coefficients determine the damped weight. δ₁-shift c − 2m·δ₁ has same receiver.
- THEOREM exists_logMoment_separating: no parity pair with a positive member is invisible to the log moment hierarchy (if all agree, W_c = W_{c₊} on e ≥ 2 ⇒ c ∈ {c₊, c₊ − 2m₊δ₁} ⇒ same receiver). Order of first separating moment = least ω(e) with W_c(e) < 0.
## LogarithmicMomentsAreACompleteReceiverInvariant
- M(c·log^k) = −Σ_dc(d)log^{k+1}d/d = ±(k+1)-st derivative at s = 1 of L_c(s) = Σc(d)d^{−s} (holomorphic Re s > 1−ε). Equal log moments of all orders ⇒ L_c − L_{c'} has vanishing positive-order derivatives at 1 ⇒ constant near 1 (Taylor) ⇒ constant on half-plane (identity theorem) ⇒ c − c' ∈ ℝδ₁ (uniqueness of Dirichlet coefficients, Mathlib LSeries.eq_of_LSeries_eventually_eq). Receiver blind to δ₁.
- THEOREM exists_logMoment_ne: any two damped weights with different receiver behaviour are separated by some M(c·log^k). Log pronic moments are a COMPLETE receiver invariant (whatever the signs).
## RealCharacterParityRowsAtEveryHorizon
- Horizon-N row c_N = χ(d)d^{−ε}1_{d≤N}; twin c'_N = scaledSelbergRow with completed signs χ̂ (χ, or 1 where χ = 0): coordinates agree up to sign, two-point moments agree; cubic moment separates at all large N at an odd n₀ with χ(n₀) = −1 (exists for non-principal χ); character rows uniformly paid (partial sums ≤ q, energy ≤ 36q²Σn^{2θ−2}); twins → signed positive twin (L/O)1_{(d,q)=1}d^{−ε} divisor by divisor (Möbius inversion of unit mass) ⇒ energy → ∞ for ε ≤ 1/2. THEOREM char_parityRows (every real character carries parity rows at every horizon).
## FiniteKernelsRepairAPositiveDriftOnlyAtItsPole (start)
- Mellin cells Δ_n(σ) = n^{−σ} − (n+1)^{−σ} ∈ [σ(n+1)^{−σ−1}, σn^{−σ−1}]; Φ_c(σ) = Σ_nA_c(n)Δ_n(σ) (Mellin transform of step A_c(⌊x⌋)). Cells telescope over [M, M+j).
- Dilation law of cells: Σ_r<jΔ_{tj+r}(σ) = j^{−σ}Δ_t(σ) ⇒ Σ_nf(⌊n/j⌋)Δ_n(σ) = j^{−σ}Σ_nf(n)Δ_n(σ); with convolution law Φ_{k⋆q}(σ) = K(σ)Φ_q(σ) + m_qΦ_k(σ), K(σ) = Σk(j)j^{−σ}.
- Receiver bound (Cauchy–Schwarz vs pronic weights, Δ_n²n(n+1) ≤ 2σ²n^{−2σ}): energy ≤ E ⇒ |Φ(σ)| ≤ √E·√(2σ²(1 + 1/(2σ−1))) for σ > 1/2 (O(δ^{−1/2}) at σ = 1−ε+δ, ε ≤ 1/2).
- Drift ⇒ pole: q ≥ 0 with drift nΣ_{(n,2n]}q(d)/d ≥ κn^{1−ε} ⇒ Φ_q(1−ε+δ) ≤ −(κσ/2)(N₀+1)^{−δ}/δ (order δ^{−1}). Finite kernel prefix |A_k(n)| ≤ Σ|k(j)|.
- THEOREM not_receiverBounded_dirichletConv_of_drift: 0 < ε ≤ 1/2, q ≥ 0 in damped class with drift, k finite: K(1−ε) ≠ 0 ⇒ receiver of k ⋆ q UNBOUNDED. Finite kernels repair a positive drift only if their Dirichlet polynomial vanishes at the pole s = 1−ε (single separating number K(1−ε)).
## EvenFlipOfTheFermiSquarefreeWeightBreaksTheReceiver (start)
- o(d) = 1_{odd sqf}(d)d^{−ε} (pole at s = 1−ε). Fermi weight F = (δ₁ − 2^{1−ε}δ₂) ⋆ o: Fermi factor 1 − 2^{1−ε−s} vanishes exactly at pole. F(d)d^ε = (g ⋆ s₀)(d), g(j²) = μ(j)1_odd(j) (1_{odd sqf} = g ⋆ 1_odd, square-root Möbius normalization), s₀ = 1_odd ⋆ (δ₁ − 2δ₂) = period-4 pattern (0,1,−2,1) by n mod 4 (mean zero, partial sums ∈ {−1,0,1}).
- F ∈ 𝒞(3,ε); F vanishes on multiples of 4 and non-squarefree d. Even flip F^♭ = ((1 − 2^{1−ε})δ₁ + 2^{1−ε}δ₂) ⋆ o = F + c_e ⋆ (δ₁ − 2δ₂): F^♭(d) = F(d) + F(2d) (d odd), −F(d) (d even).
- Even flip twists Ramanujan coefficients by σ₂ (−1 on even e) for any four-free damped weight: W_{c^♭}(e) = σ₂(e)W_c(e) ⇒ equal amplitudes, same mean.
- F = g ⋆ s₀d^{−ε} with g critical Wiener, s₀ partial sums bounded ⇒ F receiver bounded ∀ε > 0. Odd squarefree drift ≥ (D/3)2^{−1−ε}n^{1−ε} (D = 6/π²). F^♭ kernel at pole: 2 − 2^{1−ε} ≠ 0 ⇒ F^♭ unbounded ∀ε ≤ 1/2. THEOREM fermi_evenFlip_parityPair. Fermi factor at 2 is the only local factor whose zero sits on the pole.
## FermiRowsAgreeInEveryTranslatedMomentAndSplitTheReceiver
- Fermi row F_N = F·1_{≤N} squarefree ⇒ even flip invisible to EVERY translated moment of every degree at every horizon; F_N^♭(d) = F^♭(d) once N ≥ 2d. Fixed-cutoff receivers converge ⇒ Fermi rows bounded (eventually, each T), flipped rows exceed any C at large N. THEOREM fermi_parityRows: trans-horizon receiver separates a pair no additive observable of any degree can (strengthens χ₄ case where cubic moments separate).
## FinitePerturbationsOfTheSmoothPowerAreDecidedAtThePole
- k on [1,J], 0 < ε ≤ 1/2: receiver of k ⋆ d^{−ε} bounded ⇔ K(1−ε) = Σk(j)j^{−(1−ε)} = 0. (⇐: s = k̃ ⋆ 1 with k̃(j) = k(j)j^ε has partial sums Σk̃(j)(⌊n/j⌋ − n/j) bounded since mean = K(1−ε) = 0; ⇒: pole law + drift 2^{−1−ε}n^{1−ε}.) Paid finite perturbations of smooth power = hyperplane K(1−ε) = 0.
## EvenFlipPairsOverTheOddSquarefreeWeightAreClassifiedAtThePole (start)
- Commuting convolutions via toAF; o = g ⋆ (1_odd d^{−ε}).
- Pole law over o: receiver of k ⋆ o bounded ⇔ K(1−ε) = 0 (⇐: o = g ⋆ 1_odd d^{−ε}, k̃ ⋆ 1_odd partial sums Σk̃(j)⌈⌊n/j⌋/2⌉ = ½Σk̃(j)⌊n/j⌋ + O(Σ|k̃|) bounded when mean ½K(1−ε) = 0).
- Two-point classification: c = (αδ₁ + βδ₂) ⋆ o, even flip c^♭ = ((α+β)δ₁ − βδ₂) ⋆ o (dilation acts (α,β) ↦ (α+β, −β)); same amplitudes; c paid ⇔ α + 2^{ε−1}β = 0; c^♭ paid ⇔ α + (1 − 2^{ε−1})β = 0; both paid only for α = β = 0 (2^{ε−1} ≠ 1/2). Fermi pair = line α = −2^{ε−1}β.
## CriticalWienerKernelsRepairAPositiveDriftOnlyAtItsPole
- Bounded pronic energy ⇒ absolute Mellin pairing for σ > 1/2. Critical Wiener kernel prefix energy ≤ ((√2+1)W)². Double series Σ_nΣ_jk(j)A_q(⌊n/j⌋)Δ_n(σ) absolutely convergent (|A_q(m)| ≤ Cm^{1−ε}).
- THEOREM not_receiverBounded_dirichletConv_of_drift_critical: pole law extends to the whole critical Wiener algebra: k critical Wiener, q ≥ 0 drift weight, Σk(j)j^{−(1−ε)} ≠ 0 ⇒ k ⋆ q unbounded (K(σ) → K(1−ε) by dominated convergence).
## MultiplicativeWeightsPretendingToOneArePaidOnlyByAVanishingLocalFactor (start)
- f multiplicative, f(p) = 1, |f| ≤ B: f = g ⋆ 1, g = f ⋆ μ, g(p) = 0, |g(p^k)| ≤ 2B ⇒ g d^{−ε} critical Wiener; pole value Σg(n)/n = Π_pλ_p, λ_p = (1 − 1/p)L_p, L_p = Σ_ef(p^e)p^{−e}, Σ|λ_p − 1| < ∞. No L_p vanishes ⇒ unbounded ∀ε ≤ 1/2. |f| ≤ 1 ⇒ L_p ≥ 1 + 1/p − 1/(p(p−1)) ≥ 1 ⇒ EVERY multiplicative f with f(p) = 1, |f| ≤ 1 has unbounded receiver regardless of higher prime-power signs.
- Proof: λ_p = Σg(p^e)p^{−e} = (1 − 1/p)L_p, |λ_p − 1| ≤ 4(2B+1)p^{−2}; Euler product of nonzero factors with Σ|λ_p − 1| < ∞ nonzero (tprod_one_add_ne_zero_of_summable). THEOREMS not_receiverBounded_of_primes_one_of_poleLocalFactor_ne_zero; not_receiverBounded_of_primes_one_of_abs_le_one. Receiver reads higher prime powers only via local factor value at pole (steps k ↦ pk along one prime summed against p^{−k}).
## FinitePerturbationsOfCoprimeWeightsAreDecidedAtThePole
- u_q = 1_{(d,q)=1}d^{−ε} (damped principal character), |N_q(y) − yφ(q)/q| ≤ q (each q-window has φ(q) coprimes). Drift ≥ (2q)^{−1}2^{−1−ε}n^{1−ε}. THEOREM: receiver of k ⋆ u_q bounded ⇔ K(1−ε) = 0 (same hyperplane for every modulus; local factors at p | q never move the pole).
## AVanishingFiniteLocalFactorPaysAWeightPretendingToOne (start)
- Multiplicative split at a prime: f = f₀ ⋆ (1_{(·,p₀)=1}f), f₀ = localPart (f on {1,p₀,…,p₀^K}). f₁ = h ⋆ 1_{(·,p₀)=1}, h = 1_{(·,p₀)=1}(f ⋆ μ) zero at primes ⇒ critical Wiener. f d^{−ε} = h d^{−ε} ⋆ (f₀d^{−ε} ⋆ u_{p₀}); inner pole value = L_{p₀}.
- THEOREM receiverBounded_of_poleLocalFactor_eq_zero: f multiplicative, f(p) = 1, |f| ≤ B, finite local factor at p₀ with L_{p₀} = 0 ⇒ bounded at every ε > 0. THEOREM receiverBounded_iff_exists_poleLocalFactor_eq_zero (all local factors finite, 0 < ε ≤ 1/2): bounded ⇔ ∃p: Σ_kf(p^k)p^{−k} = 0.
## TheEvenFlipShiftsThePoleValueByTheEvenPartOfTheKernel
- k on [1,J], vanishing on multiples of 4: k = k_o + δ₂ ⋆ k_e (k_o = 1_odd·k, k_e(j) = 1_odd(j)k(2j)); even flip of k ⋆ o is k^♭ ⋆ o, k^♭ = k_o + k_e − δ₂ ⋆ k_e. Pole values K = K_o + 2^{ε−1}K_e, K^♭ = K_o + (1 − 2^{ε−1})K_e ⇒ EXACT LOSS K^♭(1−ε) = K(1−ε) + (1 − 2^ε)K_e(1−ε).
- THEOREM evenFlip_exactLoss: same amplitudes; each paid iff its pole value = 0; K_e(1−ε) = 0 ⇒ flip preserves receiver; else never both paid.
## APoleOfOrderTwoNeedsAZeroOfOrderTwo (start)
- Log drift nΣ_{(n,2n]}q(d)/d ≥ κn^{1−ε}log n (double pole, e.g. log(d)d^{−ε}): Σ_{m≥M}log m·m^{−1−δ} ≥ (2/δ²)(M^{−δ} − 2/3) (via log x ≥ (2/δ)(1 − x^{−δ/2})) ⇒ Φ_q(1−ε+δ) ≤ −(κσ/2)((2/δ²)((N₀+1)^{−δ} − 2/3) − log 2/δ) (order δ^{−2}).
- Bounded receiver of k ⋆ q forces K(1−ε) = 0 AND K'(1−ε) = 0 (pole slope Σk(j)log j·j^{−(1−ε)}): double pole needs double zero. Fermi kernel δ₁ − 2^{1−ε}δ₂ pays d^{−ε} but never log(d)d^{−ε} (slope log 2 ≠ 0).
- THEOREM poleValue_eq_zero_and_poleSlope_eq_zero_of_receiverBounded (proof: δ²·LB(δ) → 2/3; K(σ) has derivative −poleSlope at 0). log(d)d^{−ε} drifts like 2^{−1−ε}n^{1−ε}log n.
## FinitePerturbationsOfEveryPositiveWeightPretendingToOneAreDecidedAtThePole
- b ≥ 0 multiplicative, b(p) = 1, |b| ≤ B (μ², 1, odd squarefree indicator, 1_{(·,q)=1}μ², …): receiver of k ⋆ (b d^{−ε}) bounded ⇔ K(1−ε) = 0 (necessity: b = 1 on squarefree ⇒ dominates odd squarefree drift; sufficiency: b = g ⋆ 1, g critical Wiener, convolutions commute). Paid hyperplane UNIVERSAL: independent of the base and its local factors.
## DampedMoebiusFirstMomentAndChebyshevTangentBridge (start)
- Δ_n(1) = 1/(n(n+1)) ⇒ Mellin pairing at σ = 1 = first pronic moment. Mean of c·log^{k+1} = −M(c·log^k); mean of c·log = −M(c).
- Leibniz: log(a⋆b) = (log a)⋆b + a⋆(log b) pointwise ⇒ M(a ⋆ b) = m_bM(a) + m_aM(b) on damped class; a ⋆ b = δ₁ ⇒ m_am_b = 1, M(a) = −m_a²M(b).
- Docstring claims (to verify below): Cauchy–Schwarz (Σ_{n≤T}A/(n(n+1)))² ≤ E_T; readout–variance decomposition; M(c_ε) = ρ_εΣΛ(n)n^{−1−ε} = Re(−ζ'/ζ²(1+ε)) > 0 → 1 as ε → 0; twin and negated Möbius have M = −M(c_ε); dual residual gap 4Σ_{n≤T}A_{c_ε}(n)/(n(n+1)); log-power receivers bounded for ε > 1/2; A_{c_η·log} = −∂_ηA_{c_η}.
- PROVED (DampedMoebiusFirstMomentAndChebyshevTangentBridge §1–3): Leibniz law M(a⋆b) = m_bM(a) + m_aM(b); M(δ₁) = 0; inverse law. Finite-horizon CS: (Σ_{n≤T}f/(n(n+1)))² ≤ (1 − 1/(T+1))Σf²/(n(n+1)); dual residual bound. Orthogonal readout–variance decomposition for bounded receivers: E_∞ = M² + Var, Dual_∞ = (1 − M)² + Var, Var = Σ(A(n) − M)²/(n(n+1)).
- c_ε ⋆ d^{−ε} = δ₁ ⇒ M(c_ε) = ρ_εΣΛ(e)e^{−1−ε} = moebiusDampedReadout ε = Re(−ζ'(1+ε)/ζ(1+ε)²) > 0 (no Euler products); M(u_ε) = −m_uΣΛe^{−1−ε}; BVI energy mean pmPowerDampedRamanujanEnergyMean ε Q → M(c_ε); M(c_ε) → 1 as ε → 0 (residue of ζ at 1 + (s−1)²ζ' → −1).
- M(c'_ε) = M(c_b) = −M(c_ε) < 0 (→ −1); first-moment gap of Möbius parity pair = 2·readout. Dual residual gap (c_b vs c_ε) = 4Σ_{n≤T}A_{c_ε}(n)/(n(n+1)) → 4·readout > 0 at every ε > 0.
- UNCONDITIONAL for ε ≥ 1/2: c_ε receiver bounded and readout–variance decomposition with M = readout.
- §4: log^k absorbed into d^δ: c·log^k ∈ 𝒞(K((k+1)/δ)^k, ε−δ).
- §4 proved: every log-power weight c·log^k with c ∈ 𝒞(K,ε), ε > 1/2, has bounded receiver (unconditional). TANGENT LAW: ∂_η A_{c_η}(n) = −A_{c_η·log}(n) (centered Möbius prefix derivative in damping; dρ/dη = readout).
## DoubleZeroKernelsAndLogarithmicSmoothPowerAtThePole (start)
- ReceiverBounded is a real vector space on weights with summable c(d)/d (Minkowski for pronic norm).
- g_α(d) = d^{−α}(log d + 1/α) nonnegative antitone on d ≥ 1 (via u = d^α, log(u₂/u₁) ≤ u₂/u₁ − 1); d^{−α}log d = g_α(d) − (1/α)d^{−α} ⇒ Abel block bound 2Bk^{−α}(log k + 2/α).
- Hyperbola split at D = ⌊n^{1/(2+ε/2)}⌋: |A_{log(s d^{−ε})}(n)| ≤ (18B/ε)n^{1/(2+ε/2)} ⇒ bounded-sign patterns twisted by log d pay the receiver at every ε > 0 (energy ≤ 324(B/ε)²Σn^{2θ−2}); in particular χ₄·log and fermiPattern·log.
- Leibniz rearranged: k ⋆ log c = log(k ⋆ c) − (log k) ⋆ c; poleValue(log k) = poleSlope(k).
- THEOREM receiverBounded_dirichletConv_logWeight_unit_iff (0 < ε ≤ 1/2): k ⋆ (d^{−ε}log d) bounded ⇔ poleValue = 0 ∧ poleSlope = 0 (sufficiency every ε > 0). Critical Wiener closed under log at half damping. Universal double-pole law for log(b d^{−ε}), b ≥ 0 multiplicative pretending to one.
- Fermi: first-order kernel δ₁ − 2^{1−ε}δ₂ has pole value 0, slope −log 2 ⇒ pays d^{−ε}, never d^{−ε}log d (ε ≤ 1/2); its convolution square δ₁ − 2·2^{1−ε}δ₂ + 2^{2(1−ε)}δ₄ has double zero ⇒ pays both at every ε > 0.
## SignBlindDampingsStopAtHalfAndSignedDampingsReadTheZeros
- signBlindDampings K = {ε : one budget bounds every receiver of 𝒞(K,ε)} = (1/2, ∞) exactly; inf 1/2; derived set [1/2, ∞).
- receiverDampings (RHTower; ε admissible ⇔ μ(d)d^{−ε} receiver bounded) ⊇ signBlindDampings ∪ [1/2, ∞). Accumulation point ε₀ of receiverDampings ⇒ every zero ρ in strip has Re ρ ≤ 1/2 + ε₀ (RHTower.re_le_of_accPt_receiverDampings). Signed dampings read the zeros below 1/2; sign-blind reading stops at the trivial rate 1/2. (RH ⇔ receiver dampings accumulate at 0 — the premise is open.)
## TheHorizonLimitCommutesWithTheReceiverExactlyFromHalfDamping (start)
- Dyadic rows c_J = c·1_{d≤2^J} centred at own finite mean. Bounded rows always pay the limit (fixed-cutoff energy continuous). ε > 1/2: uniform constant bounds weight and all rows on 𝒞(K,ε). At ε = 1/2: tail |Σ_{d>N}c(d)/d| ≤ 2KN^{−1/2} (Nτ_N² ≤ 4K²); beyond horizon row = sawtooth sum bounded by Σ_{d≤N}|c| ≤ 3K√N, pronic tail ≤ 9K²; pronic tail mass Σ_{X≤n≤T}1/(n(n+1)) = 1/X − 1/(T+1).
- Below 1/2: explicit c ∈ 𝒞(1,ε) with Σc(d)/d = 0, paid, but dyadic rows unbounded (lacunary windowed dipoles b(d) = ±αd on (N−w,N] ∪ (N,N+w]; split row keeps negative half: drift αw·n on [1,N], energy ≥ α²w²N/4; exponents N = 2^{Lt}, w = 2^{Mt} exist iff ε < 1/2). ⇒ horizon limit commutes with receiver on 𝒞(1,ε) ⇔ ε ≥ 1/2.
- PROVED rowEnergy_le_of_half: at ε = 1/2, paid weight (energy ≤ E) ⇒ every dyadic row energy ≤ 2E + 17K². rows_bounded_iff_receiverBounded_of_half_le: ε ≥ 1/2 ⇒ rows uniformly paid ⇔ weight paid.
- Hyperbola swap Σ_{d≤n}c(d)⌊n/d⌋ = Σ_{k≤n}S(⌊n/k⌋), S plain partial sums.
- Windowed dipole b = αd on (N,N+w], −αd on (N−w,N]: harmonic mass 0 beyond window; negative half mass −αw; plain mass beyond window P = αw²; total variation ≤ 2wα(N+w). Prefix beyond window = bounded sawtooth; |A(n)| ≤ P⌊n/(N+w)⌋ + (Σ|b|)·D(n), D(n) = #{k : ⌊n/k⌋ strictly in window} ≤ 1 when 2wn ≤ (N−w+1)²; pronic mass of D below cut X ≤ ⌊X/l⌋·2w/l², l = N−w+1. One-dipole energy E_T ≤ 2P²X/(N+w)² + 2B²⌊X/l⌋2w/l² + B²/X.
- Lacunary witness: stage t dipole centre N_t = 2^{Lt}, half-width w_t = 2^{Mt}, amplitude α_t = (2N_t)^{−1−ε} (puts weight in 𝒞(1,ε)); cut X_t = 2^{Qt}. M + 3 ≤ L ⇒ windows disjoint, ordered; one active stage per d; α_tw_t ≤ 8^{−t} ⇒ mean zero (absolutely). Weighted CS (Σa_t)² ≤ Σ2^t a_t² ⇒ E(c) ≤ Σ2^tE(b_t); 2^tE_T(b_t) ≤ 514(q₁^t + q₂^t), q₁ = 2^{1+3M+Q−L−(2+2ε)L}, q₂ = 2^{1+2M+2L−Q−(2+2ε)L}.
- Splitting row (horizon N_t) mean −α_tw_t; below horizon row = weight + drift α_tw_t·n; row energy ≥ (αw)²N/4 − E₀ = 2^{−2−2ε}/4·2^{(2M+L−(2+2ε)L)t} − E₀.
- Exponents exist iff ε < 1/2: need (1+2ε)L < 2M, 3M+Q+1 < (3+2ε)L, 2M+1 < Q+2εL (L = ⌈26/(1−2ε)⌉, M ≈ (1+2ε)L/2 + 1, Q = L+6).
- THEOREM horizonLimit_commutes_with_receiver_iff (ε ≥ 0): on 𝒞(1,ε), [rows uniformly paid ⇔ weight paid for every c] ⇔ ε ≥ 1/2. Same threshold as parity barrier.
## CriticalWienerPerturbationsOfTheSmoothPowerAreDecidedAtThePole (start)
- 0 < ε < 1/2, q = d^{−ε}: for every critical Wiener kernel k (Σ|k(j)|j^{−1/2} < ∞), k ⋆ q receiver bounded ⇔ K(1−ε) = Σk(j)j^{−(1−ε)} = 0. Paid perturbations = hyperplane.
- Sufficiency: k = Σk(j)(δ_j − j^{−(1−ε)}δ₁); pole-free dilation b_j = (δ_j − j^{−(1−ε)}δ₁) ⋆ q = d^{−ε}(j^ε[j|d] − j^{−(1−ε)}); sign partial sums j^ε(⌊N/j⌋ − N/j), bounded by j^ε; mean (1/j − j^{−(1−ε)})m_q.
- Sharp hyperbola bound for damped signs: |A_c(n)| ≤ Σ_{d≤D}|c(d)| + 4Bn(D+1)^{−1−ε}.
- Below dilation (n < j): A_{b_j}(n) = −j^{−(1−ε)}A_q(n) − m_q n/j; energy O(j^{2ε−2}Σ_{n<j}n^{−2ε}) = O(1/j) (uses ε < 1/2). Beyond (n ≥ j): split D = ⌊√(jn)⌋ gives |A_{b_j}(n)| ≤ 2 + (2/(1−ε)+4)(n/j)^{(1−ε)/2}; tail energy ≤ (8 + 2C₁²(1+1/ε))/j. ⇒ E_T(b_j) ≤ C/j; Minkowski ‖A_{k⋆q}‖ ≤ √C Σ|k(j)|j^{−1/2}.
- Assembly lemma: if E_T(b_j) ≤ w(j)², Σ|k(j)|w(j) < ∞, kernel absolutely convergent at pole and s=1, K(1−ε) = 0 ⇒ k ⋆ d^{−ε} paid with energy ≤ (Σ|k|w)² (Minkowski on truncations, fixed-cutoff continuity). THEOREM receiverBounded_dirichletConv_unit_iff_critical (0<ε<1/2, CriticalWiener k): paid ⇔ Σk(j)j^{−(1−ε)} = 0.
## APaidReceiverContinuesTheDirichletSeriesAcrossItsAbscissa (start)
- Complex Mellin pairing Φ(s) = Σ_n A(n)(n^{−s} − (n+1)^{−s}); |n^{−s} − (n+1)^{−s}| ≤ |s|n^{−Re s−1}; bounded pronic energy ⇒ Σ|f(n)|n^{−b−1} < ∞ for b > 1/2 ⇒ Φ holomorphic on Re s > 1/2.
- Φ(σ) = ζ(σ)(C(σ) − m) on a < σ < 1 (C(σ) = Σc(d)d^{−σ}, m = Σc(d)/d, abscissa a). PAID RECEIVER ⇒ ζ(s)(C(s) − m) continues holomorphically to Re s > 1/2; C − m = Φ/ζ meromorphic there with poles only at zeta zeros.
- ζ(σ) ≠ 0 on 0 < σ < 1 (|ζ(σ)| ≥ σ/(1−σ)). For 1/2 < a < 1: Φ/ζ analytic at a ⇒ C Lipschitz at abscissa: |C(σ) − L| ≤ M(σ − a). If κu^α ≤ |C(a+u) − L| ≤ Ku^α then α ∈ ℕ (order of vanishing). Two-sided power-law comparability forces equal exponents.
- Quantization of prime mean: Euler product: exp(P(w) ∓ 2S) brackets Σf(n)n^{−w} (f completely multiplicative, |f(p)| ≤ 1, S = Σp^{−2}); |−log(1−y) − y| ≤ 2y² for |y| ≤ 1/2. THEOREM not_receiverBounded_of_primeMean: 0<ε<1/2, prime mean ν (Σf(p)p^{−w} = ν log(1/(w−1)) + O(1)), ν ∉ {0,−1,−2,…} ⇒ f(d)d^{−ε} unpaid (ν>0: series unbounded vs Lipschitz; ν<0: non-integral order). Mertens at the pole |Σp^{−w} − log(1/(w−1))| ≤ 2S + log(3/2); ζ-series ∈ [1/(w−1), 1+1/(w−1)]. Corollary not_receiverBounded_of_flippedDensity: ±1 completely multiplicative with flipped density ρ (ν = 1−2ρ) paid below half damping ⇒ ρ ∈ {1/2} ∪ [1,∞).
- At half damping abscissa = boundary 1/2 of the holomorphy half-plane; only growth bound O((σ−1/2)^{−1/2}) survives.
## TheHalfDampedHyperplaneLawHasAnExactLogarithmicThreshold (start)
- Little-o pole law: Σ|c(d)|d^{−θ} < ∞ ∀θ>1/2 and paid ⇒ √u·|C(1/2+u) − m| → 0 as u↓0 (head bounded; tail √(tail energy)·O(u^{−1/2}); |ζ(σ)| ≥ 1 on [1/2,1)). Mellin cell ≤ 1; head–tail: √u|ΣAΔ| ≤ √uΣ_{n≤N}|A| + 2√R.
- Half damping: pole-free dilation energy O((1+log j)/j) (below j: |A_q(n)| ≤ 5√n, harmonic sum).
- THEOREM receiverBounded_dirichletConv_unit_half_iff: on √log-weighted algebra Σ|k(j)|j^{−1/2}√(1+log j) < ∞, k ⋆ d^{−1/2} paid ⇔ K(1/2) = 0.
- Lacunary kernel k = Σ_r 2^{−(r+1)}J_r^{1/2}δ_{J_r} − δ₁, J_r = 2^{4^{r+1}}: K(σ) = Σ2^{−(r+1)}J_r^{−(σ−1/2)} − 1; K(1/2) = 0; K(1/2+u) ≤ −2^{−(R+1)}(1 − J_R^{−u}); probe u_R = 4^{−(R+1)} gives J_R^{−u_R} = 1/2; Σd^{−1/2}d^{−(1/2+u)} ≥ 1/u ⇒ √u_R|C(1/2+u_R)| ≥ 1/2 ⇒ unpaid (little-o law). Lies in Σ|k|j^{−1/2}(1+log j)^α < ∞ for every α < 1/2, not in √log algebra.
- THEOREMS criticalWiener_hyperplaneLaw_iff_lt_half (0<ε≤1/2): hyperplane law on whole critical Wiener algebra ⇔ ε < 1/2. halfDamped_hyperplaneLaw_logarithmic_threshold: at ε = 1/2 exact threshold is weight √log; no power (log)^α, α < 1/2, suffices.
## TheHalfDampedReceiverIsTheLogarithmicEnergyOfThePartialPoleSums (start)
- q = d^{−1/2}, k critical Wiener; S_k(n) = Σ_{j≤n}k(j)j^{−1/2} (partial pole sums), L_T(k) = Σ_{n≤T}S_k(n)²/(n+1). |√E_T(k⋆q) − 2|Z|√L_T(k)| ≤ B uniformly in T; Z = lim(Σ_{d≤N}d^{−1/2} − 2√N) ≤ −1 (halfZeta). ⇒ k ⋆ d^{−1/2} paid ⇔ Σ_n S_k(n)²/(n+1) < ∞.
- Half-harmonic defect g(N) = H(N) − 2√N antitone, g(1) = −1, ≥ −2; rate g(N) − N^{−1/2} ≤ Z ≤ g(N).
- Hyperbola identity (s = ⌊√n⌋): Σ_{d≤n}f(d)⌊n/d⌋ = Σ_{d≤s}f(d)⌊n/d⌋ + Σ_{m≤s}(F(⌊n/m⌋) − F(s)). A_q(n) = 2Z√n + O(n^{1/4}): |R(n)| ≤ 14⌊√n⌋^{1/2} (n^{3/4} terms cancel into −(2/√s)(√n − s)²).
- Convolution law A_{k⋆q}(n) = Σ_{j≤n}k(j)A_q(⌊n/j⌋) + m_qA_k(n); main terms 2Z√n S_k(n); dilated remainders pronic norm O(j^{−1/2}).
- PROVED |R(N)| ≤ 14N^{1/4}; each dilated remainder pronic norm ≤ √3(14+2|Z|)j^{−1/2}. THEOREM receiverBounded_dirichletConv_unit_half_iff_summable_partialPoleSum (critical Wiener k): k ⋆ d^{−1/2} paid ⇔ Σ_n S_k(n)²/(n+1) < ∞. Half-damped receiver = logarithmic mean square of partial pole sums (signed quantity); lacunary kernel has S_k = −2^{−(R+1)} on [J_R, J_{R+1}) ⇒ diverges.
## CriticalWienerPerturbationsOfTheSmoothPowerHaveUniformlyPaidRows (start)
- 0<ε<1/2, critical Wiener k: rows of k⋆d^{−ε} uniformly paid ⇔ k⋆d^{−ε} paid ⇔ K(1−ε) = 0. Convolution structure excludes the dipole obstruction.
- Row below horizon = weight + drift (m − m_J)n; beyond = sawtooth, |A| ≤ Σ_{d≤2^J}|c(d)|. Row of damped sign = damped row of sign; truncation keeps partial sums bounded. Rows of smooth power energy O(N^{1−2ε}) (drift 0 ≤ τ ≤ N^{−ε}/ε).
- Pole-free dilation rows energy O(1/j) uniformly in N. Finite decomposition row(k⋆q) = Σ_{j≤N}k(j)row(b_j) + P_N row(q), P_N = Σ_{j≤N}k(j)j^{−(1−ε)}; K(1−ε)=0 ⇒ |P_N| ≤ N^{−(1/2−ε)}Σ|k|j^{−1/2}, exactly paying growth N^{1/2−ε}.
- Beyond-dilation bound holds for every damped sign dominated by pole-free sign with partial sums ≤ j^ε; drift of row of b_j |m_{b_j} − m_N| ≤ 2j^ε(N+1)^{−1−ε} ≤ 2/j. N < j: row(b_j) = −j^{−(1−ε)}row(q), energy j^{−(2−2ε)}·O(N^{1−2ε}) ≤ O(1/j). THEOREM rows_bounded_of_critical: rows energy ≤ ((√C + √C_q)G)², G = Σ|k|j^{−1/2}; tail |P_N| ≤ (N+1)^{−(1/2−ε)}G.
- THEOREMS rows_bounded_iff_receiverBounded_dirichletConv_unit, rows_bounded_iff_dirichletConv_unit_poleValue_eq_zero (0<ε<1/2, critical Wiener): rows uniformly paid ⇔ paid ⇔ K(1−ε) = 0.
## TheOscillatingSmoothPowerIsPaidExactlyAtTheZerosOfZeta (start)
- c_{ε,t}(d) = d^{−ε}cos(t log d) = Re d^{ρ−1}, ρ = 1−ε+it. THEOREM receiverBounded_oscillatingSmoothPower_iff (0<ε≤1/2): paid ⇔ ζ(1−ε+it) = 0. RH ⇔ no oscillating smooth power below half damping is paid; at half damping paid frequencies = ordinates of critical-line zeros. (Exact reformulation; no RH progress.)
- Mean of c_{ε,t} = Re ζ(2−ρ). Strip remainder g(N) = Σ_{d≤N}d^{−w} − N^{1−w}/(1−w): step ‖g(M+1)−g(M)‖ ≤ ‖w‖M^{−1−Re w}; rate ‖g(N) − ζ(w)‖ ≤ ‖w‖(1+1/Re w)N^{−Re w}; real-cutoff version with C_w = (‖w‖(1+1/Re w)+1)2^{Re w}.
- Floor-sum asymptotic (hyperbola at √n): ‖Σ_{d≤n}d^{ρ−1}⌊n/d⌋ − ζ(2−ρ)n − ζ(ρ)n^ρ/ρ‖ ≤ K_ρ(√n)^{Re ρ} for 0 < Re ρ < 1. Three main terms of order n^{(1+Re ρ)/2} cancel to n^{ρ/2}(√n − ⌊√n⌋)/ρ. ⇒ A(n) = Re(ζ(ρ)n^ρ/ρ) + O(n^{(1−ε)/2}).
- Sufficiency: at a zero A(n) = O(n^{(1−ε)/2}), finite pronic energy since (1−ε)/2 < 1/2. Necessity: away from zero main term n^{1−ε}Re(a n^{it}), a = ζ(ρ)/ρ, carries log energy (|a|²/4)H_T + O(1); oscillating part Re(a²Σn^{−(1−2it)}) bounded for t ≠ 0.
- Sequence of size K(√n)^{1−ε} has pronic energy ≤ K²(1+1/ε). Necessity proof: t = 0 case: Re(ζ(1−ε)/(1−ε)) < 0 ⇒ main term Re(a)n^{1−ε} carries harmonic energy; t ≠ 0: u² = (|a|² + Re(a²n^{2it}))/2, Σn^{−(1−2it)} bounded (partial sums of ζ at 1−2it with rate). THEOREMS receiverBounded_oscillatingSmoothPower_half_iff (ζ(1/2+it)=0) and riemannHypothesis_iff_forall_not_receiverBounded_oscillatingSmoothPower (RH ⇔ no paid d^{−ε}cos(t log d), 0<ε<1/2).
## TheLogTwistedOscillatingSmoothPowerIsPaidExactlyAtTheDoubleZerosOfZeta (start)
- d^{−ε}cos(t log d)log d paid ⇔ ζ(ρ) = ζ'(ρ) = 0 (0<ε≤1/2). All zeros in 1/2 ≤ Re s < 1 simple ⇔ no such weight paid. Receiver reads order of vanishing.
- Floor remainder R_n(ρ) holomorphic off {0,1}; uniform bound |R_n(ρ)| ≤ K(√n)^{1−ε/2} on disc |ρ−ρ₀| ≤ ε/2 (discRate); Cauchy: |∂_ρR_n(ρ₀)| ≤ (2K/ε)(√n)^{1−ε/2}; iterated k!K(√n)^{1−ε/2}/(ε/2)^k.
- Log-twisted mean = −Re ζ'(2−ρ) (ζ' = −L(log) on Re s > 1); prefix = Re(n^ρ(b + a log n)) + O(n^{1/2−ε/4}), a = ζ(ρ)/ρ, b = ζ'(ρ)/ρ − ζ(ρ)/ρ². Double zero ⇒ paid. Necessity: energy grows ~(|a|²/4)(log T)³ if a ≠ 0 (oscillating part O((log T)²) by Abel), ~(|b|²/4)log T if a = 0 ≠ b.
- Paid receiver with prefix n^{1−ε}u(n) + O((√n)^{1−δ}) ⇒ Σu(n)²/n bounded. Harmonic windows N < n ≤ N²: Σ1/n ∈ [log N − 1, 1 + 2log N]. Oscillating harmonic sums ‖Σ_{n≤T}n^{iτ}/n‖ ≤ B_τ (τ ≠ 0). Window energy of Re(c n^{it}) ≥ (|c|²/2)Σ1/n − |c|²B_{2t}. Logarithmic amplification lemma: (w + v log n)² window energy ~ (log N)³ beats O(log N).
- THEOREMS receiverBounded_logOscillatingSmoothPower_iff, _half_iff (paid ⇔ 1/2+it zero of order ≥ 2), forall_simple_zero_iff_forall_not_receiverBounded (simplicity of zeros in 1/2 ≤ Re s < 1 ⇔ no log-twisted weight paid). Reformulation; open.
## TheReceiverReadsTheZeroDivisorOfZetaOnTheOscillatingSpectrum (start)
- c(d) = Σ_{t∈J}Σ_{k≤K}Re(p(t,k)d^{ρ_t−1}(log d)^k), J finite positive frequencies: paid ⇔ at every t, ζ vanishes at ρ_t to order > log-degree of p(t,·). Frequencies don't interact. d^{−ε}cos(t log d)(log d)^k paid ⇔ ζ^{(i)}(ρ_t) = 0 ∀ i ≤ k. Receiver reads the complete zero divisor (with multiplicity) in 1/2 ≤ Re s < 1.
- Taylor in exponent: Σd^{ρ−1}(log d)^k⌊n/d⌋ − L_k(2−ρ)n = ∂^kR_n(ρ) + Σ_{i≤k}C(k,i)f^{(i)}(ρ)n^ρ(log n)^{k−i}, f = ζ/id, L_k(s) = Σ(log d)^kd^{−s}; ζ^{(k)}(s) = (−1)^kL_k(s) on Re s > 1. Triangular coefficient matrix q(t,m) = Σ_{k≥m}p(t,k)C(k,m)f_t^{(k−m)}; all q = 0 ⇔ f vanishes up to degree. Cross frequencies t ± s ≠ 0 have bounded harmonic sums; descend in degree.
- Triangular law: all q(m) = 0 ⇔ F vanishes up to deg p (strong induction from top index). ζ/id and ζ vanish to same order at ρ ≠ 0,1. Multi-frequency window energy: Σ_{N<n≤T}Re(Σc_tn^{it})²/n ≥ (Σ|c_t|²/2)Σ1/n − D(J,c), D from cross frequencies t±s (positive frequencies ⇒ t+s ≠ 0). Power-amplified windows: ((log n)^Kv + w)² with |w| ≤ β(log n)^{K−1} unbounded. Descent: top coefficient vanishes, induct down.
- THEOREMS receiverBounded_oscillatingLogPolynomial_iff, receiverBounded_logPowerCosine_iff (t>0): d^{−ε}cos(t log d)(log d)^k paid ⇔ order of zero at 1−ε+it > k.
## TheProbeReceiverEnergyMeasuresTheModulusOfZeta (start)
- Probe d^{−ε}cos(t log d): 0<ε<1/2: E_T/T^{1−2ε} − Φ(T) → 0, Φ(T) = |a|²/(2(1−2ε)) + ½Re(a²T^{2it}/(1−2ε+2it)), a = ζ(ρ)/ρ. ε = 1/2, t ≠ 0: E_T/log T → |ζ(1/2+it)|²/(2|1/2+it|²). Receiver is calibrated instrument for |ζ|² on vertical lines.
- |ζ(1/2+it)|² = 2|1/2+it|² lim E_T/log T (evaluates ζ from finite divisor-sum energies). Below half damping Φ ∈ |a|²/2(1/(1−2ε) ∓ 1/|1−2ε+2it|) ⇒ E_T ≍ |ζ(ρ)|²T^{1−2ε} for t ≠ 0 (eventually_probeEnergy_le_and_ge).
- Mechanism: |√E_T − ‖M‖_T| ≤ K√(1+1/ε), M(n) = Re(a n^ρ); M²/(n(n+1)) = |a|²/2·n^{1−2ε}/(n+1) + ½Re(a²n^β/(n+1)), β = 1−2ε+2it; Σ_{n≤T}n^β/(n+1) = T^β/β + O(1) (0 < Re β < 1); |Σ1/(n+1) − log T| ≤ 2; ‖Σn^{iτ}/(n+1)‖ ≤ B_τ + 2. Lemma: |√E−x| ≤ B, |x² − κ log T| ≤ C ⇒ E/log T → κ.
- THEOREMS tendsto_probeEnergy_half_div_log (t ≠ 0), norm_sq_riemannZeta_half_eq_limit: 2|1/2+it|²E_T/log T → |ζ(1/2+it)|².
## TheHalfDampedReceiverIsDiagonalOnTheProbeSpectrum
- c = Σ_{t∈J}α_t d^{−1/2}cos(t log d), J positive frequencies: E_T(c)/log T → Σα_t²|ζ(1/2+it)|²/(2|1/2+it|²). Receiver on span of half-damped probes is asymptotically diagonal with spectral weight |ζ|²/(2|ρ|²); paid ⇔ every active frequency is a critical-zero ordinate. Main term √n Re(Σc_tn^{it}) + O(n^{1/4}); orthogonality Σ_{n≤T}Re(Σc_tn^{it})²/(n+1) = (Σ|c_t|²/2)log T + O(1).
## TheHalfDampedReceiverReadsTheDirichletSeriesAtTheCriticalPoint (start)
- Half-damped class |c(d)| ≤ Kd^{−1/2}: Mellin pairing Σ_nA(n)Δ_n(σ) = ζ(σ)(C(σ) − m) for 1/2 < σ < 1. Prefix A(n) = Σ_dc(d)α_d(n), α_d(n) = ⌊n/d⌋ − n/d (single-modulus defect; α_d(n+1) = α_d(n) + [d|n+1] − 1/d; |α_d(n)| ≤ (n/d)^θ). One modulus pairs to ζ(σ)(d^{−σ} − 1/d): summation by parts, along N = dM growing terms d^{−σ}M^{1−σ} and (dM)^{1−σ}/d cancel exactly; strip remainder Σ_{k≤N}k^{−σ} − N^{1−σ}/(1−σ) → ζ(σ). Fubini paid by min(1,n/d) ≤ (n/d)^θ, 1/2 < θ < σ.
- Re ζ(σ) ≤ −σ/(1−σ) on (0,1) ⇒ |ζ(σ)| ≥ 1 on [1/2,1). General pole law: paid half-damped weight has |C(σ) − m| ≤ 2√E(2σ−1)^{−1/2} (no factorization needed).
- Strip remainder ≤ 1 − 1/(1−σ) (integral comparison) ⇒ Re ζ(σ) ≤ −σ/(1−σ), |ζ(σ)| ≥ σ/(1−σ) on (0,1). Pole law exact constant: |C(σ) − m| ≤ ((1−σ)/σ)√E√(2σ²(1+1/(2σ−1))); half-damped asymptotic form 2√E(2σ−1)^{−1/2}.
- Few flipped primes: real Euler product Σf(n)n^{−w} = exp(Σ_p −log(1 − f(p)p^{−w})); all-ones ≥ 1/(w−1); flipping prime costs ≤ 2p^{−w} + 2p^{−2w} in exponent ⇒ flipped density δ: Σf(n)n^{−w} ≥ e^{−2B−2S}(w−1)^{−(1−2δ)}. Pattern series growing faster than (w−1)^{−1/2} never paid at half damping ⇒ THEOREM not_receiverBounded_of_flippedDensity_lt_quarter: f(p) = ±1 with flipped upper density δ < 1/4 ⇒ f(d)d^{−1/2} unpaid. (Liouville has δ = 1: not covered.)
## APaidReceiverBoundsTheDirichletSeriesUpToItsAbscissa (start)
- 1/2 < a, Σ|c|d^{−θ} < ∞ ∀θ > a, paid ⇒ |C(σ) − m| ≤ √E√(2(1+1/(2a−1))) uniformly on a < σ < 1. Blow-up of |C(σ)| at abscissa ⇒ unpaid. On 𝒞(K,ε), ε < 1/2, abscissa 1−ε.
- Product law Σ(k⋆q)(n)n^{−σ} = K(σ)Q(σ) (absolute convergence). Dirichlet series absolutely convergent at a right-continuous at a; nonneg divergent series → +∞.
- THEOREM not_receiverBounded_dirichletConv_of_not_summable (0<ε<1/2): q ≥ 0 in 𝒞(K,ε) with Σq(d)d^{−(1−ε)} = ∞, Σ|k(j)|j^{−(1−ε)} < ∞, K(1−ε) ≠ 0 ⇒ k⋆q unpaid. Kernel needs absolute convergence only at abscissa 1−ε (not 1/2); |k⋆q| ≤ |k|⋆|q|.
## TheHalfDampedReceiverIsTheBoundaryEnergyOfItsDirichletSeries (start)
- Half-damped c, G(s) = ζ(s)(C(s) − m): for 1/2 < σ < 1, Σ_nA(n)²(n^{−2σ} − (n+1)^{−2σ}) = (σ/π)∫|G(σ+it)/(σ+it)|²dt. Inputs: complex Mellin pairing Σ_nA(n)(n^{−s} − (n+1)^{−s}) = G(s) on 1/2 < Re s < 1 (Fubini via |n^{−s} − (n+1)^{−s}| ≤ (|s|/σ)(n^{−σ} − (n+1)^{−σ})); Mellin–Plancherel for step function y ↦ A(⌊y⌋) on (1,∞), Mellin transform at −s = G(s)/s.
- Receiver bounded ⇔ line energies of G(s)/s bounded as σ ↓ 1/2: half-damped receiver = H² boundary energy of ζ(s)(C(s) − C(1))/s on critical line. Reads ζ on strip through |ζ|², the weight through Dirichlet series near Re s = 1.
- Step source A(⌊y⌋); |A(n)| ≤ B√n ⇒ log step source e^{σu}A(⌊e^{−u}⌋) bounded by Be^{(σ−1/2)u} on u ≤ 0, integrable and L² for σ > 1/2; Mellin at −(σ+it) = Fourier transform of log step source.
- THEOREM lineEnergy_stepSource_eq: ∫|𝓜A(−σ−it)|²dt = (π/σ)Σ_nA(n)²(n^{−2σ} − (n+1)^{−2σ}) (σ > 1/2, |A(n)| ≤ B√n). THEOREM sum_sq_mul_mellinCell_eq_lineEnergy (spectral identity of half-damped receiver). Line integrand integrable on each 1/2 < σ < 1. Paid receiver caps cell energies Σ A²Δ_n(2σ) ≤ 4σE; cell energies → pronic energies as σ ↓ 1/2. THEOREM weightPronicEnergy_le_of_lineEnergy_le: line energies ≤ M on (1/2, 3/4) ⇒ pronic energy ≤ 3M/(4π).
## TheQuarterLawOfTheConstantSignPattern (start)
- f_z(n) = z^{Ω(n)}, z ∈ [0,1] (mean-field model of flipped density δ = (1−z)/2); F_z ~ ζ^z near 1. c_z(d) = f_z(d)d^{−1/2} paid ⇔ z < 1/2 (δ > 1/4).
- z < 1/2: boundary-energy identity; Euler: |F_z(w)| ≤ e^{2S}|ζ(w)|^z on Re w > 1 (one Euler factor |Re(−log(1−zy)) − z Re(−log(1−y))| ≤ 2|y|²; also reverse ≥ e^{−2S}|ζ|^z); near w=1 O(|Im w|^{−z}) square integrable ⇔ 2z < 1; at |t| ≥ 2 O(log|t|), paid by dyadic mean square of ζ on strip.
- z > 1/2: F_z(w) ≥ e^{−S}(w−1)^{−z} vs pole-law cap O((σ−1/2)^{−1/2}). z = 1/2 (saturated): |F_{1/2}|² ≥ e^{−4S}|ζ(w)| ≥ e^{−4S}/(2|w−1|) off axis ⇒ line energy at 1/2+u ≥ κlog(1/u) − O(1) vs paid cap 4πE.
- Threshold = square integrability of |w−1|^{−z} on Re w = 1; parity-barrier critical density 1/4 = order 1/2. Tools: |ζ(w)||Im w| ≤ K₀ on 1 < Re w ≤ 2, |Im w| ≤ 2 (pole-cleared ζ); ζ bounded on box [1/2,3/4]×[−2,2]; ∫_{t>2}|ζ(σ+it)|²t^{−3/2}dt bounded uniformly 1/2 ≤ σ ≤ 3 (dyadic log-cube mean square moment, RHTower).
- Line integrand even in t (conjugation). Constant-sign line energy bound: small heights Φ ≤ A₁(u^{−2z}+1) (integrable ⇔ 2z < 1), large heights ≤ Λ|ζ|²u^{−3/2} (|ζ(w)| ≤ log u + 5 on Re w ∈ (1,…], RHTower.norm_riemannZeta_le_log_add). THEOREMS receiverBounded_constantSign (0≤z<1/2), not_receiverBounded_constantSign (1/2<z≤1), not_receiverBounded_constantSign_half (|ζ(w)| ≥ 1/(2|w−1|) near pole; |ζ(s)| > 1/2 near s=1/2 since |ζ(1/2)| ≥ 1; integrand ≥ κ/(u+t) − m²/4 on [0,r]; line energy ≥ κlog((u+r)/u) − m²r/4 vs 4πE), receiverBounded_constantSign_iff (0≤z≤1): paid ⇔ z < 1/2.
## TheQuarterLawOfTheConstantSignPatternOnTheFullRange (start)
- −1 ≤ z < 0: |F_z(w)| ≤ e^{2S}(1 + 1/|ζ(w)|); 1/|ζ(w)| ≤ K|Im w|^{1/4} for 1 < Re w ≤ 2, |Im w| ≥ 9 (de la Vallée Poussin strip |1/ζ| ≤ (20/A)log⁷|t|; beyond via 3–4–1: 1/|ζ(σ+it)| ≤ (1+1/(σ−1))^{3/4}(log|2t|+5)^{1/4}); bounded on box |Im w| ≤ 9 (pole + compactness + ζ ≠ 0 on Re ≥ 1). Bounded small-height + O(|t|^{1/4}) large-height ⇒ line energy paid.
- PROVED: 1/|ζ(w)| ≤ K|Im w|^{1/4} on 1 < Re w ≤ 2, |Im w| ≥ 9; bounded on box |Im w| ≤ 9. Line-energy criterion exists_lineEnergy_le_of_bounds (|C−P| ≤ B₀ for |t| ≤ 2, ≤ B₁|t|^{1/4} for |t| ≥ 2 ⇒ uniform line energy). THEOREMS receiverBounded_constantSign_neg (−1 ≤ z < 0 paid), receiverBounded_constantSign_iff_full (z ∈ [−1,1]: z^{Ω(d)}d^{−1/2} paid ⇔ z < 1/2). z = −1 = Liouville λ(d)d^{−1/2} PAID unconditionally (uses only ζ ≠ 0 on Re ≥ 1 + zero-free region growth).
## ResidueClassFlipsAndThePrimeDensityLaws (start)
- residueFlip f_{q,S}: f(p) = −1 iff p mod q ∈ S. Exact log density ρ = #(S ∩ units)/φ(q): |Σ_{p mod q∈S}p^{−w} − ρlog(1/(w−1))| ≤ B on 1 < w < 3/2 (Mathlib Dirichlet: Σ Λ_a(n)n^{−x} = φ(q)^{−1}/(x−1) + O(1); prime powers O(1); p^{−w} − p^{−3/2} = ∫ log p·p^{−t}dt with dominated convergence). Non-unit classes contain only primes dividing q.
- Discharges density premises: half damping: 4#(S∩units) < φ(q) ⇒ f_{q,S}(d)d^{−1/2} unpaid; 0<ε<1/2: ρ ≠ 1/2 and ρ < 1 ⇒ f_{q,S}(d)d^{−ε} unpaid.
- THEOREMS not_receiverBounded_residueFlip_half (4#(S∩units) < φ(q)), not_receiverBounded_residueFlip (0<ε<1/2, 2#(S∩units) ≠ φ(q), #(S∩units) < φ(q)): unconditional (Dirichlet's theorem input).
## ThePhaseDiagramOfTheConstantSignPattern
- THEOREM receiverBounded_constantSign_damped_iff (z ∈ [−1,1], ε > 0): z^{Ω(d)}d^{−ε} paid ⇔ ε > 1/2 ∨ (ε = 1/2 ∧ z < 1/2) ∨ z = 0 ∨ (z = −1 ∧ μ(d)d^{−ε} paid). Below half damping prime mean ν = z; z = 0 is δ₁ (prefix ≡ 0); only nontrivial point is Liouville line z = −1 whose admissible dampings = Möbius receiver dampings = zero width Θ − 1/2 (open).
## TheQuarterBoundaryOfResidueClassFlips (start)
- ρ ≤ 1/4 (boundary included) ⇒ f_{q,S}(d)d^{−1/2} unpaid. Complex class density: on box 1 < Re s < 2, |Im s| < 1, Re Σ_{p≡a}p^{−s} = φ(q)^{−1}log(1/|s−1|) + O(1) (G(s) = Σp^{−s} + φ^{−1}log(s−1); G' = −Aux_a(s) + prime powers, bounded via Mathlib continuousOn_LFunctionResidueClassAux; MVT).
- Euler lower bound ±1 pattern: log|F(w)| ≥ log|ζ(w)| − 2Σ_{f(p)=−1}Re p^{−w} − 2Σp^{−2} (flipped factor Re(−log(1+y)) ≥ Re(−log(1−y)) − 2Re y − 2|y|²). With |ζ(w)| ≥ 1/(2|w−1|): ρ ≤ 1/4 ⇒ |F(w)|² ≥ κ/|w−1|; local singularity |C(s)|² ≥ κ/|s−1/2| makes line energy grow like log(1/u).
- Local |s−1/2|^{−1/2} singularity never paid (not_receiverBounded_of_sq_norm_dirichletSeries_ge). THEOREM not_receiverBounded_residueFlip_half_of_le: 4#(S∩units) ≤ φ(q) ⇒ unpaid (boundary ρ = 1/4 included). Unconditional.
## TheOscillatingSmoothPowerShiftsItsPoleOffTheRealAbscissa (start)
- c_{ε,t} ∈ 𝒞(1,ε). Real-axis Dirichlet series Σc_{ε,t}(d)d^{−σ} = Re ζ((σ+ε) − it) for σ > 1−ε. t ≠ 0: converges to Re ζ(1−it) and Lipschitz at abscissa 1−ε, paid or unpaid ⇒ real-axis blowup/Lipschitz tests cannot detect unpaid oscillating probes (pole shifted to 1−ε ± it). TRAP: real-axis tests blind to oscillation.
- Unconditional: t = 0 unpaid for all 0<ε≤1/2 (ζ ≠ 0 on (0,1)); bounded box |t| ≤ T: ∃δ, unpaid for ε ≤ min(δ,1/2) (zero-free box); VK strip: unpaid for |t| ≥ T₀ and ε < min(1/2, vkHadamardWidth|t|); uniform all-frequency width min(1/2, min(δ, vkHadamardWidth(K+1))/4). Paid dampings isolated at every frequency (zero isolation of (s−1)ζ(s)); finite set of paid dampings in (0,1/2] per t; empty derived set; finite paid frequencies in [−T,T] per ε.
- Shifted-pole hyperplane law: critical Wiener k, 0<ε<1/2: k⋆c_{ε,t} paid ⇔ K(1−ε+it) = 0 or ζ(1−ε+it) = 0.
- Shifted-pole proof: A_{k⋆c_{ε,t}}(n) = Re((ζ(ρ)/ρ)n^ρS_{k,ρ}(n)) + Σ_{j≤n}k(j)R_{ε,t}(n,j) + m_cA_k(n); dilated oscillating remainder pronic norm O(j^{−1/2}); pole-deficit 1_{n<j}n^{1−ε}j^{−(1−ε)} pronic norm ≤ √(1+1/(1−2ε))j^{−1/2}; pronicNorm(n^{1−ε}) ≤ √(1+1/(1−2ε))(T+1)^{1/2−ε}; tail τ of K(ρ) ≤ (T+1)^{−(1/2−ε)}G. ⇒ |‖A_{k⋆c}‖_T − ‖Re(K(ρ)ζ(ρ)/ρ n^ρ)‖_T| ≤ B.
- Harmonic lower bound: bounded pronic norm of Re(a n^ρ) ⇒ a = 0 (t=0 case needs a.re = 0 ⇒ a = 0). THEOREM receiverBounded_dirichletConv_oscillatingSmoothPower_iff (0<ε<1/2, critical Wiener): k⋆c_{ε,t} paid ⇔ K(1−ε+it) = 0 ∨ ζ(1−ε+it) = 0.
## SubgroupFlipsArePaidAtHalfDamping (start)
- H ≤ (ZMod q)ˣ index k ≥ 2; f_H(p) = −1 iff p ∤ q and p mod q ∉ H; flipped density 1 − 1/k ≥ 1/2 > 1/4. THEOREM receiverBounded_subgroupFlip_half: paid at half damping, unconditional.
- Orthogonality [b ∈ H] = φ^{−1}Σ_χ c_χχ(b), c_χ = Σ_{h∈H}χ(h^{−1}) ∈ {0,|H|} real nonneg. f_H(p) = −1 + 2[p|q] + (2/φ)Σ_χc_χχ(p). Euler bound: log|F(w)| ≤ −log|ζ(w)| + (2/φ)Σc_χlog|L(w,χ)| + B; principal character gives (2|H|/φ)log|ζ| ⇒ ζ exponent 2/k − 1 ∈ [−1,0]: needs only 1/|ζ| and |L| from above — no zero-free region for L. |L(w,χ)| ≪ log|t| (RHTower), 1/|ζ| ≪ log^{10}|t|; L(·,χ) entire for χ ≠ 1 ⇒ |F| ≤ B₀ (|t| ≤ 2), ≤ B₁|t|^{1/4} (|t| ≥ 2) ⇒ line-energy criterion.
- Euler log-modulus lemma |log|Σg(n)| − Re Σ_pg(p)| ≤ Σp^{−2} for completely multiplicative |g(p)| ≤ |p^{−w}|. Index k ≥ 3: density ≠ 1/2, 1 ⇒ unpaid below half damping ⇒ receiverBounded_subgroupFlip_iff: f_H(d)d^{−ε} paid ⇔ ε ≥ 1/2.
- 1/|ζ(w)| ≤ K log^{10}|Im w| (1 < Re w ≤ 2, |Im w| ≥ 9); log^{12}x ≤ 48^{12}x^{1/4}. Principal L(w,1) = ζ(w)Π_{p|q}(1−p^{−w}), log-bounded by log|ζ| + ω(q)log 2. Master estimate |F| ≤ e^{B''}(1+1/|ζ|)(1+K)² when |L(w,χ)| ≤ K for χ ≠ 1. Requires 2|H| ≤ φ(q). THEOREMS receiverBounded_subgroupFlip_half (2|H| ≤ φ), receiverBounded_subgroupFlip_iff (3|H| ≤ φ): paid ⇔ ε ≥ 1/2. Unconditional. #flipped = φ − |H|.
## OscillatingPrimePatternsReadTheCriticalZeros (start)
- f_{a,t₀}(p) = a cos(t₀ log p), a ∈ [−1,1]; c = f d^{−1/2}. f(p)p^{−w} = (a/2)(p^{−(w−it₀)} + p^{−(w+it₀)}); |log|F(w)| − (a/2)(log|ζ(w−it₀)| + log|ζ(w+it₀)|)| ≤ 2S; F ≠ 0 on Re > 1; e^{∓2S}Z^{a/2} bounds, Z = |ζ(w−it₀)||ζ(w+it₀)|.
- a ≤ 0 (all t₀): paid (1/ζ bounds right of Re = 1). 0 < a < 1, t₀ ≠ 0: paid (singularity |C(1/2+u+it)|² ≲ |t−t₀|^{−a} integrable). Real-axis prime mean 0 for t₀ ≠ 0, yet amplitude threshold is 1 not 1/2: quarter law applies height by height. t₀ = 0 reduces to constant-sign z^{Ω}. primeValueHom builds completely multiplicative from prime values.
- Tools: |ζ(w)||w−1| ≤ K₀ near pole box; 1/|ζ(v)| ≤ K(1+|Im v|)^{1/4} uniform on 1 < Re v ≤ 2; ζ bounded on tall box; |t−t₀|^r interval integrable r > −1; line-energy criterion with local integrable majorant off one height. Small-height bound |F(w)| ≤ B|w − it₀ − 1|^{−a/2}; large-height |F(w)| ≤ 10e^{2S}(Im w)^{1/4}. THEOREMS receiverBounded_oscWeight_nonpos, receiverBounded_oscWeight_pos_of_lt_one, receiverBounded_oscWeight_of_lt_one (−1 ≤ a < 1, t₀ ≠ 0 paid).
## OscillatingPrimePatternsArePaidExactlyAtTheCriticalZeros (start)
- a = 1, t₀ ≠ 0 on quarter threshold at height t₀: |C(s)|² ≍ |ζ(1+u+i(t−t₀))| ≍ 1/|s−s₀|, s₀ = 1/2+it₀. ζ(s₀) ≠ 0 ⇒ integrand ≥ κ/(u+|t−t₀|) − C' ⇒ log(1/u) line energy ⇒ unpaid. ζ(s₀) = 0 ⇒ |ζ(s)| ≤ L|s−s₀| cancels singularity ⇒ paid.
- THEOREM receiverBounded_oscWeight_iff (a ∈ [−1,1], t₀ ∈ ℝ): f_{a,t₀}(d)d^{−1/2} paid ⇔ (t₀ = 0 ∧ a < 1/2) ∨ (t₀ ≠ 0 ∧ (a < 1 ∨ ζ(1/2+it₀) = 0)). cos(t₀ log p)-pattern paid ⇔ 1/2+it₀ critical zero. (Reformulation of zeros on the line; detects critical zeros, not off-line zeros.)
- ζ Lipschitz near s₀ ≠ 1 (derivative bound on ball); |ζ| > |ζ(s₀)|/2 near nonzero s₀.
- PROVED receiverBounded_oscWeight_one_of_zero, not_receiverBounded_oscWeight_one (t₀ > 0), receiverBounded_oscWeight_one_iff (t₀ ≠ 0; zeros at ±t₀ vanish together by conjugation), receiverBounded_oscWeight_iff. Lower bound uses |ζ(1+2it₀)| > 0 (ζ ≠ 0 on Re = 1) and |ζ(s₀)| > 0.

No real-axis prime datum decides the half-damped receiver; it reads the amplitude at the critical height. (No-go for real-axis prime-statistics routes.)

- VK: ∃K,N₁: ∀ 0<ε≤1/2, n ≥ N₁: |A_ε(n)| ≤ (K/ε)n^{1−ε}exp(−√(log n)/40) for Möbius; receiver energy uniformly bounded over T and ε ∈ [ε₀,1/2] in VK band ε ≥ 1/2 − 1/(80√log T). Transfers to Liouville: λd^{−ε} = (1_□q^{−ε}) ⋆ (μd^{−ε}), square kernel critical-Wiener norm ≤ Σj^{−(1+2ε₀)}.
- Subpower transfer: |Σ_{e≤N}s(e)| ≤ C(N+1)^β ⇒ s(d)d^{−ε} paid for ε > β/2.
- Zero-width admission: every ε > Θ − 1/2 admits Möbius and Liouville receivers and Liouville parity pair (ε ≤ 1/2); RH ⇔ all ε > 0 admitted for both. (Θ = sup Re ρ; conditional on Θ.)
- Proof of hyperbola transfer: short moduli ≤ 3D^{1−ε}, long moduli ≤ (10/ε)LnD^{−ε} (k-sum Σk^{−(1−ε)} ≤ (2/ε)(n/D)^ε), tail ≤ (3/ε)LnD^{−ε}. Trivial bound |A_ε(n)| ≤ 3 + (13/ε)n. VK: |M(x)| ≤ Bx exp(−√log x/12) (exists_abs_sum_moebiusReal_le_sqrtLog), split D = ⌊exp(log n − √log n/20)⌋. Band energy: VK term ≤ (K/ε₀)²Σexp(−√log m/40)/m (summable). Liouville band: pronicNorm_dirichletConv_le with G₀ = critical-Wiener norm of damped squares (antitone in ε), |1/ζ(1+ε)| ≤ Σd^{−(1+ε₀)}.
- Subpower proof: balance at β' = min((β+2ε)/2, 1), a = 1/(2−β'), D = ⌊n^a⌋, prefix ≤ (3 + 52C/ε)n^γ with γ = a(1−ε) < 1/2.
- THEOREMS receiverBounded_moebius/liouville_of_zetaZeroAbscissa_sub_half_lt (ε > Θ − 1/2), liouville_parityPair_of_zetaZeroAbscissa_sub_half_lt (Θ−1/2 < ε ≤ 1/2; conditional on Θ), setOf_pos_and_receiverBounded_liouville_eq_receiverDampings (Liouville dampings = Möbius dampings), sInf_derivedSet_liouville_dampings = Θ − 1/2, riemannHypothesis_iff_forall_pos_receiverBounded_moebius / _liouville. ## BilinearCountertermRemainderAndDefectDriftObstructions (start)
- Short Möbius bilinear reconstruction to N²: Σ_{n≤N²}μ(n)w(n) = 2Σ_{n≤N}μw − low-product (ab ≤ N) − high-product (ab > N). Cofactor m = bk: inner sum = sieveSeq N μ (m); sub-horizon (m ≤ N) gives δ_{m,1} ⇒ trans-horizon Möbius sum Σ_{N<n≤N²}μ(n)w(n) = −Σ_{a≤N}μ(a)Σ_{N<m≤N²/a}s_{N,μ}(m)w(am).
- At trans-horizon semiprime pq > N (p,q ≤ N distinct): counterterm 0, low −3, high +2: only joint combination 0 − (−3) − 2 = 1 = μ(pq) recovers truth.
- Cutoff step N ↦ N+1: gcd(k,k+1) = 1 ⇒ no c(N+1)² diagonal in successor correlation; couples c(N+1) to inherited history. Renormalized defect stationary at −1 for M ≤ N; at M = N+1 shift μ(N+1)² − μ(N+1)μ(N+2).
- Eventually all-prefix renormalized defect ≤ 0 coexists with reserve R_N(μ) ≥ 1/576, linear budget M/576, diagonal combined defect ≥ N/576 − 1. First-octave step inequality f(k)f(k+1) − f(k)² ≤ 0 holds for every f with f² = |f| (μ², constant 1): sign-blind (trap).
- PROVED: sum_moebius_transHorizon_eq_neg_transHorizon_sieveSeq_bilinear; lowProduct_and_highProduct_at_transHorizon_semiprime (high-product divisor pairs of pq are exactly (p,q),(q,p)); sieveSeq_succ_horizon_mul_succ_sub_eq; sieveRenormalizedPrefixDefect_succ_horizon_sub_eq (diagonal jump −c(N+1)²⌊M/(N+1)⌋ + couplings); moebiusCombinedPrefixDefect_subHorizon_cutoff_drift; at M = N+1 jump μ(N+1)² − μ(N+1)μ(N+2) ≥ 0; eventually_all_prefix_renormalized_nonpos_with_unbounded_reserve (factorial return times M = qN!: combined defect 0, renormalized ≤ −qN!/576); firstOctaveDefectFunctional_nonpos_of_sq_eq_abs, _signBlind_comparison. (No-go: defect nonpositivity is sign-blind, cannot see Mertens cancellation.)
## OscillatingProbeSourceEstimatesEnergyGapAndCollarLimitations (start)
- §1: prefix bound |A_{ε,t}(n)| ≤ K(√n)^{1−ε} ⇔ paid; unpaid ⇔ ratio unbounded. Energy-gap dichotomy (0<ε<1/2, t≠0): ζ(ρ)=0 ⇒ E ≤ K_ρ²(1+1/ε); ζ(ρ)≠0 ⇒ λT^{1−2ε} ≤ E ≤ ΛT^{1−2ε}, λ = (‖ζ(ρ)/ρ‖²/4)(1/(1−2ε) − 1/|1−2ε+2it|) > 0. No intermediate growth; paid ⇔ E/T^{1−2ε} → 0. Leading rate is ‖ζ(ρ)/ρ‖² ≥ 0: supplies no sign beyond nonvanishing (TRAP: no remaining source sign).
- §2: explicit power lower bounds on zero-free box, VK strip, uniform VK collar.
- §3: planted Euler kernel on {1,2,4}, K(ρ) = (1−2^{ρ₁−ρ})(1−2^{ρ̄₁−ρ}): k ⋆ c_{ε,t} has same admission locus as c_{ε,t} off ε₁ (unpaid near ε = 0, finitely many admitted parameters) yet is paid at the planted (ε₁,t₁). ⇒ collars and finiteness of admitted sets cannot exclude all 0<ε<1/2 (no-go).
- §4: A_{ε,t} = sawtooth S(n) = Σc(d)(⌊n/d⌋ − n/d) + tail drift T(n) = n(Σ_{d≤n}c/d − Σc/d). t=0: both ≤ 0 ⇒ A ≤ −2^{−(1+ε)}n^{1−ε}, E ≥ (2^{−(1+ε)})²/2·H_T: pure source proof of t=0 unpaid. General t: T ≈ Re(n^ρ/(ρ−1)) + O(n^{−ε}), S ≈ Re((ζ(ρ)/ρ − 1/(ρ−1))n^ρ) + O((√n)^{1−ε}); T always unbounded in pronic norm; paid ⇔ S cancels T (anti-phase lock).
- §5: short sawtooth (d ≤ √n) bounded pronic norm ≤ (1+1/(1−ε))√(1+1/ε); paid ⇔ long sawtooth cancels tail. Tail two-sided power law T^{1−2ε}. Polarization E = ‖S‖² + 2⟨S,T⟩ + ‖T‖²; imperfect anti-alignment θ < 1 ⇒ E ≥ (1−θ²)‖T‖² ⇒ unpaid. RH ⇔ no long-sawtooth/tail cancellation for t ≠ 0 across 0<ε<1/2 ("Route MC" alternate receiver) — open reformulation.
- PROVED (§1–4 details): probeEnergyLowerRate_pos_iff; energy gap dichotomy; paid ⇔ E/T^{1−2ε} → 0; unpaid ⇔ ∃c > 0 eventually E ≥ cT^{1−2ε}. Planted kernel K(ρ) factors (1−uz)(1−ūz), u = 2^{ρ₁}, z = 2^{−ρ}; |uz| = 2^{ε−ε₁} ≠ 1 off ε₁. t=0 source proof: dyadic block d ∈ (n,2n] gives T_{ε,0}(n) ≤ −2^{−(1+ε)}n^{1−ε} without ζ. Tail drift = Re(n(P_{2−ρ}(n) − ζ(2−ρ))); |T − Re(n^ρ/(ρ−1))| ≤ tailDriftRate·n^{−ε}.
- §4–5 proved: sawtooth S ≈ Re((ζ(ρ)/ρ − 1/(ρ−1))n^ρ) + O((√n)^{1−ε}); general necessity lemma eq_zero_of_bdd_pronicNorm_of_abs_sub_re_cpow_le (bounded pronic norm + |f − Re(a n^ρ)| ≤ K(√n)^{1−ε} ⇒ a = 0); tail amplitude 1/(ρ−1) = 1/(−ε+it) never 0 ⇒ tail unbounded unconditionally; paid probe ⇒ |S + Re(n^ρ/(ρ−1))| = O((√n)^{1−ε}) and S unbounded. Short sawtooth |S^short| ≤ (1+1/(1−ε))(√n)^{1−ε}. tailEnergyLowerRate > 0 for t ≠ 0 unconditionally (unlike probe rate); tail profile two-sided.
- PROVED §5–6: pronicInner, polarization, Cauchy–Schwarz for pronic inner product; routeMC_alternate_receiver_sawtooth_tail_master. t=0: long sawtooth and tail both ≤ 0 ⇒ aligned (inner ≥ 0) ⇒ ‖S^long + T‖² ≥ ‖T‖² ≥ harmonic. Long sawtooth asymptotic. Frequent strict anti-alignment θ < 1 on long moduli ⇒ unpaid. General law: ‖u+v‖ bounded, ‖v‖ → ∞ ⇒ ‖u‖/‖v‖ → 1 and anti-alignment ratios → 1 (perfect anti-alignment is the only way to pay; asymptotic rigidity, but deciding it ⇔ RH).
- PROVED §6 end: ‖T_{ε,t}‖ → ∞ (t ≠ 0, ε < 1/2); paid ⇒ ‖S^long‖/‖T‖ → 1, both anti-alignment ratios → 1. not_receiverBounded_iff_exists_longSawtooth_tail_antiAlignment_gap: unpaid ⇔ ∃θ<1 eventually −2⟨S^long,T⟩ ≤ θ(‖S^long‖²+‖T‖²) (forward via probe energy two-sided rates, θ = 1 − c₀/(4(4C₀+c₀+3C_tail))). RH ⇔ quadratic anti-alignment gap ∀ ε<1/2, t≠0 (reformulation). VK collar: gap holds unconditionally there. routeMC_longSawtooth_tail_antiAlignment_master.
- §7: ‖u+v‖² = (‖u‖−‖v‖)² + 2(‖u‖‖v‖+⟨u,v⟩) (amplitude mismatch + phase decorrelation, both ≥ 0). Amplitude mismatch δ ⇒ θ = 1 − δ²/6; phase decorrelation δ ⇒ θ = 1 − δ. Harmonic amplitudes a_long = ζ(ρ)/ρ − 1/(ρ−1), a_tail = 1/(ρ−1), a_long + a_tail = ζ(ρ)/ρ; ‖ζ(ρ)/ρ‖² = (‖a_long‖−‖a_tail‖)² + 2(‖a_long‖‖a_tail‖ + Re(a_long ā_tail)). Explicit θ_long(ε,t) ≤ 1 with eventual bound; θ_long < 1 ⇔ unpaid ⇔ amplitude mismatch ∨ phase decorrelation of the harmonic amplitudes (both fail exactly at ζ(ρ)=0: a_long = −a_tail).
- §7 end: θ_long < 1 unconditional on VK collar and on bounded-height near-zero box; RH ⇔ θ_long(ε,t) < 1 ∀ ε<1/2, t≠0. Hyperbola merge: S^long + T = Σ_{√n<d≤n} c(d)⌊n/d⌋ + n(Σ_{d≤√n} c(d)/d − Σ c(d)/d) (exact). Source-only Euler–Maclaurin packet: S^long + T ≈ Re(czetaStripRemainder(ρ,⌊√n⌋) n^ρ/ρ) + O((√n)^{1−ε}), uniformly bounded pronic remainder — ζ eliminated from the main term by a finite packet at the hyperbola threshold.

## ThePhaseLawOfOscillatingPrimePatterns (start)
- f(p) = a cos(t₀ log p + φ), |a| ≤ 1, completely multiplicative; c(d) = f(d)d^{−1/2}. For t₀ ≠ 0: paid ⇔ a cos φ < 1 ∨ ζ(1/2+it₀) = 0. Only Re(a e^{iφ}) decides. Mechanism: f(p)p^{−w} = b p^{−(w−it₀)} + b̄ p^{−(w+it₀)}, b = (a/2)e^{iφ}; Re ℓ = log|ζ| carries a cos φ, Im ℓ = arg ζ carries a sin φ, arg bounded near pole and O(log log t) at height ⇒ singularity |t−t₀|^{−(a cos φ)⁺} integrable iff a cos φ < 1; at a cos φ = 1 reduces to cos(t₀ log p).
- PROVED receiverBounded_phaseWeight_iff. Proof: Euler comparison |log|F(w)| − Re(b ℓ(w−it₀) + b̄ ℓ(w+it₀))| ≤ 2Σp^{−2}; phase bound |F| ≤ exp(2S + (|Im ℓ₋|+|Im ℓ₊|)/2)(|ζ(w−it₀)||ζ(w+it₀)|)^{a cos φ/2}; small height: two-sided pole bounds m ≤ |ζ(v)||v−1| ≤ K on box ⇒ |F| ≤ A(|Im w − t₀|^{−(a cos φ)⁺/2} + 1); large height |F| ≤ B₁(Im w)^{1/4} via |ℓ| ≤ 9 log log + C. General payment lemma receiverBounded_pattern_half_of_bounds: completely multiplicative |f| ≤ 1, local singularity exponent a₀ with 2a₀ < 1 + (Im w)^{1/4} growth ⇒ f(d)d^{−1/2} paid (via line energy ∫|ζ|²|F−P|²/|s|²). At a cos φ = 1 forced sin part 0, pattern = cos(t₀ log p) ⇒ oscWeight law.

## TheSpectralLawOfFiniteFrequencyPrimePatterns (start)
- g(x) = c₀ + Σ_k Re(B_k e^{it_k x}), distinct positive t_k, |g| ≤ 1; f completely multiplicative f(p) = g(log p). Half-damped weight paid ⇔ c₀ < 1/2 ∧ ∀k (Re B_k < 1 ∨ ζ(1/2+it_k) = 0). Each frequency read separately: mean at s = 1/2 (pole), amplitude Re B_k via singularity |s − (1/2+it_k)|^{−Re B_k}, cancelled by a critical zero at that height. Re B_k can exceed 1 with |g| ≤ 1 (e.g. (9/8)cos x − (1/8)cos 3x); cap Re B_k ≤ 2 since |F(w)| ≤ ζ(Re w).
- Mechanism: local factorization log|F(w)| = −c₀ log|w−1| − Σ(Re B_k/2)(log|w−1−it_k| + log|w−1+it_k|) + O(1) on 1 < Re w ≤ 2, |Im w| ≤ H; upper half → integrable majorant (one singular height near any point); lower half → singularity κ/|s − s_k| forbidden by paid receiver.
- PROVED: local factorization exists_abs_log_norm_spectralSeries_sub_le (from |log|ζ(v)| + log|v−1|| ≤ L and |Im ℓ(v)| ≤ C on boxes); F ≠ 0 on Re w > 1 (Euler product); |F(w)| ≤ 1 + 1/(Re w − 1). Singular heights η(none)=0 exponent c₀, η(k)=t_k exponent Re B_k/2; finitely many distinct heights 2δ-separated; two-sided log-factor bounds; local lower bound log|F(w)| ≥ −e_j log|w−1−iη_j| − C within δ.
- spectralAmplitude_re_le_two: Re B_k ≤ 2 (series growth |w−1−it_k|^{−Re B_k/2} capped by ζ(Re w) ~ 1/u).
- Unpaid: c₀ ≥ 1/2 ⇒ |C(s)|² ≥ e^{−2C}/|s−1/2| near 1/2 ⇒ unpaid (mean read at s=1/2). Re B_k ≥ 1 ∧ ζ(1/2+it_k) ≠ 0 ⇒ unpaid (lower bound e^{−2C}/|s−s_k| at height t_k, forbidden by paid receiver when ζ ≠ 0 there).
- Paid range tools: at zero s₀ ≠ 1, |ζ(s)| ≤ L|s−s₀| on box; exp_sum_le_of_unique_large (at most one large log term) ⇒ product majorant; sq_mul_exp_sq_le: d²·exp(a max(0,−log d))² ≤ D² for a ≤ 1 (zero cancels singularity of amplitude ≤ 1... here a = Re B_k/2·2 ≤ ... ).
- Paid range proof: exists_lineEnergy_le_of_majorant_off_countable (integrable majorant on (0,T] off countable set + F ≤ B₁ t^{1/4} beyond ⇒ line energy bounded uniformly in σ ∈ (1/2,3/4) ⇒ receiver bounded); large-height |F| ≤ B₁(Im w)^{1/4}; upper factorization |F|² ≤ R(1 + Σ_j Y_j²) with Y_j = exp(e_j⁺ max(0,−log dist_j)), at most one height near w (separation); pointwise majorant: uncancelled height contributes |τ−η_j|^{−2e_j⁺} (integrable iff 2e_j⁺ < 1), cancelled height (critical zero) killed by |ζ(s)| ≤ L|s−s_k| using cap Re B_k ≤ 2 (exponent ≤ 1). receiverBounded_spectralWeight_iff PROVED.
- receiverBounded_cubicPattern_iff: g = (9/8)cos x − (1/8)cos 3x, |g| ≤ 1, amplitude 9/8 > 1 at base frequency; paid ⇔ ζ(1/2+it₀) = 0 (frequency 3t₀ amplitude −1/8 harmless).

## CompletePeriodQuadraticFormsAgreeOnParityRowPairs
- Imports BezoutValuationIncidence complete-period classification. sievePoincareExcess(N,c) = Σ_{e=2}^N (φ(e) − 2μ(e))Y_{N,c}(e)² depends only on squared Selberg coords ⇒ sign-character invariant.
- CompletePeriodQuadraticEq N c₁ c₂ bundles: equal mean + 12 complete-period quadratic forms (Selberg–totient diagonal, sieveVariance, Bézout coprime form, successor covariance/reserve, Poincaré excess, successor phase loss, primitive energy, variance above parity, Franel gcd form, period covariance at every lag, tuple variance every H) + all two-point translated moments. Holds whenever means equal and Y(q)² equal for q ∈ [2,N]; every mean-preserving sign character ε (ε(1)=1, ε²=1) on Selberg coords gives an indistinguishable row.
- evenFlipRow (negate even-q Selberg coords) is complete-period invisible for every row c; pointwise response shifts by −2Σ_{2|q} Y(q)c_q(k).
- Three parity families (0 < ε ≤ 1/2): χ₄ twin rows, real-character twin rows (χ ≠ 1 real), Fermi rows vs evenFlip(Fermi): CompletePeriodQuadraticEq at every N, (χ₄/char) cubic moments and pointwise responses differ (Fermi: all-degree moments agree), yet horizonPronicEnergy splits bounded vs unbounded as N → ∞. Message: all complete-period quadratic (and for Fermi all moment) statistics are blind to what the trans-horizon receiver detects.

## PrefixStabilityAndCriticalWienerClosureOfMoebiusRows (start)
- horizonRow c N = c·1_{d≤N}; below horizon n ≤ N: A_{row}(n) = A_c(n) + τ_N n, τ_N = Σ_{d>N} c(d)/d; beyond horizon |A_row(n)| ≤ Σ_{d≤N}|c(d)| (sawtooth). Linear drift pronic mass ∈ [N/2, N].
- PROVED subHorizon_horizonRowEnergy_bounded_iff (paid c): sub-horizon row energies (T ≤ N) uniformly bounded ⇔ sup_N N τ_N² < ∞ (square-root harmonic-tail law); dyadic version same.
- Docstring claims (to verify below): Möbius tail τ_N(ε) = O(N^{Θ−1−ε+δ}) ⇒ N τ_N² → 0 for ε > Θ − 1/2 (Θ-conditional/abscissa-parametrized); prefix-stable truncation: sign pattern with |S(x)| ≤ Lx for x ≥ D ⇒ every horizon row obeys same bound 3D^{1−ε} + (13/ε)L n D^{−ε}; partial sums O(x^β) ⇒ rows uniformly paid for ε > β/2; VK prefix bound for Möbius rows on zero-free band; divisor-quotient convolution law A_{row(g⋆c,N)}(n) = Σ_q g(q)A_{row(c,⌊N/q⌋)}(⌊n/q⌋) + Σ g(q)m_c(⌊N/q⌋)(⌊n/q⌋ − n/q); CriticalWiener kernels preserve uniform row boundedness; Möbius rows uniformly paid ⇔ Liouville rows.

- Möbius: for ε > Θ − 1/2 (Θ = zetaZeroAbscissa, unconditional parametrized statement via mobiusMertens_subpower_zetaZeroAbscissa, β between Θ and 1/2+ε) sub-horizon Möbius row energies uniformly bounded; RH ⇒ every ε > 0 (RH-conditional corollary, trap: not unconditional at ε < 1/2 since Θ unknown). For ε ≥ 1/2 trivial partial sum bound suffices.
- Prefix stability: Σ_{e≤x} horizonRow s N e = S(min(x,N)); linear bound |S(x)| ≤ Lx (x ≥ D) inherited by every horizon row N ≥ D; N < D ⇒ trans-horizon sawtooth ≤ 3D^{1−ε}. Hence |A_{row(N)}(n)| ≤ 3D^{1−ε} + (13/ε)L n D^{−ε} for all N (zero loss). Trivial: |A_{row}(n)| ≤ 3 + 13n/ε.
- horizonRows_bounded_dampedSign_of_partialSum_rpow: partial sums O((M+1)^β), β < 1, ε > β/2 ⇒ all horizon rows uniformly paid (∀N,T). Proof chooses D = ⌊n^a⌋, a = 1/(2−β'), giving |A| ≤ C n^γ, γ = (1−ε)/(2−β') < 1/2. Möbius: ε > Θ/2 ⇒ rows uniformly paid (Θ-parametrized; unconditional Θ ≤ 1 gives ε > 1/2 only).
- Möbius rows VK: |A_{row(N)}(n)| ≤ (K/ε)n^{1−ε}exp(−√log n/40) uniformly in N, ε ∈ (0,1/2] (from |M(x)| ≤ Bx exp(−√log x/12), D = ⌊exp(log n − √log n/20)⌋). Horizon rows uniformly paid on zero-free band ε ≥ 1/2 − 1/(80√log T), ε ≥ ε₀.
- §4 PROVED: divisor-quotient horizon-row convolution law (exact): row(g⋆c,N) = Σ_{q≤N} g(q)(δ_q ⋆ row(c,⌊N/q⌋)); mean m_{row(g⋆c,N)} = Σ (g(q)/q) m_{row(c,⌊N/q⌋)}; prefix A = Σ g(q)A_{row(c,⌊N/q⌋)}(⌊n/q⌋) + Σ g(q)m_c(⌊N/q⌋)(⌊n/q⌋ − n/q). Pronic bound ‖·‖ ≤ (Σ|g(q)|q^{−1/2})(B + √2 Σ|c(d)/d|) (dilation contracts q^{−1/2}, sawtooth ‖·‖ ≤ √(2/q)). CriticalWiener kernels preserve uniform horizon-row payment. Möbius rows uniformly paid ⇔ Liouville rows (∀ ε > 0; via λ = 1_□ ⋆ μ and μ = μ_□ ⋆ λ). Liouville rows: paid for ε > Θ/2 (parametrized), uniformly on VK band.

## MultiFrequencyPrimePatternsAndGeneralMultiplicativeSpectra (start)
- primeDirichletSeries ℓ_f(w) = Σ_p f(p)p^{−w}; |log|F(w)| − Re ℓ_f(w)| ≤ S = Σp^{−2} for any completely multiplicative |f(p)| ≤ 1; F ≠ 0 on Re > 1; e^{−S+Re ℓ} ≤ |F| ≤ e^{S+Re ℓ}.

- PROVED not_receiverBounded_pattern_half_of_primeDirichletSeries_re_ge (line integrand ≥ κ/(u+τ) − C' ⇒ ∫ ≥ κ log((u+r)/u) − C'r vs line energy ≤ 4πE).
- PROVED int_localPrimeMean_of_receiverBounded_damped: 0<ε<1/2, t₀≠0, Re ℓ_f(1+u+it₀) = ν log(1/u) + O(1), f(d)d^{−ε} paid ⇒ ν ∈ ℤ. Mechanism: paid ⇒ Mellin pairing analytic at s₁ = 1−ε+it₀, C·ζ = Q analytic; |C(s₁+u)| ≍ u^{−ν}, |ζ| ≍ u^q, |Q| ≍ u^p ⇒ ν = q − p ∈ ℤ (orders of analytic functions). |ν|<1 ⇒ ν = 0.

Separated factors: |y−t₁|^{−b₁}|y−t₂|^{−b₂} ≤ δ^{−b₂}|y−t₁|^{−b₁} + δ^{−b₁}|y−t₂|^{−b₂}, δ = |t₁−t₂|/2; product ∏(x_k+1) ≤ C(1+Σx_k). Prime-additive: ℓ_f = Σ ℓ_{g_k}, |F_f| ≤ e^S ∏(e^S|F_{g_k}|).
- multiPhasePattern f(p) = Σ_k a_k cos(t_k log p + φ_k); Σ|a_k| ≤ 1 ⇒ |f| ≤ 1.

Distinct height t₁ ≠ t₀: Re ℓ_{phase}(1+u+i(t₁+τ)) ≥ −B (via negated amplitude upper bound). Resonant mode a cos φ = 1: Re ℓ ≥ (1/2)log(1/(u+τ)) − B near 1+it₀ (|ζ(w−it₀)| ≥ 1/(2|w−it₀−1|)). not_receiverBounded_multiPhaseWeight_of_eq_one: some a_{k₀}cos φ_{k₀} = 1, ζ(1/2+it_{k₀}) ≠ 0 ⇒ unpaid (no budget needed, |f| ≤ 1).
- Budget Σ|a_k| ≤ 1 and a_{k₀}cos φ_{k₀} ≥ 1 ⇒ single mode, reduces to oscWeight 1 t_{k₀}. receiverBounded_multiPhaseWeight_iff (budget): paid ⇔ ∀k (a_k cos φ_k < 1 ∨ ζ(1/2+it_k)=0). Two active frequencies under budget ⇒ each |a_k| < 1 ⇒ always paid unconditionally (trap: multi-frequency budget patterns never test zeros).

Payment and uniform horizon-row payment transfer f → f'; equivalence for completely multiplicative pairs.

Every one-gap p-adic schedule injective. Weighted ray: y_k² ≤ (Σ_{j≤k}1/w_j)·weighted trace energy (Cauchy–Schwarz resistance); weighted mixed-chain sine bound.

## SmallHorizonDefectSignAndMontgomeryVaughanWindowLaws (start)
- Docstring: forward differences of s_{N,c} have Fourier energy 2R_N(c) over responseRoots; MV window inequality ⇒ |shortWindowDirichletEnergy − 2KR_N| ≤ 2N(N−1)R_N ≤ 4N(N−1)V; coercivity threshold N(N−1) (improves 8N²−4); window Poincaré constant 2N(N−1); long-window dissipation threshold 2N²−2; Möbius D̃_N(M;μ) ≤ 0 for M ≥ 2N²+2N−2; damped M ≥ 2N²−2+34N^{1+2ε}.
